Latent Spatial Memory for Video World Models
Authors: Weijie Wang, Haoyu Zhao, Yifan Yang, Feng Chen, Zeyu Zhang, Yefei He, Zicheng Duan, Donny Y. Chen, Yuqing Yang, Bohan Zhuang
First: 2026-06-08T17:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:59:54+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://aka.ms/latent-spatial-memory, Code: https://github.com/microsoft/LatentSpatialMemory
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Video world models that maintain 3D spatial consistency across generated frames typically rely on explicit point cloud memory constructed in RGB space. This design is both computationally expensive, requiring repeated rendering and VAE encoding, and inherently lossy, as the round trip through pixel space discards rich features of the learned latent representation. In this paper, we introduce \emph{latent spatial memory} for video world models, a persistent 3D cache that stores scene information directly in the diffusion latent space, avoiding pixel-space reconstruction. Building on this, we propose Mirage, a latent-space spatial memory framework that constructs the memory by lifting latent tokens into 3D via depth-guided back-projection and queries it by synthesizing novel views through direct latent-space warping. This unified formulation eliminates both the information loss of pixel-space reconstruction and the computational burden of repeated encoding and rendering. Experiments show that latent spatial memory achieves up to \textbf{10.57}$\times$ faster end-to-end video generation and \textbf{55}$\times$ reduction in memory footprint relative to explicit 3D baselines. Leveraging the geometric prior of the diffusion model, Mirage attains state-of-the-art performance on WorldScore and strong reconstruction quality on RealEstate10K.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于视频世界模型的潜在空间记忆
在生成帧间保持3D空间一致性的视频世界模型通常依赖于在RGB空间中显式构建的点云记忆。这种设计既计算成本高昂，需要重复渲染和VAE编码，又固有地存在信息损失，因为通过像素空间的往返过程会丢弃学习到的潜在表示中的丰富特征。本文提出了一种用于视频世界模型的\emph{潜在空间记忆}，这是一种持久化的3D缓存，直接在扩散潜在空间中存储场景信息，避免像素空间重建。在此基础上，我们提出了Mirage，一种潜在空间空间记忆框架，通过深度引导的反投影将潜在标记提升至3D，并通过直接在潜在空间中变形合成新视角来查询记忆。这种统一的公式消除了像素空间重建的信息损失和重复编码与渲染的计算负担。实验表明，潜在空间记忆可实现端到端视频生成速度提升高达\textbf{10.57}$\times$，内存占用减少\textbf{55}$\times$，相比显式3D基线。利用扩散模型的几何先验，Mirage在WorldScore上达到最先进性能，并在RealEstate10K上实现高质量重建。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and information loss in existing video world models that rely on explicit RGB-based point cloud memory, this work introduces latent spatial memory—a persistent 3D cache storing scene data directly in diffusion model latent space. The proposed Mirage framework lifts latent tokens into 3D via depth-guided back-projection and queries them through latent-space warping, eliminating pixel-space reconstruction and repeated encoding. Experiments demonstrate that this approach achieves up to 10.57x faster end-to-end video generation and 55x smaller memory footprint compared to explicit 3D baselines, while maintaining state-of-the-art performance on WorldScore and strong reconstruction quality on RealEstate10K.
为解决传统视频世界模型中像素空间点云记忆导致的计算开销大和信息丢失问题，本文提出在扩散模型潜在空间中直接存储场景信息的潜在空间记忆机制。Mirage框架通过深度引导的反投影将潜在标记升维至3D，并通过潜在空间变形查询新视角，避免重复渲染与编码。实验表明，该方法相较显式3D基线可实现高达10.57倍的视频生成速度提升和55倍的内存占用降低，同时在WorldScore上达到最先进性能，并在RealEstate10K上实现高质量重建。
MemoryVLA++: Temporal Modeling via Memory and Imagination in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Hao Shi, Weiye Li, Bin Xie, Yulin Wang, Renping Zhou, Tiancai Wang, Xiangyu Zhang, Ping Luo, Gao Huang
First: 2026-06-08T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:59:53+00:00
Comments: The project is available at https://shihao1895.github.io/MemoryVLA-PP-Web
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Temporal modeling is essential for robotic manipulation, as effective control requires both memory of past interactions and imagination of future states. However, most VLA models rely primarily on the current observation and therefore struggle with long-horizon, temporally dependent tasks. Cognitive science suggests that humans rely on working memory to buffer short-lived context, the hippocampal system to preserve episodic memory of past experience, and internal models to imagine possible future state evolution. Inspired by these mechanisms, we propose MemoryVLA++, a full temporal modeling framework that equips VLA models with memory and imagination for robotic manipulation. A pretrained VLM encodes the current observation into perceptual and cognitive tokens, forming working memory. These tokens query a Perceptual-Cognitive Memory Bank to retrieve relevant historical context. This bank stores low-level details and high-level semantics from past interactions, and is updated through redundancy-aware consolidation. A world model imagines future states in a denoising latent space, and the imagined latents are integrated under memory guidance to form full temporal-aware tokens. The resulting tokens condition a diffusion action expert to predict temporally consistent action sequences. We conduct extensive experiments on 5 simulation benchmarks and 3 categories of real-robot tasks across 3 robots, covering general manipulation, long-horizon temporal tasks, robustness, and generalization. Our method achieves strong performance across Libero, SimplerEnv, Mikasa-Robo, Calvin, Libero-Plus, and diverse real-robot tasks, validating the effectiveness of full temporal modeling with memory and imagination. For example, on real robots, it achieves +9%, +26%, +28% gains on general, memory-dependent, and imagination-dependent tasks. Project Page: https://shihao1895.github.io/MemoryVLA-PP-Web
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemoryVLA++：通过记忆与想象实现视觉-语言-动作模型中的时序建模
时序建模对机器人操作至关重要，因为有效的控制需要对过去交互的记忆和对未来状态的想象。然而，大多数VLA模型主要依赖当前观测，难以处理长时序依赖任务。认知科学表明，人类依靠工作记忆缓冲短期上下文，海马体系统保存过往经历的事件记忆，内部模型用于想象未来状态演变。受此启发，我们提出MemoryVLA++，一种完整的时序建模框架，为VLA模型赋予记忆与想象能力以支持机器人操作。预训练的VLM将当前观测编码为感知与认知令牌，构成工作记忆。这些令牌查询感知-认知记忆库以检索相关历史上下文。该库存储过往交互的低层细节与高层语义，并通过冗余感知的整合更新。世界模型在去噪潜在空间中想象未来状态，经记忆引导整合后形成完整时序感知令牌。最终令牌引导扩散动作专家预测时序一致的动作序列。我们在5个仿真基准和3类真实机器人任务（覆盖3种机器人）上进行广泛实验，涵盖通用操作、长时序任务、鲁棒性与泛化能力。我们的方法在Libero、SimplerEnv、Mikasa-Robo、Calvin、Libero-Plus及多种真实机器人任务中均表现优异，验证了记忆与想象驱动的完整时序建模的有效性。例如，在真实机器人上，其在通用、依赖记忆、依赖想象任务上分别提升9%、26%、28%。项目页面：https://shihao1895.github.io/MemoryVLA-PP-Web
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation of vision-language-action models in handling long-horizon tasks due to lack of temporal modeling, MemoryVLA++ integrates memory and imagination inspired by human cognitive mechanisms. It uses a pretrained VLM to encode current observations into perceptual and cognitive tokens that form working memory, which then query a Perceptual-Cognitive Memory Bank storing historical context and updated via redundancy-aware consolidation. A world model generates imagined future states in latent space, guided by memory, to produce full temporal-aware tokens that condition a diffusion action expert for consistent action prediction. Experiments across 5 simulation benchmarks and 3 real-robot tasks show strong performance, with +9%, +26%, and +28% gains on general, memory-dependent, and imagination-dependent tasks respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型因依赖当前观测而难以处理长时序任务的问题，MemoryVLA++提出一种受人类认知机制启发的框架，融合工作记忆、情景记忆与想象能力。该方法利用预训练视觉语言模型将当前观测编码为感知与认知令牌，查询存储历史上下文的感知-认知记忆库（通过冗余感知机制更新），并由世界模型在去噪潜在空间中生成未来状态，结合记忆引导形成时序感知令牌，最终驱动扩散动作专家生成一致的动作序列。在五个仿真环境与三类真实机器人任务中，该方法表现优异：在通用任务上提升9%，记忆依赖任务提升26%，想象依赖任务提升28%，验证了完整时序建模的有效性。
OmniGameArena: A Unified UE5 Benchmark for VLM Game Agents with Improvement Dynamics
Authors: Mingxian Lin, Shengju Qian, Yuqi Liu, Yi-Hua Huang, Yiyu Wang, Wei Huang, Yitang Li, Fan Zhang, Zeyu Hu, Lingting Zhu, Xin Wang, Xiaojuan Qi
First: 2026-06-08T17:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:59:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language model (VLM) agents are increasingly deployed in interactive game environments. Yet game benchmarks for VLM agents typically report a single first-attempt score per (agent, game) pair, focus on single-agent Solo play, and lack unified protocols for evaluating heterogeneous agent classes (commercial VLMs, open-weight VLMs, and specialized game policies) on the same footing. We address these gaps with OmniGameArena, a real-time benchmark of twelve newly built Unreal Engine 5 games spanning Solo (7), PvP (3), and Coop (2) with unified action interfaces, and the Improvement Dynamics Curve (IDC), an agentic-reflection harness in which a tool-using reflector LLM autonomously refines a bounded skill prompt across multiple rounds. Beyond cold-start leaderboard scores, IDC exposes two additional observables for each (agent, game) pair: how the score evolves across reflection rounds, and how the learned skill behaves on held-out task variants. We report these observables for twelve VLM agents on the cold-start leaderboard and four top agents under IDC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniGameArena：面向视觉语言模型游戏代理的统一UE5基准与改进动态评估
视觉语言模型（VLM）代理正越来越多地部署在交互式游戏环境中。然而，现有游戏基准通常仅报告每个（代理，游戏）对的首次尝试得分，聚焦单代理单人模式，且缺乏统一协议，以同等标准评估异构代理类别（商业VLM、开源权重VLM及专用游戏策略）。我们通过OmniGameArena解决这些不足：该基准包含十二款新构建的虚幻引擎5游戏，涵盖单人（7款）、玩家对战（3款）与合作（2款），提供统一动作接口，并引入改进动态曲线（IDC），一种代理反思机制，使工具使用型反思LLM可自主在多轮中优化限定技能提示。除冷启动排行榜得分外，IDC为每个（代理，游戏）对提供两个额外可观测指标：得分随反思轮次的演变趋势，以及所学技能在未见任务变体上的表现。我们报告了十二个VLM代理在冷启动排行榜上的表现，以及四个顶尖代理在IDC下的表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized benchmarks for vision-language model (VLM) agents in game environments, the authors introduce OmniGameArena, a unified Unreal Engine 5 benchmark featuring twelve games across Solo, PvP, and Coop modes with consistent action interfaces. To enable deeper evaluation, they propose the Improvement Dynamics Curve (IDC), a reflection mechanism where a tool-using LLM iteratively refines agent prompts over multiple rounds. Experimental results show that while cold-start scores vary across agents and games, IDC reveals consistent improvement trajectories and robustness to task variants for top-performing agents, offering richer insights than static scores alone.
为解决视觉语言模型（VLM）游戏代理缺乏统一评估基准的问题，作者提出了OmniGameArena，一个包含十二款Unreal Engine 5游戏（涵盖Solo、PvP和Coop模式）且具备统一操作接口的实时基准。为评估代理随时间改进的能力，他们设计了改进动态曲线（IDC），通过工具型LLM在多轮中自主优化受限技能提示。实验结果显示，虽然冷启动得分因代理和游戏而异，但IDC揭示了顶尖代理在多轮优化中的稳定提升轨迹和对未见任务的良好泛化能力，比静态得分提供了更深入的学习动态洞察。
An Agency-Transferring Model-Free Policy Enhancement Technique
Authors: Anton Bolychev, Georgiy Malaniya, Sinan Ibrahim, Pavel Osinenko
First: 2026-06-08T17:59:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:59:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training reinforcement learning (RL) policies from scratch is costly: it requires careful reward and environment design, extensive tuning, and substantial computation. Yet many control problems already have a functional but suboptimal policy available as a baseline. This paper proposes a method for embedding such a baseline into the RL training process, simultaneously improving training efficiency relative to from-scratch methods and producing a learning policy that outperforms the baseline. At each step, the method arbitrates between the baseline policy and a trainable learning policy, initially relying strongly on the baseline policy and then progressively transferring agency to the learning policy. By the end of training, the learning policy is a standalone neural network that operates without baseline policy support. The paper formalizes what it means for the baseline policy to be functional: under this policy, the agent reaches a goal set and remains there with high probability. The proposed arbitration mechanism is designed to exploit this property during training, yielding high goal-reaching rates right from the beginning of training. A theoretical analysis provides a formal interpretation of this behavior under stated assumptions and extends it to the final baseline-free regime, where explicit lower bounds are derived for the goal-reaching probability of the standalone learning policy. Empirical results on continuous-control benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves returns that match or exceed those of competitive approaches, while maintaining the highest goal-reaching rates throughout training among the compared methods -- including in the final stage, where the learning policy operates without any baseline support.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种无需代理转移的策略增强技术
从零开始训练强化学习（RL）策略成本高昂：需要精心设计奖励和环境，大量调参和大量计算。然而，许多控制问题已存在一个功能但次优的基线策略。本文提出了一种将该基线策略嵌入RL训练过程的方法，同时提升训练效率（相比从零开始的方法），并生成优于基线的策略。在每一步，该方法在基线策略和可训练学习策略之间进行仲裁，初始阶段高度依赖基线策略，随后逐步将决策权转移给学习策略。训练结束时，学习策略成为一个独立的神经网络，无需基线策略支持。本文形式化了基线策略“功能完备”的定义：在该策略下，智能体能以高概率到达目标集并保持在其中。所提出的仲裁机制旨在利用这一特性，使训练初期即获得高目标达成率。理论分析在给定假设下对该行为进行形式化解释，并将其扩展至最终无基线阶段，推导出独立学习策略的目标达成概率的明确下界。在连续控制基准测试中的实验证明，该方法所获得的回报可与现有竞争方法相媲美，且在整个训练过程中（包括最终阶段，学习策略无任何基线支持时）保持最高的目标达成率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of training reinforcement learning policies from scratch, this paper introduces a model-free policy enhancement technique that leverages an existing suboptimal baseline policy to accelerate learning and improve final performance. The method dynamically arbitrates between the baseline and a trainable policy, initially relying heavily on the baseline and gradually transferring control to the learning policy. Theoretical analysis formalizes the requirement that the baseline must be functional—i.e., it must reliably reach and maintain a goal state—and shows how this property enables early success and guarantees on the final policy’s goal-reaching probability. Empirical results on continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves competitive or superior returns while maintaining the highest goal-reaching rates throughout training, including in the final phase where the learning policy operates independently of the baseline.
为降低从零训练强化学习策略的成本，本文提出一种代理转移机制，利用已有但次优的基线策略加速学习并提升最终性能。该方法在训练过程中动态权衡基线策略与可训练策略，初期高度依赖基线，随后逐步将决策权转移给学习策略。理论分析明确基线需能稳定抵达并维持目标状态，并证明该特性可确保训练早期即取得高成功率，同时为最终脱离基线的独立策略提供目标达成概率的下界保证。实验表明，该方法在连续控制任务上表现不逊于当前最优方法，且在训练全过程（包括基线完全移除后）均保持最高的目标达成率。
Causally Evaluating the Learnability of Formal Language Tasks
Authors: Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson, Anej Svete, Josef Valvoda, Reda Boumasmoud, Brian DuSell, Ryan Cotterell
First: 2026-06-08T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:58:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models, as multi-task learners, acquire a wide range of abilities during training. A fundamental question is how much task-specific data is needed to learn a given task. Answering this for natural language is difficult: tasks are hard to delineate and can confound one another. To rigorously investigate the relationship between data frequency and learnability, we turn to a controlled setting using formal languages induced from probabilistic finite automata. These serve as a methodological testbed to demonstrate that standard correlational evaluation practices are inherently flawed. To enable causal analysis, we introduce the binning semiring, an algebraic object that lets us control how often a targeted property occurs in a sampled corpus. We formulate the experimental pipeline as a causal graphical model and derive decomposed Kullback-Leibler divergence metrics to measure the learnability of specific sub-tasks. Our experiments show that evaluating learnability without causal intervention leads to incorrect conclusions due to confounders in correlational analysis, and serve as a warning about correlational pitfalls in natural-language settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果评估形式语言任务的可学习性
语言模型作为多任务学习者，在训练过程中获得广泛能力。一个基本问题是学习特定任务需要多少任务特定数据。对自然语言回答此问题困难：任务难以界定且易相互干扰。为严谨研究数据频率与可学习性的关系，我们采用由概率有限自动机诱导的形式语言的受控环境。这些环境作为方法论测试平台，证明标准相关性评估实践固有缺陷。为支持因果分析，我们引入二分语义环，一种代数对象，可控制目标属性在采样语料库中出现频率。我们将实验流程建模为因果图模型，并推导分解的Kullback-Leibler散度度量以衡量特定子任务的可学习性。实验表明，无因果干预评估可学习性会导致错误结论，因相关性分析中存在混杂变量，警示自然语言环境中相关性分析的陷阱。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of determining how much task-specific data is needed for language models to learn formal language tasks, the study uses probabilistic finite automata to create a controlled environment where data frequency can be precisely manipulated. It introduces the binning semiring as an algebraic tool to causally control the occurrence of target properties in sampled corpora, enabling causal analysis rather than correlational evaluation. Experiments using decomposed Kullback-Leibler divergence metrics reveal that without causal intervention, correlational methods produce misleading conclusions due to confounding factors, highlighting the inherent flaws in standard evaluation practices for learnability.
为探究语言模型学习形式语言任务所需的具体数据量，作者构建了基于概率有限自动机的可控实验环境，确保任务边界清晰。他们引入了“分箱幺半环”这一代数工具，以精确控制语料库中目标属性的出现频率，并将实验框架建模为因果图模型。通过分解的KL散度指标，他们证明了相关性分析因混杂因素而无法准确评估任务可学习性，强调在自然语言场景中必须采用因果干预方法以避免错误结论。
Rethinking the Divergence Regularization in LLM RL
Authors: Jiarui Yao, Xiangxin Zhou, Penghui Qi, Wee Sun Lee, Liefeng Bo, Tianyu Pang
First: 2026-06-08T17:58:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:58:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component of post-training large language models (LLMs). In practice, LLM RL is often off-policy because of training-inference mismatch and policy staleness, making trust-region control essential for stable optimization. Mainstream methods such as PPO and GRPO approximate this control with a ratio-clipping mechanism, but the importance ratio can be a poor proxy for distributional shift in long-tailed vocabularies. Recent work such as DPPO addresses this mismatch by replacing ratio-based clipping with a divergence-based mask, yielding a trust region defined by the sampled token's absolute probability shift. However, DPPO still relies on a hard mask: once a token crosses the trust-region boundary in a harmful direction, its gradient is discarded rather than corrected. To address this, we propose Divergence Regularized Policy Optimization (DRPO), which replaces the hard mask with a smooth advantage-weighted quadratic regularizer on policy shift. DRPO preserves the same trust-region geometry as DPPO while inducing bounded, continuous gradient weights that attenuate diverging updates and provide corrective signals beyond the boundary. Experiments across model scales, architectures, and precision settings show that DRPO improves the stability and efficiency of LLM RL training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考LLM RL中的发散正则化
强化学习（RL）已成为大语言模型（LLM）后训练的关键组成部分。实践中，由于训练-推理不匹配和策略过时，LLM RL常为离策略，因此需要信任区域控制以确保稳定优化。主流方法如PPO和GRPO通过比率裁剪机制近似该控制，但重要性比率在长尾词汇表中可能无法准确反映分布偏移。近期工作如DPPO通过将比率裁剪替换为基于发散的掩码来解决此不匹配问题，从而定义一个由采样token绝对概率偏移决定的信任区域。然而，DPPO仍依赖硬掩码：一旦token在有害方向越过信任区域边界，其梯度即被丢弃而非修正。为此，我们提出发散正则化策略优化（DRPO），用平滑的、优势加权的二次正则化器替代硬掩码，对策略偏移进行约束。DRPO保持与DPPO相同的信任区域几何结构，同时诱导有界、连续的梯度权重，以衰减发散更新并提供边界外的修正信号。在不同模型规模、架构和精度设置下的实验表明，DRPO提升了LLM RL训练的稳定性和效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of off-policy reinforcement learning in large language models due to poor ratio-based trust-region approximation, this paper introduces DRPO, a divergence-regularized policy optimization method that replaces hard clipping with a smooth quadratic regularizer weighted by advantage. This allows continuous gradient correction beyond trust-region boundaries while preserving DPPO’s geometric trust region. Experiments across multiple model scales and architectures demonstrate DRPO’s superior training stability and efficiency compared to PPO and DPPO.
为了解决大语言模型强化学习中因长尾词汇导致的比率机制在分布偏移估计上的不足，作者提出DRPO方法，用基于优势加权的平滑二次正则化替代DPPO的硬掩码机制，从而在保持相同信任区域几何结构的同时实现连续梯度衰减与越界修正。实验表明，DRPO在不同规模模型和架构上均提升了训练稳定性和效率。
Weighted universal approximation of differentiable maps on infinite-dimensional manifolds
Authors: Philipp Schmocker, Josef Teichmann
First: 2026-06-08T17:57:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:57:40+00:00
Comments: 77 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We generalize the universal approximation theorem for functional input neural networks (FNN) to differentiable maps by including the approximation of the derivatives. A FNN maps the input from a possibly infinite-dimensional weighted manifold to the real-valued hidden layer, on which a non-linear scalar activation function is applied, and then returns the output into a Banach space via some linear readouts. By proving a weighted Nachbin theorem, we establish a universal approximation theorem (UAT) for differentiable maps, which goes beyond the usual formulation on compact sets and also includes the approximation of the derivatives. This leads us to approximation results for non-anticipative functionals including the horizontal and vertical derivatives. As a further application, we show that linear functions of the signature are able to approximate path space functionals including their directional derivatives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无限维流形上可微映射的加权通用逼近
我们将功能输入神经网络（FNN）的通用逼近定理推广到可微映射，通过包含导数的逼近。FNN将来自可能无限维加权流形的输入映射到实值隐藏层，在该层上应用非线性标量激活函数，然后通过某些线性读出将输出映射到巴拿赫空间。通过证明加权Nachbin定理，我们建立了适用于可微映射的通用逼近定理（UAT），该定理超越了通常在紧集上的表述，同时包含导数的逼近。这使我们能够得到非预知函数的逼近结果，包括水平和垂直导数。作为进一步应用，我们表明签名的线性函数能够逼近路径空间函数及其方向导数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to approximate differentiable maps and their derivatives on infinite-dimensional manifolds, this paper extends the universal approximation theorem for functional input neural networks to include derivative approximation. The method leverages a weighted Nachbin theorem to establish a universal approximation result for differentiable maps from weighted manifolds to Banach spaces, with nonlinear activation and linear readouts. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework successfully approximates non-anticipative functionals and their directional derivatives, including showing that linear functions of the signature can approximate path space functionals and their derivatives.
为使神经网络不仅能逼近函数值还能逼近导数，本文将功能输入神经网络的通用逼近定理推广至无限维加权流形上的可微映射。方法通过证明加权Nachbin定理，建立同时逼近函数及其导数的通用逼近定理，突破传统紧集限制。实验表明，此类网络可有效逼近非预知函数及其方向导数，且证明了签名的线性组合可逼近路径空间函数及其导数。
PQR: A Framework to Generate Diverse and Realistic User Queries that Elicit QA Agent Failures
Authors: Yunan Lu, Luigi Liu, Omar Yahia, Arpit Sharma, Zhou Yu
First: 2026-05-15T18:50:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:56:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating LLM-based agents remains challenging because identifying meaningful failure cases often requires substantial human effort to design realistic test scenarios. Prior works primarily focus on automatically discovering agent failures induced by adversarial users, while overlooking queries with real user intents that also trigger agent failures. We introduce PQR, a framework that not only surfaces agent failures with respect to specific objectives (e.g., helpfulness, safety, etc.) but also resembles real users' intents. PQR operates through an iterative interaction between two complementary modules. The query refinement module performs rewrites to explore diverse query variations, while the prompt refinement module uses prior feedback to derive new objective-violating strategies and realism policies for refining prompts, which in turn generate failure-triggering yet realistic queries. We evaluate PQR on detecting an e-commerce QA agent's unhelpful responses. Our method uncovers 23% - 78% more unhelpful responses, and our generated queries are more diverse and realistic compared to previous methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PQR：一种生成多样且真实用户查询以引发问答代理失败的框架
评估基于大语言模型的代理仍具挑战性，因为识别有意义的失败案例通常需要大量人力设计真实测试场景。先前研究主要关注自动发现由对抗性用户引发的代理失败，而忽视了同样能触发代理失败的真实用户意图查询。我们提出PQR框架，不仅能针对特定目标（如有用性、安全性等）揭示代理失败，还能模拟真实用户意图。PQR通过两个互补模块的迭代交互运作：查询优化模块进行改写以探索多样化的查询变体；提示优化模块利用先前反馈推导新的目标违反策略和真实感策略，从而优化提示，生成能引发失败却更真实的查询。我们在检测电商问答代理无用回复的实验中评估PQR，结果发现该方法比先前方法多发现23%至78%的无用回复，且生成的查询更具多样性和真实性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of manually crafting realistic test cases to evaluate LLM-based QA agents, this paper introduces PQR, a framework designed to generate diverse and realistic user queries that expose agent failures. PQR employs an iterative process between two modules: a query refinement module that explores query variations and a prompt refinement module that adapts to feedback to generate more realistic, failure-inducing queries. Evaluated on an e-commerce QA agent, PQR successfully uncovered 23% to 78% more unhelpful responses than prior methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in revealing agent weaknesses while maintaining query realism and diversity.
为解决人工设计真实测试场景以评估基于LLM的问答代理的困难，本文提出PQR框架，旨在生成多样且贴近真实用户意图的查询，以暴露代理的失败情况。该框架包含两个互补模块：查询优化模块通过重写探索查询变体，提示优化模块则利用反馈动态调整策略，以针对特定失败目标（如无用性、安全性）同时保持查询的真实性。在电商问答代理上的实验表明，PQR比现有方法多发现23%至78%的无用回答，且生成的查询更具多样性和真实性。
PTL-Diffusion: Manifold-Aware Diffusion with Periodic Terminal Laws
Authors: Danqi Zhuang, Jisui Huang, Xiaoyue Xi, Andrew Kiggins, Xiaojie Wang, Ke Chen, Yue Wu
First: 2026-06-08T17:56:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:56:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Standard diffusion models typically use a single time-homogeneous Gaussian terminal distribution as the reference law for generation. While this choice is analytically convenient and empirically powerful, it provides little explicit structure for data concentrated near low-dimensional manifolds, where different regions of the data distribution may correspond to distinct local geometric or semantic factors. As a result, the reverse model must recover manifold-level structure almost entirely from an unstructured terminal reference distribution. We propose PTL-Diffusion, a proof-of-concept diffusion framework whose forward noising process converges to a nonconstant periodic family of Gaussian terminal laws rather than to a single invariant law. Unlike a phase-conditioned DDPM, where phase information only enters the denoising network while the forward process remains unchanged, PTL-Diffusion embeds phase structure directly into the forward noising dynamics. The proposed construction remains close to standard denoising diffusion models: for a periodically forced Ornstein--Uhlenbeck-type forward process, we derive closed-form forward marginals, the limiting periodic Gaussian terminal family, and explicit Gaussian reverse posteriors, enabling standard noise-prediction training. We also introduce an invariant-average regularization term coupling the phase-conditioned reverse dynamics through the averaged periodic reference law. Experiments on torus and cylinder point-cloud benchmarks and the Olivetti face dataset show that PTL-Diffusion improves manifold-level distributional matching over matched DDPM baselines, reducing phase-conditioned errors, feature-space covariance errors, and nearest-neighbour manifold distances. These results suggest structured terminal reference laws as a promising direction, while motivating more expressive phase constructions and larger-scale evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PTL-Diffusion：具有周期终端律的流形感知扩散模型
标准扩散模型通常使用单一时间齐次高斯终端分布作为生成的参考律。虽然这一选择在分析上便利且在经验上有效，但它对集中在低维流形附近的数据提供极少的显式结构，其中数据分布的不同区域可能对应不同的局部几何或语义因素。因此，反向模型必须几乎完全从无结构的终端参考分布中恢复流形级结构。 我们提出PTL-Diffusion，一种概念验证的扩散框架，其前向噪声过程收敛到非恒定的周期性高斯终端律族，而非单一不变律。与仅在去噪网络中引入相位信息的相位条件DDPM不同，PTL-Diffusion将相位结构直接嵌入前向噪声动力学中。 所提出的构造仍接近标准去噪扩散模型：对于周期强迫的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck型前向过程，我们推导出闭式前向边缘分布、极限周期高斯终端族及显式高斯反向后验，支持标准噪声预测训练。我们还引入一个不变平均正则化项，通过平均周期参考律耦合相位条件的反向动力学。在环面和圆柱点云基准及Olivetti人脸数据集上的实验表明，PTL-Diffusion在流形级分布匹配上优于匹配的DDPM基线，降低了相位条件误差、特征空间协方差误差及最近邻流形距离。这些结果表明结构化终端参考律是一个有前景的方向，同时激励更表达力的相位构造及更大规模评估。

Motivated by the limitation of standard diffusion models in capturing manifold structures in low-dimensional data, PTL-Diffusion introduces a forward noising process that converges to a periodic family of Gaussian terminal distributions rather than a single invariant law, embedding phase structure directly into the dynamics. This allows the reverse model to better recover manifold-level geometry by leveraging structured terminal references. Experiments on torus, cylinder, and Olivetti face datasets show improved manifold distributional matching, with reductions in phase-conditioned errors, feature-space covariance errors, and nearest-neighbor distances compared to matched DDPM baselines.
为解决标准扩散模型在低维流形数据中结构表达不足的问题，PTL-Diffusion提出一种收敛于周期性高斯终端分布族的前向噪声过程，将相位结构直接嵌入动态中，使反向模型能更有效地恢复流形几何结构。在环面、圆柱点云及Olivetti人脸数据集上的实验表明，该方法在流形分布匹配上优于基线DDPM模型，显著降低相位误差、特征空间协方差误差及最近邻流形距离。
iMaC: Translating Actions into Motion and Contact Images for Embodied World Models
Authors: Zhenyu Wu, Xiuwei Xu, Yukun Zhou, Yifan Li, Qiuping Deng, Xiaofeng Wang, Zheng Zhu, Bingyao Yu, Ziwei Wang, Jiwen Lu, Haibin Yan
First: 2026-06-08T17:55:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:55:41+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://imac-wm.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Embodied world models have emerged as a pivotal paradigm for visual robotic decision-making and interactive environment simulation. However, conventional embodied frameworks rely on low-dimensional structured action vectors (e.g., joint angles and end-effector poses), which suffer from limited expressive capacity, poor generalization across diverse embodiments, and unnatural dynamic modeling for complex physical interactions. To address these limitations, this paper proposesiMac (Image as Action Control), a novel unified control paradigm that treats raw visual images as native action representations for embodied world models. Departing from traditional explicit kinematic action encoding, iMac formulates continuous visual manipulation as image-based action tokens, which inherently encapsulate spatial motion intentions, interactive geometric constraints and subtle physical dynamics. We construct a dual-branch embodied architecture consisting of an image-action encoder and a dynamic world predictor: the encoder compresses target-driven visual images into compact action embeddings, while the predictor learns environment transition rules conditioned on image actions to achieve high-fidelity future state prediction and closed-loop embodied control. Extensive experiments are conducted on public embodied manipulation benchmarks and real-world robotic scenarios. The results demonstrate that iMac outperforms vector-based action control baselines in prediction accuracy, task success rate and cross-scene generalization ability. Moreover, our image-action design eliminates the reliance on manually defined action spaces, realizing flexible and universal control for heterogeneous embodied agents. This work provides an innovative visual-action perspective for embodied world models, offering a simple yet effective paradigm for scalable robotic perception and manipulation.

The method encodes visual images into compact action tokens that capture motion intent, geometric constraints, and physical dynamics, then uses a dual-branch architecture to predict future states and enable closed-loop control. Experiments on multiple benchmarks and real robots show iMaC achieves superior prediction accuracy, task success rates, and cross-scene generalization compared to vector-based baselines, while eliminating the need for predefined action spaces and enabling flexible control across diverse agents.
为了解决传统低维动作向量在具身机器人系统中的表达能力有限、泛化能力差和物理交互建模不自然等问题，本文提出iMaC方法，将原始视觉图像作为原生动作表示。该方法构建双分支架构：图像-动作编码器将视觉输入压缩为紧凑的动作嵌入，动态世界预测器则根据图像动作学习环境过渡规则，实现高保真未来状态预测与闭环控制。实验表明，iMaC在多个具身操作基准和真实机器人任务中显著优于传统向量控制基线，在预测精度、任务成功率和跨场景泛化能力上均有提升，且无需预定义动作空间，支持异构具身代理的灵活控制。
Scalable Inference-Time Annealing with Surrogate Likelihood Estimators
Authors: Daniel Peñaherrera, Rishal Aggarwal, David Ryan Koes
First: 2026-05-29T16:20:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:55:28+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 5 figures, submitted to JMLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
A long standing challenge in computational chemistry and biophysics is efficiently sampling the Boltzmann distribution of molecules. Advances in generative modeling have been proposed to address the limitations of conventional sampling techniques by eliminating the computational cost of simulation. A promising direction is iteratively finetuning diffusion models along a temperature ladder whereby training data is generated via importance sampling during inference-time annealing. Unfortunately, these methods require computing a divergence over the score field to estimate importance weights, rendering them intractable for larger systems. Here we present scalable inference-time annealing (SITA), which retrains flow-based models to generate samples at progressively lower temperatures using an energy-based model to facilitate fast surrogate likelihoods. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both Alanine Dipeptide and Alanine Tripeptide while avoiding costly divergence terms. Our code is available at https://github.com/countrsignal/sita.git
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于代理似然估计器的可扩展推理时退火
计算化学和生物物理领域长期面临的一个挑战是高效采样分子的玻尔兹曼分布。生成模型的进展被提出以克服传统采样技术的局限性，通过消除模拟的计算成本。一个有前景的方向是在推理时退火过程中，沿着温度梯度迭代微调扩散模型，其中训练数据通过重要性采样在推理时生成。不幸的是，这些方法需要计算得分场上的散度以估计重要性权重，导致其在更大系统中不可行。本文提出可扩展推理时退火（SITA），通过能量模型重新训练流式模型，以在逐步降低的温度下生成样本，从而实现快速的代理似然估计。我们在丙氨酸二肽和丙氨酸三肽上展示了最先进的性能，同时避免了昂贵的散度项。代码可在 https://github.com/countrsignal/sita.git 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient sampling of molecular Boltzmann distributions in computational chemistry, this work introduces Scalable Inference-Time Annealing (SITA), a method that iteratively refines diffusion models at decreasing temperatures using surrogate likelihoods derived from energy-based models to bypass costly divergence computations. Unlike prior approaches that require expensive score-field divergence estimation, SITA re-trains flow-based models to generate samples at progressively lower temperatures, enabling scalable inference without simulation overhead. Experimental results show SITA achieves state-of-the-art performance on both Alanine Dipeptide and Alanine Tripeptide benchmarks while avoiding the computational bottlenecks of traditional annealing methods.
为解决计算化学中分子玻尔兹曼分布高效采样的长期难题，本文提出可扩展推理时退火（SITA）方法，通过能量模型构建代理似然估计，避免计算昂贵的得分场发散项，从而在推理时迭代优化扩散模型于逐步降低的温度下采样。实验表明，SITA在丙氨酸二肽和三肽基准上达到当前最优性能，同时规避了传统方法所需的高成本发散计算。
AHA-WAM:Asynchronous Horizon-Adaptive World-Action Modeling with Observation-Guided Context Routing
Authors: Jisong Cai, Long Ling, Shiwei Chu, Zhongshan Liu, Jiayue Kang, Zhixuan Liang, Wenjie Xu, Yinan Mao, Weinan Zhang, Xiaokang Yang, Ru Ying, Ran Zheng, Yao Mu
First: 2026-06-08T17:55:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:55:18+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://serene-sivy.github.io/aha-wam/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
World-action models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation, jointly modeling visual scene dynamics and actions to inject physical priors into policy learning. However, existing world-action models couple world prediction and action execution at the same temporal resolution, forcing the world branch to model near-term frame variations that are redundant and weakly informative. We posit that strictly binding world prediction and action execution to the same temporal rhythm may underutilize the potential of the video branch for embodied control. Therefore, we propose AHA-WAM, an Asynchronous Horizon-Adaptive World-Action Model built on a dual Diffusion Transformer (DiT) architecture that reorganizes world-action modeling around this temporal asymmetry. AHA-WAM instantiates the video DiT as a low-frequency world planner that maintains rolling key-value memory over past observations and exposes reusable layerwise latent context encoding long-horizon scene evolution, while a high-frequency action DiT executes short action chunks in closed loop by querying this context through layerwise joint attention. To support asynchronous execution, we introduce horizon-adaptive offset training and Observation-Guided Video-Context Routing (OVCR), which together let the action expert exploit long-horizon world context while remaining responsive to real-time execution state without rerunning the video DiT. Experiments on RoboTwin and real-world manipulation tasks show that AHA-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance without any robot-data pretraining, attaining 92.80% average success on RoboTwin and 78.3% success across 4 real-world tasks, while reaching 24.17 Hz closed-loop control with a 4.59x speedup over Fast-WAM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AHA-WAM：基于观察引导上下文路由的异步地平线自适应世界-动作建模
世界-动作模型已成为机器人操作领域有前景的范式，通过联合建模视觉场景动态与动作，将物理先验注入策略学习。然而，现有模型将世界预测与动作执行绑定在同一时间分辨率下，迫使世界分支建模冗余且信息较弱的近帧变化。我们提出，严格绑定世界预测与动作执行至同一时间节奏可能未充分挖掘视频分支在具身控制中的潜力。因此，我们提出AHA-WAM，一种基于双扩散Transformer（DiT）架构的异步地平线自适应世界-动作模型，围绕该时间非对称性重构世界-动作建模。AHA-WAM将视频DiT作为低频世界规划器，维护过去观测的滚动键值记忆，并暴露可复用的层间潜在上下文编码以表征长时程场景演化；而高频动作DiT通过层间联合注意力查询该上下文，在闭环中执行短动作片段。为支持异步执行，我们引入地平线自适应偏移训练与观察引导视频上下文路由（OVCR），使动作专家能在不重新运行视频DiT的前提下，利用长时程世界上下文并保持对实时执行状态的响应。在RoboTwin和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，AHA-WAM无需任何机器人数据预训练即达到最先进性能，在RoboTwin上平均成功率92.80%，在4个真实任务中成功率78.3%，闭环控制频率达24.17Hz，较Fast-WAM提速4.59倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of coupling world prediction and action execution at the same temporal resolution in existing world-action models, the authors propose AHA-WAM, an asynchronous, horizon-adaptive model using a dual Diffusion Transformer architecture. The video-based world planner maintains long-term latent context from past observations, while the action module executes short-term actions via layerwise joint attention to this context. To enable asynchronous execution without re-running the video branch, the model employs horizon-adaptive offset training and Observation-Guided Video-Context Routing. Experiments on RoboTwin and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate AHA-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance without robot-data pretraining, with 92.80% average success on RoboTwin and 78.3% success across four real-world tasks, while operating at 24.17 Hz — a 4.59x speedup over Fast-WAM.
为了解决现有世界-动作模型在相同时间分辨率下耦合世界预测与动作执行导致视频分支潜力浪费的问题，作者提出了AHA-WAM，一种基于双Diffusion Transformer架构的异步时域自适应世界-动作模型。视频DiT作为低频世界规划器，维护滚动观测记忆并编码长时程场景演化；动作DiT则通过层间联合注意力查询该上下文，执行短时动作片段。为支持异步执行，模型引入时域自适应偏移训练与观测引导视频上下文路由机制，使动作模块可访问长时世界上下文而不需重跑视频DiT。在RoboTwin和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，AHA-WAM无需机器人数据预训练即达到最优性能，RoboTwin平均成功率92.80%，四个真实任务成功率78.3%，闭环控制频率达24.17Hz，较Fast-WAM提速4.59倍。
Evaluation Cards: An Interpretive Layer for AI Evaluation Reporting
Authors: Avijit Ghosh, Anka Reuel, Jenny Chim, Wm. Matthew Kennedy, Srishti Yadav, Jennifer Mickel, Yanan Long, Andrew Tran, Anastassia Kornilova, Damian Stachura, Kevin Klyman, Felix Friedrich, Jeba Sania, Max Lamparth, Jan Batzner, Anoop Mishra, Eliya Habba, Yixiong Hao, Nathan Heath, Shalaleh Rismani, Usman Gohar, Andrea Loehr, David Manheim, Ruchira Dhar, Sree Harsha Nelaturu, Aarush Sinha, Leshem Choshen, Drishti Sharma, Ishan Khire, Amit Saha, Subramanyam Sahoo, Michael Hardy, Michael Alexander Riegler, Kabir Manghnani, Michelle Lin, Yanan Jiang, Yilin Huang, Asaf Yehudai, Jessica Ji, Aris Hofmann, Mubashara Akhtar, Nuno Moniz, Yacine Jernite, Stella Biderman, Zeerak Talat, Sanmi Koyejo, Mykel Kochenderfer, Irene Solaiman
First: 2026-06-08T17:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:55:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI evaluation results are produced at scale but reported inconsistently across leaderboards, model cards, benchmark papers, and company blogs. The cost is interpretive: readers cannot reliably compare results across sources, identify what a report omits, or trace an aggregate claim to its underlying evidence. Recent efforts address isolated components but leave three gaps: they cover only narrow slices of the evaluation lifecycle and do not compose into a single interpretable record; they specify static representations that do not differentiate the questions different stakeholders bring to the same evidence; and they remain proposals on paper, lacking the extraction infrastructure required for adoption at scale. We present \EvalCards{}, an operational reporting layer that composes benchmark metadata, evaluation run data, and model metadata into a unified record. We (1) derive a reporting schema from a structured review of 52 papers and 10 stakeholder interviews, (2) implement four interpretive signals (reproducibility, documentation completeness, provenance and risk, and score comparability), rendered through reader modes calibrated to research and non-research audiences, and (3) deploy a monitoring tool that applies \EvalCards{} across 5,816 models, 635 benchmarks, and 101,843 results, surfacing systematic gaps in current reporting practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估卡片：AI评估报告的解释性层
AI评估结果虽大规模生成，但在排行榜、模型卡片、基准论文和公司博客中报告方式不一致。其代价是解释性缺失：读者无法可靠比较不同来源的结果，无法识别报告遗漏的内容，也无法追溯综合声明背后的证据。近期努力仅解决孤立组件，留下三个缺口：仅覆盖评估生命周期的狭窄部分，无法组合成单一可解释记录；仅指定静态表示，未区分不同利益相关者对同一证据提出的问题；仍停留在纸面提案，缺乏规模化采用所需的提取基础设施。我们提出EvalCards，一个可操作的报告层，将基准元数据、评估运行数据和模型元数据整合为统一记录。我们（1）从52篇论文和10次利益相关者访谈的结构化审查中推导出报告模式；（2）实现四种解释性信号（可复现性、文档完整性、溯源与风险、分数可比性），通过针对研究与非研究受众校准的读者模式呈现；（3）部署监控工具，将EvalCards应用于5,816个模型、635个基准和101,843个结果，揭示当前报告实践中的系统性缺陷。

Motivated by the inconsistent reporting of AI evaluation results across platforms, the authors introduce EvalCards, an interpretable reporting layer that unifies benchmark metadata, evaluation runs, and model metadata into a single record. They derive a reporting schema from 52 papers and 10 stakeholder interviews, implement four interpretive signals—reproducibility, documentation completeness, provenance and risk, and score comparability—tailored for different audiences, and deploy a monitoring tool across 5,816 models, 635 benchmarks, and 101,843 results, revealing systematic gaps in current reporting practices.
为解决AI评估结果在不同平台间报告不一致的问题，作者提出了EvalCards，一个将基准元数据、评估运行数据和模型元数据统一整合为可解释记录的报告层。他们从52篇论文和10次利益相关者访谈中提炼出报告结构，实现四个解释性信号——可复现性、文档完整性、溯源与风险、分数可比性，并针对不同读者群体进行适配，最终在5816个模型、635个基准和101843个结果上部署监控工具，揭示了当前报告实践中的系统性缺陷。
Topological Neural Operators
Authors: Lennart Bastian, Samuel Leventhal, Mustafa Hajij, Tolga Birdal
First: 2026-06-08T17:54:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:54:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce Topological Neural Operators (TNOs), a principled framework for operator learning on cell complexes that lifts neural operators (NOs) from functions on points and/or edges to topological domains. TNOs represent data as features defined on cells of varying dimension and model their interactions through Discrete Exterior Calculus, enabling explicit cross-dimensional coupling via gradient-, curl-, and divergence-type operators. The key design principle is to decouple where information flows, as governed by fixed topological operators, from how it is transformed (which is learned), yielding models that respect the geometric support of physical quantities and expose conservation and compatibility structure. We further propose Hierarchical TNOs (HTNOs), which incorporate learned coarse complexes to propagate long-range and topology-dependent information. Our framework subsumes existing NOs as a special case, providing a unified perspective on operator learning across discretizations. Across a range of PDE benchmarks, including irregular-geometry flow problems, TNOs and HTNOs improve accuracy; controlled studies further isolate the benefits of native higher-rank and topological structure. Project page: https://circle-group.github.io/research/TNO
中文标题/摘要
标题：拓扑神经算子
我们提出了拓扑神经算子（TNOs），这是一种针对细胞复形的算子学习原理框架，将神经算子（NOs）从点和/或边上的函数提升至拓扑域。TNOs 将数据表示为定义在不同维度细胞上的特征，并通过离散外微分几何建模其相互作用，通过梯度、旋度和散度型算子实现跨维度显式耦合。其核心设计原则是将信息流动路径（由固定拓扑算子决定）与信息变换方式（由模型学习）解耦，从而构建尊重物理量几何支撑并揭示守恒与兼容结构的模型。我们进一步提出分层TNOs（HTNOs），通过学习粗化复形传播长程和拓扑依赖信息。该框架包含现有NOs作为特例，为不同离散化下的算子学习提供统一视角。在多种偏微分方程基准测试中（包括不规则几何流问题），TNOs和HTNOs均提升精度；受控实验进一步凸显原生高阶与拓扑结构的优势。项目页面：https://circle-group.github.io/research/TNO
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model physical phenomena on complex, irregular geometries, this work introduces Topological Neural Operators (TNOs), which extend neural operators to operate on cell complexes by representing data as features on cells of varying dimension and modeling interactions via Discrete Exterior Calculus. This enables explicit cross-dimensional coupling through gradient, curl, and divergence operators, decoupling information flow (governed by fixed topological operators) from learned transformations, thereby respecting geometric support and exposing conservation laws. The framework further introduces Hierarchical TNOs (HTNOs) that use learned coarse complexes to propagate long-range, topology-dependent information. Experiments across PDE benchmarks, including irregular-geometry flow problems, show that TNOs and HTNOs achieve higher accuracy than standard neural operators, with controlled studies confirming the benefit of native higher-rank and topological structure.
为应对在复杂不规则几何上建模物理现象的需求，本文提出拓扑神经算子（TNOs），将神经算子扩展至细胞复合体上，通过在不同维度的细胞上定义特征，并利用离散外微分算子建模跨维度交互，实现梯度、旋度和散度型耦合，从而将信息流动路径（由固定拓扑算子决定）与变换方式（由模型学习）解耦，尊重几何支撑并揭示守恒结构。进一步提出层次化TNOs（HTNOs），通过学习粗网格复合体传播长程拓扑依赖信息。在包括不规则几何流问题在内的多个PDE基准测试中，TNOs和HTNOs表现优于传统神经算子，控制实验进一步验证了原生高阶与拓扑结构带来的性能优势。
Echo-Memory: A Controlled Study of Memory in Action World Models
Authors: Wayne King, Zeyue Xue, Yuxuan Bian, Jie Huang, Haoran Li, Yaowei Li, Yaofeng Su, Yuming Li, Haoyu Wang, Shiyi Zhang, Songchun Zhang, Yuwei Niu, Sihan Xu, Junhao Zhuang, Haoyang Huang, Nan Duan
First: 2026-06-08T17:54:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:54:10+00:00
Comments: 9 figures and 28 pages, Code at \href{https://github.com/Echo-Team-Joy-Future-Academy-JD/Echo-Memory}{this URL}
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present \textbf{Echo-Memory}, a controlled study of memory mechanisms in action-conditioned world models. These models generate multi-segment videos from a first frame, text prompt, and camera-action sequence, but their central failure is often memory rather than local image synthesis: after the camera leaves and returns, the scene or salient object may silently change. Existing memory designs are hard to compare because gains are entangled with backbone, training, retrieval, and evaluation differences. Echo-Memory fixes the action-to-video interface and varies only how history is stored and read by the generator. Under a shared video diffusion backbone, optimizer, camera-action representation, sampler, and evaluation pipeline, we compare raw context, compression-based memory, spatial summaries with different read-out paths, and state-space recurrence. This matched matrix separates four otherwise conflated axes: \emph{capacity}, \emph{compression}, \emph{read-out}, and \emph{recurrence}. We also evaluate memory through a three-branch protocol: replay quality, in-domain loop revisit, and open-domain return probes. The branches routinely disagree, showing that replay fidelity is not a sufficient proxy for remembering a world. Three findings follow. Raw context is a strong capacity baseline and improves open-domain return far more than it improves replay metrics. Compactness is not a free substitute for capacity: aggressive spatial and hybrid-compression memories lose the salient evidence needed for return. Finally, block-wise state-space recurrence is the strongest open-domain return mechanism in our matrix, showing that the structure of implicit memory matters as much as the decision to use it. These results provide a compact protocol for studying memory in action world models beyond isolated replay metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Echo-Memory：动作条件世界模型中记忆机制的受控研究
我们提出了\textbf{Echo-Memory}，一项针对动作条件世界模型中记忆机制的受控研究。这些模型从第一帧、文本提示和相机动作序列生成多段视频，但其核心缺陷常在于记忆而非局部图像合成：当相机离开并返回时，场景或显著物体可能悄然变化。现有记忆设计难以比较，因为性能提升与骨干网络、训练、检索和评估差异交织。Echo-Memory固定了动作到视频的接口，仅改变生成器如何存储和读取历史信息。在共享的视频扩散骨干网络、优化器、相机动作表示、采样器和评估流程下，我们比较了原始上下文、基于压缩的记忆、不同读出路径的空间摘要以及状态空间递归。该匹配矩阵分离了四个此前混杂的维度：\emph{容量}、\emph{压缩}、\emph{读出}和\emph{递归}。我们还通过三分支协议评估记忆：重放质量、领域内循环重访和开放域返回探测。各分支结果常不一致，表明重放保真度不足以作为记忆世界能力的充分代理。三个主要发现如下：原始上下文是强大的容量基线，其对开放域返回的提升远超对重放指标的提升；紧凑性并非容量的免费替代品：激进的空间和混合压缩记忆会丢失返回所需的显著证据；块状状态空间递归是矩阵中最强的开放域返回机制，表明隐式记忆的结构与是否使用它同等重要。这些结果提供了一种紧凑协议，用于研究动作世界模型中的记忆，超越孤立的重放指标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the frequent failure of action-conditioned world models to maintain scene continuity after camera movement, this study systematically evaluates memory mechanisms under a fixed interface. By isolating memory storage and retrieval methods—raw context, compression-based memory, spatial summaries, and state-space recurrence—while keeping all other components constant, the authors identify key performance axes: capacity, compression, read-out, and recurrence. Experimental results show that raw context serves as a strong baseline for open-domain return, while aggressive compression sacrifices critical scene evidence. Block-wise state-space recurrence emerges as the most effective mechanism for open-domain return, demonstrating that memory structure matters as much as its use. The three-branch evaluation protocol reveals that replay fidelity alone is insufficient to gauge world memory.
为了解决动作条件世界模型在相机移动后场景连续性丢失的问题，本研究在固定视频生成接口的前提下，系统性评估了不同记忆机制的效果。通过保持视频扩散主干、优化器、相机动作表示、采样器和评估流程一致，作者对比了原始上下文、压缩型记忆、空间摘要及状态空间递归等方法，分别考察容量、压缩、读取方式和递归结构四个维度。实验结果表明，原始上下文作为容量基线表现强劲，显著提升开放域返回性能，但对重放指标提升有限；过度压缩的记忆机制会丢失关键视觉证据，导致性能下降；而块状状态空间递归在开放域返回中表现最优，证明记忆结构的重要性不亚于是否使用记忆。三阶段评估协议（重放质量、域内循环重访、开放域返回）结果常不一致，说明仅凭重放精度无法充分衡量世界记忆能力。
Bandits for Efficient Experimentation: Adapting to Control Group, Preferences, and Context Drifts
Authors: Udvas Das, Waris Radji, Debabrota Basu, Odalric-Ambrym Maillard
First: 2026-06-08T17:53:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:53:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider a variant of the linear contextual stochastic multi-armed bandits, where the learner must provide recommendations to a group of users, each having its personalized preference vector, and in the presence of context distributions that are drifting over time. Under practitioner-friendly assumptions, we reduce this setting to linear bandit with stationary mean but heteroskedastic and non-stationary noise. We further study the case when the learner must ensure the mean reward of each decision must exceed that of a baseline strategy $\boldsymbolπ_0$ at each decision step. We introduce Dri-MED, an algorithm inspired from the linear version of the MED strategy, and carefully adapted to handle the non-stationary heteroskedastic noise. We show that the instance-dependent regret scales as $\tilde{\mathcal O}\left(\fracκ{\tildeΔ}d^2(\log(T)\right)$, where $\tildeΔ$ is the constraint-aware sub-optimality gap subject to policy $π_0$, with variance-aware multiplicative term $κ$ that we carefully handle using heteroskedastic regression. We further show Dri-MED enjoys $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$ expected constraint violations. Our numerical results suggest that Dri-MED significantly outperforms conservative baselines that ignores the drift and preference structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于高效实验的强盗算法：适应控制组、偏好及上下文漂移
我们考虑线性上下文随机多臂强盗问题的一个变体，其中学习者需为一组用户（每个用户具有个性化偏好向量）提供推荐，且上下文分布随时间漂移。在便于实践者使用的假设下，我们将该设置简化为具有平稳均值但异方差且非平稳噪声的线性强盗问题。我们进一步研究学习者必须确保在每一步决策中，每个决策的平均奖励均超过基线策略 $\boldsymbolπ_0$ 的情形。我们提出Dri-MED算法，该算法受线性MED策略启发，并经过精心调整以处理非平稳异方差噪声。我们证明，实例相关的遗憾按 $\tilde{\mathcal O}\left(\fracκ{\tildeΔ}d^2(\log(T)\right)$ 标度，其中 $\tildeΔ$ 是受策略 $π_0$ 约束的次优差距，$κ$ 是我们通过异方差回归精心处理的方差感知乘数项。我们进一步证明Dri-MED的预期约束违反次数为 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$。数值结果表明，Dri-MED显著优于忽略漂移和偏好结构的保守基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt recommendations to drifting user contexts and personalized preferences while ensuring performance exceeds a baseline, the paper proposes Dri-MED, a variant of the MED algorithm tailored for non-stationary, heteroskedastic noise in linear contextual bandits. The method leverages heteroskedastic regression to handle time-varying variances and incorporates constraint-aware regret bounds that account for the baseline policy. Experimental results show that Dri-MED achieves instance-dependent regret scaling as $\tilde{\mathcal O}\left(\fracκ{\tildeΔ}d^2\log(T)\right)$ and maintains $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$ expected constraint violations, significantly outperforming baselines that ignore drift and preference heterogeneity.
为应对用户上下文漂移与个性化偏好带来的挑战，同时确保每步决策优于基线策略，本文提出Dri-MED算法，该算法基于MED策略并针对非平稳异方差噪声进行调整。方法将问题转化为具有平稳均值但异方差非平稳噪声的线性上下文多臂老虎机问题，并在每步强制满足约束。实验表明，Dri-MED的实例相关遗憾为$\tilde{\mathcal O}\left(\fracκ{\tildeΔ}d^2\log(T)\right)$，期望约束违反次数为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$，显著优于忽略漂移与偏好结构的保守基线方法。
FASE: Fast Adaptive Semantic Entropy for Code Quality
Authors: Shizhe Lin, Ladan Tahvildari
First: 2026-06-08T17:53:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:53:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent code generation offers a promising paradigm for autonomous software development by simulating the human software engineering lifecycle. However, system reliability remains hindered by LLM hallucinations and error propagation across interacting agents. While semantic entropy provides a principled way to quantify uncertainty without ground-truth answers, current methods often rely on costly LLM-driven equivalence checks. In this work, we introduce Fast Adaptive Semantic Entropy (FASE), a novel metric that approximates functional correctness based on the minimum spanning tree of structural and semantic dissimilarity graphs. Evaluations on HumanEval and BigCodeBench demonstrate that FASE outperforms state-of-the-art semantic entropy by LLM entailment, achieving a 25% average improvement in Spearman correlation and a 19% increase in ROCAUC score against Pass@1 from ground-truth test cases when using the Qwen3-Embedding-8B model. Furthermore, by eliminating costly LLM-driven equivalence evaluation, FASE incurs negligible computational overhead, requiring only approximately 0.3% of the runtime cost of traditional semantic entropy approaches. These results position FASE as a practical, cost-effective solution for optimizing uncertainty quantification in real-world multi-agent workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FASE：用于代码质量的快速自适应语义熵
多智能体代码生成通过模拟人类软件工程生命周期，为自主软件开发提供了一种有前景的范式。然而，系统可靠性仍受大语言模型幻觉及交互智能体间错误传播的制约。尽管语义熵可无需真实答案来量化不确定性，但现有方法常依赖昂贵的大语言模型驱动的等价性检查。本文提出快速自适应语义熵（FASE），一种基于结构与语义差异图最小生成树的新型度量方法，用于近似功能正确性。在HumanEval和BigCodeBench上的评估表明，与基于大语言模型蕴含的现有语义熵方法相比，FASE在Spearman相关性上平均提升25%，在使用Qwen3-Embedding-8B模型时，相对于真实测试用例的Pass@1，ROCAUC得分提升19%。此外，FASE通过消除昂贵的大语言模型等价性评估，仅需传统语义熵方法约0.3%的运行时开销，展现出极低的计算开销。这些结果使FASE成为优化现实世界多智能体工作流中不确定性量化的一种实用且经济高效的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve reliability in multi-agent code generation systems plagued by LLM hallucinations and error propagation, this work introduces FASE, a novel semantic entropy metric that approximates functional correctness using minimum spanning trees of structural and semantic dissimilarity graphs. Unlike prior methods requiring expensive LLM entailment for equivalence checks, FASE eliminates this bottleneck while maintaining high accuracy. Experimental results on HumanEval and BigCodeBench show FASE achieves a 25% higher Spearman correlation and 19% greater ROCAUC score than state-of-the-art LLM-based semantic entropy, using only 0.3% of the runtime cost.
为提升多智能体代码生成系统中因LLM幻觉和错误传播导致的可靠性问题，本文提出FASE方法，通过构建结构与语义差异图的最小生成树来近似功能正确性，避免依赖昂贵的LLM等价性判断。在HumanEval和BigCodeBench上的实验表明，FASE相较现有语义熵方法在Spearman相关性上提升25%，ROCAUC得分提高19%，且仅消耗传统方法0.3%的运行时开销。
SynManDex: Synthesizing Human-like Dexterous Grasps from Synthetic Human Pre-Grasps
Authors: Yanming Shao, Zanxin Chen, Wenwei Lin, Mingjie Zhou, Tianxing Chen, Xiaokang Yang, Yichen Chi, Yao Mu
First: 2026-06-08T17:52:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:52:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human hand-object interactions encode functional intent, but direct transfer to robotic hands often fails under morphology, contact, and reachability constraints. We present SynManDex, a synthetic pipeline that uses generated human pre-grasps as affordance-aware proposals and resolves the final contacts with robot-native optimization. SynManDex samples object-conditioned digital human pre-grasps, retargets them to dexterous robotic hand poses, optimizes force-closure contacts on the target embodiment, and admits trajectories that pass checks from each step. The resulting keyframes support both grasp-and-lift demonstrations and various prehensile manipulation tasks such as tea pouring, photo taking, and flute playing, designed via VLM agents. As a result, SynManDex combines high grasp quality (86.4\% grasp stability) with 4.67/5 human-likeness (93.4\%). It achieves 80.7\% successes in simulation and 25/30 (83.3\%) real-robot successes when applied to a 36-DOF bimanual dexterous robotic platform.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SynManDex：从合成的人类预抓取动作合成类人灵巧抓取
人类手-物体交互蕴含功能意图，但直接迁移到机器人手时，常因形态、接触和可达性约束而失败。我们提出SynManDex，一种合成管道，利用生成的人类预抓取作为具感知能力的提案，并通过机器人原生优化解决最终接触。SynManDex采样对象条件化的数字人类预抓取，将其重定向至灵巧机器人手姿态，优化目标形态上的力闭合接触，并允许通过每一步检查的轨迹。生成的关键帧支持抓取-提升演示及多种灵巧操作任务（如倒茶、拍照、吹笛），由视觉语言模型（VLM）代理设计。结果表明，SynManDex兼具高抓取质量（86.4% 抓取稳定性）与4.67/5的人类相似度（93.4%）。在36自由度双臂灵巧机器人平台上，仿真成功率80.7%，真实机器人实验25/30（83.3%）成功。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of transferring human-like grasps to robotic hands due to morphological and kinematic constraints, SynManDex synthesizes dexterous robotic grasps by first sampling human pre-grasps conditioned on objects, then retargeting them to robotic hand poses, optimizing for force-closure contacts, and validating trajectories across steps. Experimental results show the method achieves 86.4% grasp stability and 4.67/5 human-likeness (93.4%) in simulation, and successfully executes 25 out of 30 tasks (83.3%) on a 36-DOF bimanual robotic platform, enabling complex manipulation tasks like pouring tea, taking photos, and playing flute.
为解决因形态与运动学限制导致人类抓取动作难以直接迁移到机器人手的问题，SynManDex通过采样对象条件化的人类预抓取姿态，将其重定向至机器人手配置，优化力闭合接触点，并逐阶段验证轨迹可行性。实验表明，该方法在仿真中实现86.4%的抓取稳定性与4.67/5的人类相似度，在真实36自由度双臂机器人上完成25/30项任务（成功率83.3%），涵盖倒茶、拍照、吹笛等操作，验证其在复杂操作中的鲁棒性。
Beyond Spherical Harmonics: Rethinking Appearance Models for Radiance Reconstruction
Authors: Ewa Miazga, Jorge Condor, Piotr Didyk
First: 2026-06-08T17:50:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:50:41+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
View-dependent appearance modeling remains a challenging problem in novel-view synthesis and reconstruction. Accurately representing complex angular effects often requires substantial memory and computational resources. For new learning-based methods, a common approach is to rely on SH. However, capturing high-frequency phenomena such as specular reflections demands high-order expansions, which increase memory usage and computational cost. Consequently, most methods employ low-order SH, which limits the ability to model complex view-dependent effects, resulting in overly smooth or diffuse representations. To address these limitations, we systematically evaluate a wide range of spherical functions in the context of scene reconstruction. Some of them are introduced to graphics and computer vision for the first time in this paper. Based on the insights from the experiment, we develop a novel spherical formulation, the Normalized Anisotropic Spherical Gabor function that enables efficient modeling and learning of high-frequency appearance effects while maintaining compact representation. Compared to existing approaches, our function achieves higher-quality reconstruction of view-dependent phenomena such as glints, while being up to five times more memory-efficient and more efficient to evaluate. We validate its performance in radiance-field reconstruction tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越球谐函数：重新思考用于辐射重建的外观模型
视图依赖的外观建模在新视角合成与重建中仍是一个具有挑战性的问题。准确表示复杂的角效应通常需要大量内存和计算资源。对于新兴的基于学习的方法，常用依赖球谐函数（SH）的方式。然而，捕捉高频频现象（如镜面反射）需要高阶展开，这会增加内存使用和计算成本。因此，大多数方法采用低阶SH，限制了对复杂视图依赖效应的建模能力，导致过于平滑或弥散的表示。为解决这些局限，我们系统评估了多种球面函数在场景重建中的表现。其中部分函数首次在本文中引入到图形学和计算机视觉领域。基于实验洞察，我们提出了一种新的球面公式——归一化各向异性球面Gabor函数，该函数可在保持紧凑表示的同时，高效建模和学习高频外观效应。与现有方法相比，我们的函数在重建视图依赖现象（如镜面高光）方面质量更高，且内存效率提升高达五倍，评估速度也更快。我们在辐射场重建任务中验证了其性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of spherical harmonics in capturing high-frequency view-dependent effects like specular glints, this work explores alternative spherical functions for radiance reconstruction. The authors systematically evaluate various spherical representations, introducing several new ones to graphics and vision, and propose the Normalized Anisotropic Spherical Gabor function, designed to efficiently model anisotropic appearance while maintaining compactness. Experimental results show this method achieves superior reconstruction quality for view-dependent phenomena compared to existing approaches, while being up to five times more memory-efficient and faster to evaluate.
为解决球谐函数在建模高频率视角依赖效应（如镜面高光）时的不足，本文系统评估多种球面函数并提出一种新型归一化各向异性球面Gabor函数，该函数能高效建模复杂视角依赖现象且保持紧凑表示。实验表明，该方法在辐射场重建任务中显著优于现有方法，重建质量更高，内存占用降低达五倍，评估效率也更优。
End-to-End Optimization of Incoherent Imaging for Classification Under Detector-Limited Readout
Authors: Archer Wang, Joshua Chen, Sachin Vaidya, Marin Soljačić
First: 2026-06-08T17:48:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:48:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
End-to-end co-optimization of optical front-ends (e.g. metasurfaces) and neural network back-ends has been widely applied to imaging tasks, yet a formalism characterizing when and why such systems outperform conventional lens-based imaging is largely lacking. This paper focuses on object classification, a central imaging task, and asks when end-to-end optimization of a phase mask for incoherent imaging improves performance over a conventional focusing lens. We find that these gains arise primarily under constrained detector readout and are limited under full detector readout. In the latter setting, we prove that no incoherent phase mask exceeds the ideal-channel mutual information between detector measurements and class labels; a conventional focusing lens approaches this ceiling, and joint optimization yields no empirical gain. When detector readout is constrained -- by coarse spatial sampling or a limited number of measurements -- optimized optics can substantially improve classification by increasing class separability in the detector measurements. These gains are largest under low detector noise and shrink as noise grows, because the optics shape the signal before it reaches the detector but cannot remove noise added afterward. The advantage also depends on the spectral structure of the task: co-design helps most when class-discriminative content is concentrated at lower spatial frequencies than within-class variation. We develop a theoretical framework formalizing these distinctions and test its predictions on synthetic data and standard benchmarks (MNIST, FashionMNIST, SVHN).
中文标题/摘要
标题：在探测器受限读出条件下，非相干成像分类的端到端优化
端到端优化光学前端（如超表面）与神经网络后端已在成像任务中广泛应用，但尚缺乏形式化理论说明此类系统何时及为何优于传统透镜成像。本文聚焦于物体分类这一核心成像任务，探讨在何种条件下，针对非相干成像的相位掩模进行端到端优化可提升性能。我们发现，性能增益主要出现在探测器读出受限的情形下，而在全读出条件下则受限。在后者情形下，我们证明：任何非相干相位掩模的互信息均不超过理想信道下探测器测量与类别标签间的互信息；传统聚焦透镜已接近此上限，联合优化无实际增益。当探测器读出受限——如空间采样粗糙或测量次数有限——优化光学元件可通过增强探测器测量中的类别可分性显著提升分类性能。该增益在低探测器噪声下最大，噪声增大时减小，因为光学元件仅在信号到达探测器前塑造信号，无法消除后续添加的噪声。该优势还取决于任务的频谱结构：当类别判别性内容集中于比类内变化更低的空间频率时，协同设计效果最佳。我们构建了理论框架以形式化这些区别，并在合成数据及标准基准（MNIST、FashionMNIST、SVHN）上验证其预测。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates when end-to-end optimization of incoherent imaging systems, combining phase masks with neural networks, outperforms conventional lens-based imaging for object classification. The authors find that performance gains occur primarily under detector-limited readout conditions — such as coarse sampling or limited measurements — where optimized optics enhance class separability in detector outputs. Under full readout, no incoherent phase mask can surpass the mutual information limit achievable by a conventional lens, meaning joint optimization yields no empirical gain. The advantage also depends on noise levels and spectral content: gains are largest with low noise and when discriminative features reside at lower spatial frequencies. These insights are supported by theoretical analysis and experiments on synthetic and standard datasets including MNIST and SVHN.
该研究探讨了在何种条件下，结合相位掩模与神经网络的端到端优化的非相干成像系统在物体分类任务中优于传统透镜成像。研究发现，性能提升主要发生在探测器读出受限的情形下，如空间采样粗糙或测量数量有限，此时优化光学结构可增强探测器输出中的类别可分性。在全读出条件下，任何非相干相位掩模都无法超越探测器测量与类别标签之间的互信息上限，而传统透镜已接近此极限，联合优化无实际增益。性能优势随探测器噪声增大而减弱，且在类别判别信息集中在低频区域时效果最显著。这些结论通过理论框架和在MNIST、FashionMNIST、SVHN等数据集上的实验得到验证。
Embedded Graph Convolutional Networks for Real-Time Event Data Processing on SoC FPGAs
Authors: Kamil Jeziorek, Piotr Wzorek, Krzysztof Blachut, Andrea Pinna, Tomasz Kryjak
Venue: Journal of Systems Architecture, Volume 177, August 2026, 103850
First: 2024-06-11T14:47:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:47:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The utilisation of event cameras represents an important and swiftly evolving trend aimed at addressing the constraints of traditional video systems. Particularly within the automotive domain, these cameras find significant relevance for their integration into embedded real-time systems due to lower latency and power consumption. One effective approach to ensure the necessary throughput and latency for event processing is through the utilisation of graph convolutional networks (GCNs). In this study, we introduce a custom EFGCN (Event-based FPGA-accelerated Graph Convolutional Network) designed with a series of hardware-aware optimisations tailored for PointNetConv,a graph convolution designed for point cloud processing. The proposed techniques result in up to 100-fold reduction in model size compared to Asynchronous Event-based GNN (AEGNN), one of the most recent works in the field, with a relatively small decrease in accuracy (2.9% for the N-Caltech101 classification task, 2.2% for the N-Cars classification task), thus following the TinyML trend. We implemented EFGCN on a ZCU104 SoC FPGA platform without any off-chip external memory resources, achieving a throughput of 13.3 million events per second (MEPS) and real-time partially asynchronous processing with low latency. Across multiple event-based classification benchmarks, our approach achieves competitive accuracy while providing state-of-the-art computational efficiency per event, small model size, and high scalability, customisability and resource efficiency. We publish both software and hardware source code in an open repository: https://github.com/vision-agh/gcnn-dvs-fpga.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向SoC FPGA的嵌入式图卷积网络实时事件数据处理
事件相机的利用代表了一项重要且迅速发展的趋势，旨在解决传统视频系统的局限性。特别是在汽车领域，由于其低延迟和低功耗特性，事件相机在嵌入式实时系统中的集成具有重要意义。为确保事件处理所需的吞吐量和延迟，可采用图卷积网络（GCN）。本研究提出了一种定制的EFGCN（基于事件的FPGA加速图卷积网络），该网络针对点云处理设计的PointNetConv图卷积进行了硬件感知优化。所提技术相比最新工作之一的异步事件图神经网络（AEGNN），模型尺寸缩小达100倍，同时准确率仅小幅下降（N-Caltech101分类任务下降2.9%，N-Cars分类任务下降2.2%），符合TinyML趋势。我们在无任何片外外部存储资源的ZCU104 SoC FPGA平台上实现了EFGCN，达到1330万事件/秒（MEPS）的吞吐量，并实现低延迟的实时部分异步处理。在多个基于事件的分类基准测试中，我们的方法在保持竞争力准确率的同时，实现了业界领先的每事件计算效率、小型模型尺寸、高可扩展性、可定制性和资源效率。我们将在开源仓库发布软件和硬件源代码：https://github.com/vision-agh/gcnn-dvs-fpga。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for low-latency, low-power event processing in embedded automotive systems, this study proposes EFGCN, a hardware-optimized graph convolutional network tailored for FPGA deployment. Built upon PointNetConv architecture, EFGCN incorporates FPGA-aware optimizations that reduce model size by up to 100-fold compared to AEGNN while maintaining minimal accuracy loss (2.9% and 2.2% on N-Caltech101 and N-Cars tasks, respectively). Implemented on a ZCU104 SoC FPGA without off-chip memory, the design achieves 13.3 million events per second throughput and real-time asynchronous processing with low latency, demonstrating state-of-the-art efficiency per event, scalability, and resource efficiency across multiple event-based classification benchmarks.
为满足嵌入式汽车系统对低延迟、低功耗事件处理的需求，本研究提出EFGCN，一种面向FPGA实时事件数据处理的硬件优化图卷积网络。基于PointNetConv架构并引入FPGA感知优化，EFGCN相比最新方法AEGNN模型体积缩小100倍，同时仅损失少量精度（N-Caltech101和N-Cars任务分别为2.9%和2.2%）。在无外部存储的ZCU104 SoC FPGA上实现，达到每秒1330万事件的吞吐量，支持实时部分异步处理，展现出事件处理效率、可扩展性、资源效率方面的最先进水平。
OPRD: On-Policy Representation Distillation
Authors: Shenzhi Yang, Guangcheng Zhu, Bowen Song, Haobo Wang, Mingxuan Xia, Xing Zheng, Yingfan Ma, Zhongqi Chen, Weiqiang Wang, Gang Chen
First: 2026-06-04T11:13:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:47:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises the student only in output space by matching next-token probabilities. This output-only paradigm has two limits: (1) sampling variance from Monte Carlo KL estimates over large vocabularies (e.g., Qwen's ~150k tokens) persists throughout training, and (2) it treats the teacher as a black-box, discarding all intermediate hidden states after the LM head. We propose On-Policy Representation Distillation (OPRD), which lifts distillation into hidden-state space by aligning student and teacher representations across selected layers on the same rollouts, bypassing the LM head entirely. Theoretically, OPRD eliminates sampling variance and provides richer per-layer structural information. Empirically, OPRD closes the student-teacher gap on AIME 2024/2025 and AIMO, while output-space OPD baselines plateau below the teacher. OPRD also trains 1.44x faster and uses 54% less memory than top-k OPD. Code: https://github.com/ShenzhiYang2000/OPRD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OPRD：在线策略表示蒸馏
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）仅通过匹配下一词概率在输出空间监督学生模型。该输出空间范式存在两个局限：（1）在大词汇表（如Qwen的约15万词）上，蒙特卡洛KL估计的采样方差贯穿训练始终；（2）将教师视为黑盒，丢弃LM头之后的所有中间隐藏状态。我们提出在线策略表示蒸馏（OPRD），通过在相同 rollout 上对选定层的学生与教师表示进行对齐，完全绕过LM头，将蒸馏提升至隐藏状态空间。理论上，OPRD消除了采样方差并提供更丰富的逐层结构信息。实证上，OPRD在AIME 2024/2025和AIMO中缩小了学生-教师差距，而输出空间OPD基线则低于教师水平。OPRD还比顶级k值OPD训练速度快1.44倍，内存占用减少54%。代码：https://github.com/ShenzhiYang2000/OPRD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of output-only on-policy distillation—persistent sampling variance over large vocabularies and disregard of teacher’s hidden states—OPRD introduces a method that aligns student and teacher representations across selected hidden layers during the same rollout, bypassing the language model head entirely. This approach eliminates Monte Carlo estimation variance and leverages richer per-layer structural information. Experimentally, OPRD closes the student-teacher performance gap on AIME 2024/2025 and AIMO benchmarks, outperforming output-space baselines that plateau below the teacher, while also achieving 1.44x faster training and 54% less memory usage.
为解决输出空间仅匹配导致的采样方差持续存在及忽略教师隐藏状态的问题，OPRD提出在相同 rollout 中对选定层的师生模型隐藏状态进行对齐，完全绕过语言模型头。该方法理论上消除了蒙特卡洛估计的方差，利用了更丰富的层间结构信息。实验表明，OPRD在AIME 2024/2025和AIMO基准上显著缩小了师生性能差距，超越了输出空间基线方法，同时训练速度提升1.44倍，内存消耗降低54%。
POTATR: A Lightweight Image-to-Graph Model for Page-Level Table Extraction
Authors: Brandon Smock, Libin Liang, Max Sokolov, Amrit Ramesh, Valerie Faucon-Morin, Tayyibah Khanam, Maury Courtland
First: 2026-06-08T17:43:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:43:44+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, split from PubTables-v2 paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale document processing requires contextually aware table extraction (TE) that is both accurate and efficient. Yet current approaches require billions of parameters, hundreds of autoregressive steps, or costly API inference. Motivated by this, we introduce the Page-Object Table Transformer (POTATR), a lightweight 29M parameter image-to-graph model that extends the Table Transformer (TATR) for contextualized page-level TE. POTATR outperforms all models tested on the PubTables-v2 Single Pages benchmark -- including frontier MLLMs -- achieving $\textrm{GriTS}_\textrm{Con}$ of 0.964 while running over 130$\times$ faster at roughly 300$\times$ lower cost. Further, POTATR's output is spatially grounded: every recognized element has a bounding box, enabling visual verification and geometric text assignment. As a result, POTATR performs unified page-level TE while composing with other models, enabling extension to scanned documents via external OCR and to full-document TE via techniques like cross-page merging. Code and models will be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：POTATR：一种轻量级图像到图模型，用于页面级表格提取
大规模文档处理需要准确且高效的上下文感知表格提取（TE）。然而，当前方法需要数十亿参数、数百步自回归推理或昂贵的API调用。为此，我们提出了页面对象表格变换器（POTATR），这是一种轻量级的2900万参数图像到图模型，扩展了表格变换器（TATR），用于上下文感知的页面级TE。POTATR在PubTables-v2单页基准测试中超越所有测试模型——包括前沿的MLLMs——在GriTS_Con指标上达到0.964，同时运行速度快130倍，成本低约300倍。此外，POTATR的输出具有空间定位性：每个识别元素都有边界框，支持视觉验证和几何文本分配。因此，POTATR可实现统一的页面级TE，并能与其他模型协同，通过外部OCR扩展至扫描文档，通过跨页合并等技术扩展至全文档TE。代码和模型将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and accurate table extraction from scanned documents without relying on large models or costly inference, POTATR introduces a lightweight 29M parameter image-to-graph model that extends the Table Transformer architecture for page-level context awareness. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on PubTables-v2 Single Pages, outperforming all tested models including frontier MLLMs with a GriTS_Con score of 0.964, while running 130 times faster and at roughly 300 times lower cost. Its spatially grounded output, with bounding boxes for each recognized element, enables visual verification and geometric text assignment, supporting integration with other models for scanned document processing or full-document extraction via cross-page merging.
为解决现有表格提取模型计算成本高、效率低的问题，POTATR提出了一种轻量级的29M参数图像到图模型，专用于页面级表格提取。该模型在Table Transformer架构基础上扩展，直接处理图像输入并保留空间上下文，每个识别元素均附带边界框，便于视觉验证和几何文本分配。在PubTables-v2单页基准测试中，POTATR超越所有对比模型（包括前沿大语言模型），GriTS_Con得分达0.964，同时运行速度快130倍、成本低约300倍。其空间定位能力还支持与其它模型结合，实现扫描文档处理或跨页合并后的全文表格提取。
Zero Touch Predictive Orchestration: Automating Time-Series Models for the Cloud-Edge Continuum
Authors: Abd Elghani Meliani, Arora Sagar, Adlen Ksentini, Raymond Knopp
First: 2026-06-08T17:43:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:43:41+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Cloud-Edge Continuum (CEC) enables latency-critical applications by distributing resources to the far edge, but its extreme volatility makes proactive Zero Touch Management via time-series forecasting essential. However, orchestrators face a severe "cold start" problem: newly discovered nodes lack the historical data required to train localized predictive models, while generalized models fail to capture unique hardware and microservice behaviors. To solve this, we propose a fully automated time-series prediction architecture driven by a novel data-mixing methodology. At the infrastructure level, we introduce a lightweight, technology-agnostic Resource Exposer (RE) that dynamically discovers nodes and continuously collects customizable telemetry (e.g., compute, network, energy). To overcome the sparsity of these initial local samples, our framework automatically merges them with TimeTrack, our publicly available, high-resolution dataset collected at 45-second intervals. This synergizes TimeTrack's foundational, high-frequency temporal patterns with the precise calibration of the local node data. Processed through a Neural Architecture Search (NAS) engine, the system automatically generates highly accurate baseline models. Experimental results demonstrate that merging the target data with TimeTrack effectively mitigates the cold start challenge. This integration significantly improves forecasting accuracy measured in Mean Squared Error (MSE), Mean Absolute Error (MAE), and Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) and accelerates convergence compared to training on the sparse local samples alone, training solely on generic datasets, or mixing the target data with standard alternative datasets, establishing a robust foundation for continuous MLOps deployment.

DIJIT: A Robotic Head for an Active Observer
Authors: Mostafa Kamali Tabrizi, Mingshi Chi, Bir Bikram Dey, Kelly Yuan, Markus D. Solbach, Yiqian Liu, Michael Jenkin, John K. Tsotsos
Venue: IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters, Vol. 11, No. 6, pp. 7038-7045, June 2026
First: 2025-12-08T19:37:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:43:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present DIJIT, a novel binocular robotic head expressly designed for mobile agents that behave as active observers. DIJIT's unique breadth of functionality enables active vision research and the study of human-like eye and head-neck motions, their interrelationships, and how each contributes to visual ability. DIJIT is also being used to explore the differences between how human vision employs eye/head movements to solve visual tasks and current computer vision methods. DIJIT's design features nine mechanical degrees of freedom, while the cameras and lenses provide an additional four optical degrees of freedom. The ranges and speeds of the mechanical design are comparable to human performance. DIJIT attains 85\% of the peak human saccade speed. Our design includes the ranges of motion required for convergent stereo, namely, vergence, version, and cyclotorsion. Here, we present DIJIT and some aspects of its performance. We also present a novel method for saccadic camera movements, using a direct relationship between camera orientation and motor values. The resulting saccadic camera movements are close to human movements in terms of their accuracy, with 1.17$^\circ$ and 1.14$^\circ$ mean error for the left and right cameras, respectively.

Motivated by the need to study human-like visual behavior and active vision in mobile robotic systems, DIJIT is a binocular robotic head designed with nine mechanical degrees of freedom and four optical degrees of freedom to replicate human eye and head-neck motion. Cohort-based Semantic Labeling: AI-Enabled Recovery of Visualization Semantics from Deployed SVGs
Authors: Jeongah Lee, Hima Varshini Surisetty, Durga Nirmaleswaran, Jahnavi Sharma, Srikiran Kavuri, Narges Mahyar, Ali Sarvghad
First: 2026-06-08T17:40:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:40:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many web-based visualizations are deployed as Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG), a format that faithfully preserves visual appearance but typically omits the higher-level semantic structure needed for machine interpretation. Once rendered and published, information about a visualization's components, roles, and encodings is no longer explicitly available, limiting downstream operations such as querying, accessibility augmentation, explanation, personalization, and transformation. To address this gap, we introduce CSL, an AI-enabled, multi-stage pipeline for automatically recovering visualization semantics from deployed SVGs through two complementary mechanisms: (1) cohort-based decomposition, which organizes heterogeneous SVG primitives into structurally coherent subsets that reduce the semantic assignment space, and (2) hybrid semantic grounding, which combines model-based inference with deterministic structural validation and propagation to make labeling both context-sensitive and structurally anchored. CSL produces Semantic SVG (SSVG), a representation in which SVG elements are annotated with graphical mark type, visualization role, and data role. We implemented CSL as an end-to-end prototype and evaluated it on 102 SVG visualizations, achieving global macro-averaged accuracies of 0.822 for mark type, 0.853 for visualization role, and 0.860 for data-role recovery. An ablation against a non-cohort whole-chart baseline showed that cohorting significantly improves accuracy (paired t-test: t > 20, p < 0.001; Cohen's d > 2.0), and repeated labeling of a randomly selected SVG over 100 runs yielded mean agreement above 91.9% across all three attributes. These results provide strong evidence that CSL can transform deployed SVGs into machine-usable semantic representations, enabling more accessible, adaptive, and user-steerable visualization systems.

标题：基于队列的语义标注：AI驱动的从已部署SVG中恢复可视化语义
许多基于网页的可视化以可缩放矢量图形（SVG）格式部署，该格式忠实保留视觉外观，但通常省略机器解释所需的高层语义结构。一旦渲染并发布，关于可视化组件、角色和编码的信息便不再显式可用，限制了后续操作，如查询、可访问性增强、解释、个性化和转换。为解决这一问题，我们引入CSL，一种AI驱动的多阶段管道，通过两种互补机制自动从已部署SVG中恢复可视化语义：（1）基于队列的分解，将异构SVG原语组织为结构上连贯的子集，以减少语义分配空间；（2）混合语义锚定，结合基于模型的推理与确定性结构验证和传播，使标注既上下文敏感又结构锚定。CSL生成语义SVG（SSVG），其中SVG元素被标注为图形标记类型、可视化角色和数据角色。我们实现了一个端到端原型，并在102个SVG可视化上进行了评估，标记类型、可视化角色和数据角色的全局宏平均准确率分别为0.822、0.853和0.860。与非队列全图基线的消融实验表明，队列显著提升准确率（配对t检验：t > 20，p < 0.001；Cohen's d > 2.0），且对随机选择的SVG重复标注100次，三类属性平均一致性均超过91.9%。这些结果有力证明CSL可将已部署SVG转化为机器可使用的语义表示，从而实现更易访问、自适应和用户可控的可视化系统。
