Motivated by the lack of semantic structure in deployed SVG visualizations that hinders machine interpretation and downstream tasks, the paper introduces CSL, an AI-driven pipeline that recovers visualization semantics through cohort-based decomposition and hybrid semantic grounding. The method first groups SVG primitives into structurally coherent cohorts to reduce ambiguity, then combines model inference with structural validation to assign mark type, visualization role, and data role. Evaluated on 102 SVGs, CSL achieved macro-averaged accuracies of 0.822, 0.853, and 0.860 for mark type, visualization role, and data role respectively, with cohorting significantly boosting performance over a whole-chart baseline (t > 20, p < 0.001) and high inter-run agreement (mean > 91.9%).
为解决部署SVG可视化缺乏语义结构导致机器难以理解的问题，本文提出CSL方法，通过群体分解与混合语义锚定机制自动恢复可视化语义。该方法先将SVG元素按结构聚类以降低语义歧义，再结合模型推理与结构验证，标注图形标记类型、可视化角色和数据角色。在102个可视化实例上测试，CSL在三类语义标注上的宏平均准确率分别为0.822、0.853和0.860，聚类显著提升性能（t>20, p<0.001），且重复标注一致性达91.9%以上。
Greedy Grammar Induction with Indirect Negative Evidence
Authors: Joseph Potashnik
First: 2023-12-23T18:43:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:39:27+00:00
Comments: 29 pages (including appendices and references)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes a non-lexicalized grammar-induction procedure that separates two tests: recognition of the observed finite presentation, and rejection of short preterminal strings generated by a hypothesis but unsupported by the evidence. The central object is the rule-coverage bound \(\ell^*(G)\): the maximum, over rules in \(G\), of the length of the shortest preterminal string whose derivation uses that rule. This bound induces the comparison universe \(Σ_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\le \ell^*(G)}\), where unsupported generated strings serve as indirect evidence against overgenerating hypotheses. We give a greedy search algorithm over rule sets and prove a conditional weak-recovery theorem: under explicit reachability conditions and sufficient saturation of the presentation, the exact learner reaches a grammar weakly equivalent to the unknown target. The complexity analysis is slice-wise: for each fixed incrementality radius \(k\), the search explores polynomially many rule-set extensions in the finite rule universe. Across 31 benchmark runs spanning Dyck-\(k\) languages \((1\le k\le4)\), palindromes, \(a^n b^n\), English-like recursive fragments, and an inherently ambiguous union language, grammar-level analysis establishes weak equivalence between every returned grammar and its target.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用间接负证据的贪婪语法归纳
本文提出了一种非词法化的语法归纳过程，将两个测试分离：识别观察到的有限表示，以及拒绝由假设生成但无证据支持的短前终字符串。 核心对象是规则覆盖界 \(\ell^*(G)\)：在 \(G\) 中所有规则上取最大值，即使用该规则推导的最短前终字符串的长度。该界诱导比较宇宙 \(Σ_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\le \ell^*(G)}\)，其中无证据支持的生成字符串作为对过度生成假设的间接证据。 我们给出一个在规则集上的贪婪搜索算法，并证明了一个条件弱恢复定理：在显式可达性条件和表示充分饱和的前提下，精确学习者将得到一个与未知目标弱等价的语法。复杂度分析是分片的：对于每个固定的增量半径 \(k\)，搜索在有限规则宇宙中仅探索多项式数量的规则集扩展。在31个基准运行中（涵盖Dyck-\(k\)语言 \((1\le k\le4)\)、回文、\(a^n b^n\)、类似英语的递归片段及固有歧义的并集语言），语法层面分析表明每个返回的语法与其目标弱等价。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, non-lexicalized grammar induction, this paper introduces a greedy algorithm that separates grammar recognition from rejection of unsupported hypotheses using indirect negative evidence. The method defines a rule-coverage bound \(\ell^*(G)\) to constrain the universe of preterminal strings considered, enabling the algorithm to prune overgenerating hypotheses. Experimental results across 31 benchmarks—including Dyck-k languages, palindromes, and ambiguous unions—show that under sufficient presentation saturation and explicit reachability, the algorithm consistently returns grammars weakly equivalent to the target, with polynomial search complexity per incrementality radius.
为实现高效非词法化语法归纳，本文提出一种贪心算法，通过间接负例（未支持的预终串）分离语法识别与过度生成假设的排除。核心方法是定义规则覆盖界 \(\ell^*(G)\) 以限制预终串搜索空间，从而剪枝过度生成假设。实验在31个基准（含Dyck语言、回文、a^n b^n、英语递归片段及歧义并集语言）上验证，当数据饱和且可达性明确时，算法总能返回与目标语法弱等价的语法，且每步增量半径下的搜索复杂度为多项式。
Who Earns the Safety? Intervention-Aware Quantum Predictive Control with Safety Attribution
Authors: Yifan Wang
First: 2026-06-08T17:39:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:39:22+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hard safety filters are increasingly placed downstream of learned controllers to guarantee constraint satisfaction at run time. Yet a filtered controller that never violates a constraint may still have learned nothing about safety: the filter can silently repair an incompetent upstream policy, so that post-filter success measures the filter, not the policy. We argue that safe policy learning should ask who earns the safety - the policy or its protective layers - and we make this question measurable. We introduce Intervention-Aware Variational Quantum Differentiable Predictive Control (IA-VQC-DPC), which (i) trains a compact variational quantum circuit (VQC) policy under a primal-dual intervention budget that penalizes reliance on a differentiable Control-Barrier-Function (CBF) projection, and (ii) is evaluated with a safety-attribution protocol that decomposes the executed-trajectory correction into a CBF term and a deployment runtime-guard term, and stress-tests the policy with guard-off evaluation. On closed-loop, high-fidelity BOPTEST building-control emulators (5 seeds, 60 episodes per method), intervention-aware training significantly lowers the quantum policy's raw pre-filter violation and total safety-layer reliance (both p < 10^-4) with no significant energy regression; at an equal approximately 400-parameter budget the quantum policy is significantly safer and more comfortable than a matched classical policy. Guard-off evaluation confirms the improvement is policy-level and exposes a valuable negative result: a learned differentiable energy head is only safe when paired with a distribution-aware runtime guard. The attribution protocol is general beyond quantum policies and buildings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：谁真正保障安全？具备干预感知的量子预测控制与安全归因
为确保运行时约束满足，越来越多的硬安全滤波器被置于学习控制器下游。然而，即使经过滤波的控制器从未违反约束，它也可能并未真正学习到安全知识：滤波器可静默修复上游无能策略，因此后滤波的成功度量的是滤波器而非策略本身。我们主张安全策略学习应明确“安全由谁真正获得”——是策略本身还是其保护层，并使该问题可度量。我们提出干预感知变分量子可微预测控制（IA-VQC-DPC），其（i）在主-对偶干预预算下训练紧凑的变分量子电路（VQC）策略，惩罚对可微控制屏障函数（CBF）投影的依赖；（ii）通过安全归因协议评估，将执行轨迹修正分解为CBF项与部署运行时保护项，并通过关闭保护项评估对策略进行压力测试。在闭环、高保真BOPTEST建筑控制仿真器（5个种子，每方法60个回合）上，干预感知训练显著降低量子策略的原始前置滤波违反率与总安全层依赖度（两者p<10^-4），且无显著能耗退化；在约400参数预算下，量子策略显著更安全、更舒适，优于匹配的经典策略。关闭保护项评估证实改进源于策略层面，并揭示重要负结果：仅当与分布感知的运行时保护器配对时，学习到的可微能量头才安全。该归因协议不仅适用于量子策略与建筑系统，具有一般性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem that safety filters can mask policy incompetence, this work seeks to attribute safety responsibility to the policy itself rather than protective layers. It introduces IA-VQC-DPC, a quantum policy trained under a primal-dual intervention budget that penalizes reliance on a differentiable CBF projection, and evaluates it via a safety-attribution protocol that decomposes trajectory corrections into CBF and runtime-guard components. Experiments on BOPTEST building emulators show that intervention-aware training significantly reduces pre-filter violations and safety-layer reliance (both p < 10^-4) without energy degradation, and outperforms a classical policy at similar parameter count. Guard-off evaluation confirms the safety gain is policy-driven and reveals that a differentiable energy head requires a distribution-aware guard to be safe.
为解决安全滤波器可能掩盖策略无能的问题，本文旨在将安全责任归因于策略本身而非保护层。提出IA-VQC-DPC方法，通过原-对偶干预预算训练量子策略，惩罚对可微CBF投影的依赖。通过分解轨迹修正为CBF与运行时保护项的归因协议评估，该方法显著降低预滤波违规率和安全层依赖度（p < 10^-4），且无能耗损失，同等参数规模下优于经典策略。运行时保护关闭的评估证实改进源于策略本身，并揭示：可微能量头仅在搭配分布感知保护时才安全。
AetheRock: An Arm-Worn Robot Teaching System for Force-Guided Vision-Tactile Learning
Authors: Hong Li, Yue Xu, Yihan Tang, Yankang Dong, Chenyuan Liu, Chenyang Yu, Xuyang Li, Siyuan Huang, Yujun Shen, Nan Xue, Yong-Lu Li
First: 2026-06-08T17:38:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:38:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Force and tactile sensing are indispensable in contact-rich manipulation. However, force-aware robot learning faces critical challenges due to the incompatible assembly of tactile and force sensors in handheld or wearable devices. To address these limitations, we first introduce AetheRock for gripper-force, vision, and tactile data collection, which is an arm-worn device featuring a modular and easily manufactured visuo-tactile sensor, GelSlim-MiniFab, at the fingertip, a resistive pressure sensor at the human finger contact region, a customized PCB module, and a wearable kit for comfortable and robust collection. Building on this, we propose ForceVT, a representation learning framework that uses force and vision to guide fidelity-agnostic tactile learning, enabling robust inference in any tactile situation. Real-world experiments show that AetheRock achieves qualified data efficiency and that ForceVT effectively alleviates inefficiencies when visuo-tactile sensors exhibit manufacturing and utilization inconsistencies. Overall, our work mitigates the limitations of gripper-force vision-tactile robot learning through innovative hardware design and algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AetheRock：一种用于力引导视觉-触觉学习的臂戴式机器人教学系统
力与触觉感知在接触密集型操作中不可或缺。然而，由于手持或可穿戴设备中触觉与力传感器的组装不兼容，力感知机器人学习面临关键挑战。为解决这些限制，我们首先引入AetheRock，该设备用于抓取力、视觉和触觉数据采集，其特点是在指尖配备模块化且易于制造的视觉-触觉传感器GelSlim-MiniFab、位于人手指接触区域的电阻压力传感器、定制PCB模块以及舒适的可穿戴套件，以实现稳定高效的数据采集。在此基础上，我们提出ForceVT，一种利用力与视觉引导无精度依赖触觉学习的表征学习框架，使机器人能在任何触觉环境下实现稳健推理。实际实验表明，AetheRock实现了合格的数据效率，且ForceVT有效缓解了视觉-触觉传感器在制造与使用中不一致所导致的效率低下。总体而言，我们的工作通过创新的硬件设计与算法，缓解了抓取力-视觉-触觉机器人学习的局限性。

Motivated by the challenges of integrating tactile and force sensors in wearable robotic systems, the authors developed AetheRock, an arm-worn device equipped with a modular visuo-tactile sensor (GelSlim-MiniFab) at the fingertip, a resistive pressure sensor, and a custom PCB for robust data collection. They then introduced ForceVT, a representation learning framework that leverages force and vision signals to guide tactile learning without requiring fidelity-specific training. Experimental results demonstrate that AetheRock collects high-quality, efficient data, and ForceVT significantly improves learning robustness even when sensors exhibit manufacturing or usage inconsistencies.
为解决接触密集型机器人操作中力与触觉感知的可靠性问题，该研究提出AetheRock臂戴式机器人教学系统，集成指尖模块化视觉-触觉传感器（GelSlim-MiniFab）、指接触区电阻压力传感器及舒适耐用的穿戴套件，实现稳定数据采集。系统支持ForceVT表示学习框架，利用力与视觉信号引导无精度依赖的触觉学习，提升在不同触觉条件下的泛化能力。实验表明，AetheRock数据采集效率高，ForceVT能有效缓解因传感器制造或使用不一致导致的性能下降。
SIGA: Self-Evolving Coding-Agent Adapters for Scientific Simulation
Authors: Matthew Ho, Brian Liu, Jixuan Chen, Audrey Wang, Lianhui Qin
First: 2026-06-08T17:35:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:35:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advanced scientific simulators expose specialized input languages that turn simulation goals into executable configurations, but learning them can cost domain scientists hours to days. We study simulator setup as a problem of agent-tool interface grounding: what minimal simulator-specific adaptations are needed for an off-the-shelf coding agent to operate real scientific software? Our intuition is that coding agents already know how to navigate files, edit code, run commands, and repair outputs, but they lack the simulator's executable contract: its vocabulary, structural constraints, validation rules, and termination conditions. We introduce SIGA, a Simulator-Interface Grounding Adapter that supplies this contract through retrieval, procedural memory, in-trajectory validation, and validation-enforced termination. We primarily evaluate SIGA on GEOS, an open-source multiphysics simulator used in subsurface science. SIGA produces a complete GEOS deck in about five minutes with TreeSim above 0.90, matching an extended-budget human expert who took about three hours, a roughly 36x wall-clock speedup. On a harder held-out set, grounding raises TreeSim from 0.720 to 0.789, a roughly 10% relative gain over the bare agent, and can reduce the across-seed standard deviation by 16x. Self-evolution further improves SIGA by rewriting adapter contents from prior trajectories, yielding the highest held-out GEOS mean and matching or outperforming the strongest hand-designed configuration. Transfers to OpenFOAM and LAMMPS show that the dominant mechanism shifts by interface: validation matters most when structural completeness is the bottleneck, while memory and retrieval matter most when domain correctness is the bottleneck. These results suggest that lightweight, self-improvable grounding layers can turn general coding agents into practical operators of scientific software.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIGA：面向科学模拟的自演进编码代理适配器
先进的科学模拟器使用专用输入语言将模拟目标转化为可执行配置，但学习这些语言可能耗时数小时至数天。我们将模拟器设置视为代理-工具接口接地问题：为现成的编码代理操作真实科学软件，需要哪些最小的模拟器特定适配？我们的直觉是，编码代理已知如何导航文件、编辑代码、运行命令和修复输出，但缺乏模拟器的可执行契约：其词汇、结构约束、验证规则和终止条件。我们引入SIGA（模拟器接口接地适配器），通过检索、程序性记忆、轨迹内验证和验证强制终止提供该契约。我们主要在GEOS（用于地下科学的开源多物理模拟器）上评估SIGA。SIGA约五分钟内生成完整GEOS配置，TreeSim值高于0.90，与耗时约三小时的人类专家表现相当，实现约36倍的壁钟加速。在更难的保留集上，接地使TreeSim从0.720提升至0.789，相较裸代理提升约10%，且可将跨种子标准差降低16倍。自演进进一步改进SIGA，通过重写先前轨迹的适配器内容，获得最高的保留集GEOS均值，并匹配或超越最强的手工设计配置。在OpenFOAM和LAMMPS上的迁移实验表明，接口不同主导机制亦不同：当结构完整性为瓶颈时，验证最为关键；当领域正确性为瓶颈时，记忆与检索最为关键。这些结果表明，轻量级、可自我改进的接地层可将通用编码代理转化为科学软件的实际操作者。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost for domain scientists to learn specialized input languages for scientific simulators, this work frames simulator setup as an agent-tool interface grounding problem. SIGA, a self-evolving adapter, bridges this gap by supplying simulator-specific contracts—vocabulary, constraints, validation rules, and termination conditions—via retrieval, procedural memory, in-trajectory validation, and enforced termination. Evaluated on GEOS, SIGA generates complete simulation decks in five minutes with TreeSim 0.90, achieving a 36x speedup over human experts and improving held-out performance by 10% while reducing variance by 16x. Self-evolution further enhances performance, matching or surpassing hand-designed configurations. Transfer experiments on OpenFOAM and LAMMPS reveal that validation dominates when structural completeness is limiting, while memory and retrieval matter more when domain correctness is the bottleneck.
为解决编码代理学习领域特定仿真语言耗时耗力的问题，本文提出SIGA，一种通过检索、程序记忆、轨迹内验证和验证强制终止机制，将通用编码代理与科学仿真器对接的自进化适配器。在GEOS仿真器上，SIGA五分钟内生成完整配置，TreeSim达0.90，较人类专家提速36倍，持保留集性能提升10%，标准差降低16倍。自进化机制进一步优化结果，匹配或超越人工设计配置。迁移至OpenFOAM和LAMMPS时发现，结构完整性瓶颈下验证机制最关键，而领域正确性瓶颈下记忆与检索更关键，表明轻量级自进化对接层可有效将通用代理转化为科学软件实用操作者。
SemDINO: A DINOv3-Driven Network for Cross-Temporal Semantic Alignment in Change Detection
Authors: Xinyu Tong, Meihua Zhou, Jinxiao Sun, Yingjie Tang, Lei Wang
First: 2026-06-08T17:32:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:32:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Semantic change detection (SCD) aims to simultaneously locate land-cover changes and identify semantic categories before and after transition. However, existing methods suffer from insufficient cross-temporal alignment, weak multi-scale representation, and poor robustness to pseudo-changes caused by illumination, season, and registration noise. To address these issues, we propose a novel end-to-end semantic change detection network named SemDINO, which integrates a dual-branch encoder, multi-scale temporal interaction, semantic purification, change enhancement, and decoupled multi-task prediction into a unified framework. Specifically, we construct a dual-branch encoder that combines a CNN backbone and frozen DINOv3 features via gated pyramid fusion, enabling rich multi-scale semantic representation. Then, a multi-scale temporal bidirectional transformer interaction (M-TBTT) module is proposed to achieve global cross-temporal feature alignment and information interaction. To further enhance genuine changes and suppress pseudo-variations, we introduce semantic purification (SCP), bidirectional change enhancement (BiChangeEnhance), and multi-scale change enhancement (MCE) modules collaboratively. Finally, a multi-branch CD prediction head is designed to jointly output binary change mask, bi-temporal semantic maps, and edge constraint. Extensive experiments on public remote sensing CD datasets demonstrate that SemDINO achieves superior performance and generalization ability against state-of-the-art methods, especially in complex scenarios with interference factors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SemDINO：一种基于DINOv3的跨时态语义对齐变化检测网络
语义变化检测（SCD）旨在同时定位地表覆盖变化并识别变化前后语义类别。然而，现有方法存在跨时态对齐不足、多尺度表征弱及对光照、季节和配准噪声引起的伪变化鲁棒性差等问题。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种端到端语义变化检测网络SemDINO，其整合了双分支编码器、多尺度时态交互、语义净化、变化增强及解耦多任务预测于一体。具体而言，我们构建了一个结合CNN主干与冻结DINOv3特征的双分支编码器，通过门控金字塔融合实现丰富多尺度语义表征；随后提出多尺度时态双向Transformer交互（M-TBTT）模块，实现全局跨时态特征对齐与信息交互；为进一步增强真实变化并抑制伪变化，引入语义净化（SCP）、双向变化增强（BiChangeEnhance）及多尺度变化增强（MCE）模块协同作用；最后设计多分支CD预测头，联合输出二值变化掩码、双时态语义图及边缘约束。在公开遥感变化检测数据集上的大量实验表明，SemDINO在性能与泛化能力上优于现有最先进方法，尤其在存在干扰因素的复杂场景中表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing semantic change detection methods in handling cross-temporal misalignment, multi-scale representation, and pseudo-change interference, SemDINO introduces an end-to-end network integrating a dual-branch encoder with CNN and frozen DINOv3 features, a multi-scale temporal bidirectional transformer for global alignment, and modules for semantic purification and change enhancement. Experimental results on public remote sensing datasets show SemDINO outperforms state-of-the-art methods in accuracy and robustness, particularly under challenging conditions involving illumination variation, seasonal changes, and registration noise.
为解决现有语义变化检测方法在跨时态对齐、多尺度表达及伪变化抑制方面的不足，SemDINO提出一种端到端网络，融合CNN与冻结DINOv3特征的双分支编码器、多尺度双向时序Transformer交互模块以实现全局对齐，并结合语义净化与双向变化增强模块提升真实变化检测能力。在多个公开遥感数据集上的实验表明，SemDINO在复杂干扰环境下（如光照变化、季节差异、配准噪声）的检测精度与泛化能力显著优于现有方法。
Discovering Functionally Selective Brain Regions with a Deep Topographic Multimodal Model
Authors: Badr AlKhamissi, Johannes Mehrer, Lara Marinov, Ahmed Abdelaal, Abdulkadir Gokce, Martin Schrimpf
First: 2026-06-08T17:31:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:31:50+00:00
Comments: Preprint. First two author contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Nearby neurons in cortex share similar response profiles, producing systematic spatial organization across sensory and cognitive systems. Recent topographic models reproduce aspects of this structure but remain unimodal and spatially constrain each layer separately, yielding fragmented maps that capture neither the contiguity of cortical processing streams nor their integration across modalities. We introduce Topo-Omni, a topographic multimodal model in which visual, auditory, and language/cognitive processing share a single contiguous in-silico sheet. Built by fine-tuning a pretrained foundation model with a spatial smoothness objective, this architecture develops clusters across modalities that are consistent with human neuroimaging, from sensory to cognitive systems. Driving or suppressing a cluster selectively biases or impairs perception, paralleling human intervention studies. Finally, we use our model to screen for novel clusters in-silico and discover new natural landscape and animal networks which we validate in human data. A single spatial principle thus organizes representations across modalities and processing stages, yielding testable hypotheses about cortical organization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用深度拓扑多模态模型发现功能选择性脑区
皮层中相邻神经元具有相似的响应特征，从而在感觉和认知系统中形成系统性的空间组织。近期的拓扑模型虽能再现部分该结构，但仍是单模态且各层空间约束独立，导致碎片化地图，既无法捕捉皮层处理流的连续性，也未能体现跨模态整合。我们引入Topo-Omni，一种拓扑多模态模型，其中视觉、听觉和语言/认知处理共享单一连续的体外模拟层。该架构通过在预训练基础模型上微调并加入空间平滑性目标构建而成，其跨模态聚类与人类神经影像结果一致，涵盖从感觉到认知系统。选择性驱动或抑制某一聚类可偏倚或损害感知，与人类干预研究结果平行。最后，我们利用该模型在体外筛选新聚类，发现新的自然景观与动物网络，并在人类数据中验证。单一空间原则因此统一了跨模态与处理阶段的表征，生成可检验的皮层组织假设。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture the continuous and integrated organization of cortical processing across sensory and cognitive modalities, the authors propose Topo-Omni, a deep topographic multimodal model that unifies visual, auditory, and language/cognitive processing onto a single spatially continuous neural sheet. The model is trained by fine-tuning a pretrained foundation model with a spatial smoothness constraint, which encourages the emergence of modality-consistent clusters that align with human neuroimaging data. Experimental results show that selectively activating or suppressing these clusters can bias or impair perception, mirroring human intervention studies, and the model successfully identifies novel functional clusters and networks in simulated and human data, supporting a unified spatial principle underlying cortical representation.
为捕捉皮层处理的连续性和多模态组织特性，作者提出Topo-Omni深度拓扑模型，将视觉、听觉和语言/认知系统整合到单一连续的神经片层上。通过在预训练基础模型上施加空间平滑约束进行微调，模型自发形成与人类神经影像数据一致的跨模态聚类。实验表明，选择性激活或抑制这些聚类可偏倚或损害感知，与人类干预研究结果一致，且模型成功预测了自然与动物网络中的新功能聚类，经人类数据验证，揭示了统一的空间原则可组织跨模态与处理阶段的皮层表征。
Data Synthesis and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Low-Resource NMT: A Case Study on Q'eqchi' Mayan
Authors: Alexander Chulzhanov, Soeren Eberhardt, Arjun Mukherjee
First: 2026-06-08T17:29:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:29:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 29th International Conference on Text, Speech and Dialogue (TSD 2026). This version of the contribution has been accepted for publication, after peer review but is not the Version of Record and does not reflect post-acceptance improvements, or any corrections
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural machine translation for digitally low-resource Indigenous languages is often hindered by extreme data scarcity, prompting reliance on extractive web-scraping. To ensure data sovereignty, this study introduces a data synthesis methodology to bootstrap NMT models without scraping target-language parallel text. Focusing on Q'eqchi' Mayan, we transformed community-sourced dictionaries into a massive synthetic corpus, utilizing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) via LoRA adapters on an mT5-base model. In-domain evaluation demonstrates high structural acquisition (BLEU 42.02), proving that synthetic constraints effectively teach complex agglutinative morphology and VOS word order. However, evaluation against an organic glossary reveals a structural-semantic gap (BLEU 0.59), where the model maintains grammatical integrity but lacks the lexical grounding of natural language. The model exhibits overfitting to the constrained structural variance of the synthetic templates; despite high semantic entropy in the pipeline, it struggles with the syntactic fluidity of natural language, forcing organic inputs into rigid learned patterns. Furthermore, an ablation study utilizing a Multi-Task Learning architecture resulted in negative transfer, suggesting that auxiliary tasks competed for limited parameter capacity within the LoRA adapters, causing over-optimization for synthetic markers at the expense of organic flexibility. Ultimately, we establish that synthetic bootstrapping is a highly effective structural primer, but requires authentic data for semantic refinement via Curriculum Learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：低资源NMT的数据合成与参数高效微调：以Q'eqchi'玛雅语为例
数字资源匮乏的土著语言神经机器翻译常受极端数据稀缺制约，依赖网络爬取。为保障数据主权，本研究提出数据合成方法，无需爬取目标语言平行语料即可启动NMT模型。聚焦Q'eqchi'玛雅语，我们将其社区词典转化为大规模合成语料，基于mT5-base模型采用LoRA适配器进行参数高效微调（PEFT）。领域内评估显示结构获取能力高（BLEU 42.02），证明合成约束有效教授复杂黏着形态与VOS词序。但与有机词典对比显示结构-语义鸿沟（BLEU 0.59），模型虽保持语法完整性，却缺乏自然语言的词汇基础。模型过度拟合合成模板的结构变体；尽管管道内语义熵高，却难以应对自然语言的句法流动性，被迫将有机输入套入刚性学习模式。此外，采用多任务学习架构的消融研究显示负迁移，表明辅助任务在LoRA适配器内竞争有限参数容量，导致过度优化合成标记，牺牲有机灵活性。最终，我们确立合成引导是高效结构奠基，但需真实数据通过课程学习实现语义精炼。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of parallel data for Indigenous languages like Q'eqchi' Mayan, this study proposes a synthetic data generation method using community dictionaries to bootstrap neural machine translation without web scraping. It employs Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning via LoRA adapters on an mT5-base model, achieving high structural accuracy (BLEU 42.02) in in-domain evaluation, demonstrating effective learning of agglutinative morphology and VOS word order.
