为解决如Q'eqchi'玛雅语等低资源原住民语言缺乏平行语料的问题，本研究提出利用社区词典合成数据以无需网络爬取的方式启动神经机器翻译，并采用LoRA适配器对mT5基础模型进行参数高效微调。实验表明，模型在领域内评估中结构准确率高（BLEU 42.02），有效习得了黏着形态与VOS语序，但在与有机词典对比时出现严重语义差距（BLEU 0.59），说明模型过度拟合合成模板，难以捕捉自然语言的句法灵活性。多任务学习消融实验显示负迁移，表明LoRA适配器内参数竞争导致模型过度优化合成标记而牺牲自然灵活性，最终结论是合成数据适合作为结构预训练，需结合真实语料与课程学习实现语义优化。
iOSWorld: A Benchmark for Personally Intelligent Phone Agents
Authors: Lawrence Keunho Jang, Mareks Woodside, Geronimo Carom, Andrew Keunwoo Jang, Jing Yu Koh, Ruslan Salakhutdinov
First: 2026-06-08T17:27:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:27:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A useful phone agent needs to be personally intelligent. It should reason over a user's identity, history, and preferences as they exist on the device, not just follow isolated instructions in an impersonal sandbox. Existing mobile agent benchmarks lack this kind of personalization. We introduce iOSWorld, the first interactive native iOS simulator benchmark built around a persistent user identity spanning 26 newly built iOS apps. These apps contain connected data such as transactions, messages, travel records, social relationships, and financial activity. iOSWorld includes 133 tasks across three increasingly difficult categories. Single-app tasks (27) test one app, multi-app tasks (60) span 2 to 8 apps, and memory and personalization tasks (46) require agents to infer patterns from personal data. We evaluate frontier and open-source computer-use models in both vision-only and privileged vision+XML settings. The best configuration reaches 52\% overall but only 37\% on multi-app tasks. Privileged vision+XML access improves frontier models by up to 26 percentage points, while smaller models do not benefit from added accessibility-tree input. We release iOSWorld as an open-source benchmark with all apps, seeded data, tasks, rubrics, and evaluation code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：iOSWorld：面向个人智能手机代理的基准测试
一个有用的手机代理需要具备个人智能，应基于设备上用户的身份、历史和偏好进行推理，而不仅是在一个非个人化的沙盒中执行孤立指令。现有的移动代理基准测试缺乏这种个性化能力。我们提出了iOSWorld，这是首个围绕持久用户身份构建的交互式原生iOS模拟器基准测试，涵盖26个新开发的iOS应用。这些应用包含相互关联的数据，如交易记录、消息、旅行记录、社交关系和金融活动。iOSWorld包含133项任务，分为三个难度递增的类别：单应用任务（27项）测试单一应用，多应用任务（60项）跨越2至8个应用，记忆与个性化任务（46项）要求代理从个人数据中推断模式。我们在仅视觉和特权视觉+XML两种设置下评估前沿及开源计算机使用模型。最佳配置整体准确率为52%，但在多应用任务上仅为37%。特权视觉+XML访问可使前沿模型性能提升最高26个百分点，而较小模型则无法从增加的可访问性树输入中获益。我们以开源形式发布iOSWorld，包含所有应用、预设数据、任务、评分标准和评估代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for phone agents to understand users' personal contexts rather than just execute isolated commands, the authors introduce iOSWorld, the first interactive iOS simulator benchmark that models persistent user identity across 26 custom-built apps with interconnected personal data. The benchmark includes 133 tasks divided into single-app, multi-app, and memory/personalization categories, testing agents’ ability to reason over user history and preferences. Evaluating frontier and open-source models under vision-only and vision+XML settings, results show the best model achieves 52% overall accuracy but only 37% on multi-app tasks; privileged vision+XML access boosts frontier models by up to 26 percentage points, while smaller models show no improvement from additional accessibility-tree input.
为推动手机代理理解用户个人情境而非执行孤立指令，作者提出了iOSWorld，这是首个围绕26个iOS应用构建的、模拟持久用户身份的基准测试，这些应用包含交易、消息、旅行记录等关联个人数据。该基准包含133个任务，分为单应用、多应用及记忆/个性化三类。在视觉仅和视觉+XML两种设置下评估，前沿模型整体准确率为52%，但在多应用任务中仅为37%；特权视觉+XML设置使前沿模型性能提升最高达26个百分点，而小模型则无此收益。
Preserving Plasticity in Continual Learning via Dynamical Isometry
Authors: Andries Rosseau, Robert Müller, Ann Nowé
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-08T17:24:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:24:15+00:00
Comments: ICML26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continual training of deep neural networks under non-stationarity often leads to a progressive loss of plasticity, eventually limiting further learning. We relate plasticity to the empirical Neural Tangent Kernel, and identify dynamical isometry (the condition that layer-wise Jacobian singular values remain close to one) as a key mechanism for preserving plasticity in continual learning. We revisit a class of networks that are almost-everywhere isometric while remaining universal Lipschitz function approximators, demonstrating that near-dynamical isometry is compatible with expressive nonlinear representations. For general architectures, we propose an efficient isometry-promoting regularization scheme and identify a novel mechanism by which it can reactivate dormant ReLU units. Building on this, we introduce AdamO, an Adam-style adaptive optimizer that decouples isometry regularization from gradient updates, analogous to AdamW. We further reinterpret prior plasticity-preserving approaches through the lens of dynamical isometry, showing that they target only a partial measure of isometry. Across supervised and reinforcement-learning continual-learning benchmarks designed to induce plasticity loss, our methods consistently match or outperform existing approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过动力学等距保持持续学习中的可塑性
在非平稳条件下对深度神经网络进行持续训练，往往会导致可塑性逐步丧失，最终限制进一步学习。我们将可塑性与经验神经切线核联系起来，并识别出动力学等距（各层雅可比奇异值保持接近一）是持续学习中保持可塑性的关键机制。我们重新审视了一类几乎处处等距但仍保持通用利普希茨函数逼近能力的网络，证明近似动力学等距与表达性强的非线性表示兼容。对于通用架构，我们提出了一种高效的等距促进正则化方案，并识别出其重新激活沉睡ReLU单元的新机制。在此基础上，我们引入AdamO，一种类似Adam的自适应优化器，将等距正则化与梯度更新解耦，类似于AdamW。我们进一步从动力学等距视角重新诠释先前的可塑性保持方法，表明它们仅针对等距的部分度量。在为诱导可塑性丧失而设计的监督学习和强化学习持续学习基准测试中，我们的方法始终与现有方法相当或更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of neural network plasticity during continual learning under non-stationary conditions, this work links plasticity preservation to dynamical isometry—defined as maintaining layer-wise Jacobian singular values near one—and demonstrates that near-isometric networks can retain expressive capacity. The authors propose an efficient regularization scheme to promote isometry across architectures and introduce AdamO, an optimizer that decouples isometry enforcement from gradient updates. Experiments across supervised and reinforcement learning continual learning benchmarks show that these methods consistently match or exceed existing plasticity-preserving techniques.
为了解决深度神经网络在非平稳环境下持续学习时塑料性逐渐丧失的问题，该研究将塑料性与动力学等距性（即各层雅可比奇异值保持接近一）联系起来，证明近似等距网络仍可保持表达能力。作者回顾了几乎处处等距的网络架构，提出了一种高效的等距正则化方案，并设计了AdamO优化器，将等距约束与梯度更新解耦。在多个持续学习基准测试中，这些方法在保持塑料性方面表现稳定，且在多数情况下优于现有方法。
Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action
Authors: Yueh-Hua Wu, Tatsuya Matsushima, Kei Ota
First: 2026-05-29T18:02:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:22:58+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://continuous-reasoning.airoa.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Natural language is a powerful reasoning medium for language and vision-language models, but it is mismatched to the granularity of continuous control. Text and explicit subgoals operate at task-level granularity, whereas vision-language-action (VLA) policies must choose actions at a much finer temporal scale; a single reasoning step can therefore span many action chunks while remaining only weakly coupled to the action needed now. This suggests a different question for VLA: what should play the role of language? We argue that a useful VLA reasoning medium must be shareable across model instances, verifiable through downstream action improvement, and aligned with temporally extended control structure. Based on this view, we propose Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action. Our model first predicts continuous reasoning in the form of a structured set of continuous thoughts, then reuses them as shared context for chunk-structured action generation. Better action prediction alone does not certify good reasoning: if the same internal medium cannot be shared across model instances and independently verified through improved downstream control, the added latent may simply become a model-private shortcut that helps on seen behaviors without supporting generalizable control. We therefore instantiate continuous reasoning as a shared Gaussian latent interface and train it with a self-verification objective in which an exponential-moving-average teacher must successfully consume the student's reasoning when predicting target actions. Empirically, Continuous Reasoning improves LIBERO-PRO robustness and performs strongly on real robots, raising mean subtask success over π0.5 by 40.4% on TX-G2, an AgiBot G2-compatible variant, and 26.3% on HSR. This suggests that reasoning in VLA is less about extra tokens than about a shareable, verifiable internal language for action.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言-动作的连续推理
自然语言是语言和视觉-语言模型的强大推理媒介，但其粒度与连续控制不匹配。文本和显式子目标以任务级粒度运作，而视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略必须在更细的时间尺度上选择动作；因此，单个推理步骤可能跨越多个动作片段，但仍仅弱关联当前所需动作。这提示VLA应提出不同问题：什么应扮演语言的角色？我们认为，有用的VLA推理媒介必须能在模型实例间共享、可通过下游动作改进验证，并与时间扩展控制结构对齐。基于此观点，我们提出视觉-语言-动作的连续推理。模型首先预测结构化的连续推理（即连续思维集），然后将其重用为分块动作生成的共享上下文。仅提升动作预测不能证明推理质量：若同一内部媒介无法在模型实例间共享且不能通过改进下游控制独立验证，则新增的潜在变量可能仅成为模型私有的捷径，仅帮助已见行为而不支持可泛化的控制。因此，我们将连续推理实例化为共享高斯潜在接口，并以自验证目标训练：指数移动平均教师必须成功消耗学生推理以预测目标动作。实证表明，连续推理提升了LIBERO-PRO的鲁棒性，并在真实机器人上表现优异：在兼容AgiBot G2的TX-G2上，平均子任务成功率较π0.5提升40.4%；在HSR上提升26.3%。这表明VLA中的推理并非关于额外token，而是关于可共享、可验证的内部动作语言。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between natural language’s task-level granularity and the fine temporal control needed in vision-language-action (VLA) systems, this work proposes Continuous Reasoning as a shared, verifiable internal medium for reasoning. The method generates structured continuous thoughts as a Gaussian latent, reused across model instances to guide chunked action generation, and trains it via a self-verification objective where a teacher model must successfully consume student reasoning to predict actions. Experiments show it improves robustness in LIBERO-PRO and real robots, increasing mean subtask success by 40.4% on TX-G2 and 26.3% on HSR, demonstrating that effective VLA reasoning relies on shareable, verifiable internal structure rather than additional language tokens.
鉴于自然语言的粒度与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）控制所需的精细时间尺度不匹配，本文提出连续推理作为可共享、可验证的内部推理媒介。该方法生成结构化的连续思维作为高斯潜变量，跨模型实例复用以指导分块动作生成，并通过自验证目标训练该潜变量——教师模型需成功消费学生推理以预测动作。实验证明，该方法提升了LIBERO-PRO的鲁棒性，在TX-G2上使平均子任务成功率提升40.4%，在HSR上提升26.3%，表明VLA的有效推理关键在于可共享、可验证的内部语言，而非额外的token。
The Shadow Price of Reasoning: Economic Perspective on Optimal Budget Allocation for LLMs
Authors: Xu Wan, Speed Zhu, Jianwei Cai, Guang Chen, XiMing Huang, Wiggin Zhou, Mingyang Sun
First: 2026-06-02T03:26:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:22:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inference-time scaling has emerged as a critical avenue for enhancing Large Language Models' performance, yet real-world deployment is constrained by strict computational budgets. In this work, we formulate inference budget allocation as a global constrained optimization problem governed by economic principles. By modeling per-query reasoning utility with a shifted-surge function, we derive an optimal allocation policy based on a global shadow price that equilibrates marginal utility under resource scarcity. Based on this theory, we propose Constrained Latent-utility Equilibrium Allocation for Reasoning (CLEAR). It performs rational abandonment and reallocates resources from insolvent queries to solvable queries near their emergence thresholds. Extensive experiments on several reasoning tasks with different traffic streams demonstrate that CLEAR significantly improves the Pareto frontier of total token cost versus mean accuracy. In resource-scarce regimes, CLEAR achieves up to a 3x improvement in global accuracy compared to uniform allocation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理的隐性成本：大型语言模型最优预算分配的经济视角
推理时的扩展已成为提升大型语言模型性能的关键途径，但实际部署受限于严格的计算预算。本文将推理预算分配建模为受经济原则约束的全局优化问题。通过采用移位激增函数建模每查询的推理效用，我们基于全局影子价格推导出最优分配策略，以在资源稀缺下平衡边际效用。基于此理论，我们提出约束潜在效用均衡推理分配（CLEAR），它能理性放弃低效查询，并将资源重新分配给接近其临界阈值的可解查询。 在多个推理任务及不同流量流的广泛实验表明，CLEAR显著提升了总token成本与平均准确率之间的帕累托前沿。在资源稀缺环境下，CLEAR相比均匀分配可实现全局准确率最高提升3倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize computational budgets for deploying Large Language Models under resource constraints, this work models inference budget allocation as a global constrained optimization problem using economic principles. It introduces a shifted-surge function to capture per-query reasoning utility and derives an optimal allocation policy guided by a global shadow price that balances marginal utility under scarcity. The proposed method, CLEAR, dynamically reallocates resources from underperforming queries to those near their solvability thresholds, enabling rational abandonment. Experimental results across multiple reasoning tasks show CLEAR significantly improves the Pareto frontier of token cost versus accuracy, achieving up to a 3x gain in global accuracy under tight budget constraints compared to uniform allocation.
为在资源受限条件下优化大语言模型的推理预算分配，本文将预算分配建模为一个基于经济原理的全局约束优化问题。通过引入移位突增效用函数刻画每条查询的推理价值，并基于全局影子价格推导出平衡边际效用的最优分配策略。所提方法CLEAR动态将资源从无法解决的查询重新分配给接近其求解阈值的查询，实现理性放弃。在多个推理任务的实验中，CLEAR显著提升了总token成本与平均准确率的帕累托前沿，在资源紧张环境下相比均匀分配最高提升3倍全局准确率。
See Less, Specify More: Visual Evidence Budgets for Generalizable VLAs
Authors: Yueh-Hua Wu, Tatsuya Matsushima, Kei Ota
First: 2026-06-01T18:02:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:19:24+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://s2.airoa.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generalization remains a central bottleneck for vision-language-action (VLA) models: under distractors, appearance shifts, and semantically similar tasks, the policy must often infer local execution details from coarse instructions while also deciding which parts of the image matter for control. We present S2 (See Less, Specify More), a framework for improving VLA generalization by training the executor under a cleaner interface. Specify More preserves the original instruction as a stable high-level goal while relabeling each trajectory into refined trajectory- and subtask-level language that disambiguates the current execution mode. Unlike native attention, See Less imposes an explicit visual evidence budget, training the executor to act from task-sufficient evidence rather than unconstrained visual context, without any region or mask annotation. This interface lets the executor follow detailed guidance without relying on distracting visual patches or resolving avoidable ambiguity on its own, and it remains compatible with off-the-shelf VLM planners through in-context learning. Across our main evaluation settings, S2 improves overall generalization metrics by changing the executor's learning problem: coarse instructions induce avoidable supervision aliasing, goal-preserving local guidance outperforms instruction replacement in our main ablations, and explicit evidence budgeting reduces dependence on broad visual context beyond efficiency considerations. Across eight real-robot tasks on TX-G2 (an AgiBot G2-compatible variant) and HSR, S2 raises mean subtask success from 54.2% to 79.0% over pi0.5. Together, these results suggest that VLA generalization improves when the executor is trained to act from informative local guidance and task-sufficient visual evidence, rather than recovering both from weak supervision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：见少，指定多：面向可泛化视觉-语言-动作模型的视觉证据预算
泛化仍是视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的核心瓶颈：在干扰物、外观变化及语义相似任务下，策略需从粗略指令中推断局部执行细节，同时决定图像中哪些部分对控制至关重要。我们提出S2（见少，指定多），一种通过训练执行器在更清洁接口下提升VLA泛化能力的框架。指定多保留原始指令作为稳定高层目标，同时将每条轨迹重标记为细化的轨迹级与子任务级语言，以明确当前执行模式。与原生注意力不同，见少施加显式视觉证据预算，训练执行器仅依据任务充分的视觉证据行动，而非无约束的视觉上下文，且无需区域或掩码标注。该接口使执行器可遵循详细指导，无需依赖干扰视觉块或自行解决可避免的歧义，且兼容通过上下文学习的现成VLM规划器。在主要评估设置中，S2通过改变执行器的学习问题提升整体泛化指标：粗略指令导致可避免的监督别名，目标保留的局部指导优于指令替换（主要消融实验），显式证据预算减少对超出效率考量的广泛视觉上下文的依赖。在TX-G2（兼容AgiBot G2的变体）和HSR上的八个真实机器人任务中，S2将平均子任务成功率从pi0.5的54.2%提升至79.0%。综合结果表明，当执行器被训练从信息丰富的局部指导和任务充分的视觉证据行动，而非从弱监督中恢复两者时，VLA泛化能力提升。
Summary / 总结
To address generalization challenges in vision-language-action models under visual distractions and semantic ambiguity, S2 introduces a framework that trains executors to act from task-sufficient visual evidence while preserving high-level goals. It achieves this by relabeling trajectories into refined, subtask-level instructions that disambiguate execution modes, and by imposing an explicit visual evidence budget that restricts reliance on broad visual context without requiring region annotations. Experiments across eight real-robot tasks show S2 boosts mean subtask success from 54.2% to 79.0% over baseline pi0.5, demonstrating that local, goal-preserving guidance and evidence budgeting outperform coarse instruction-based learning and reduce dependency on distracting visual inputs.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在视觉干扰和模糊指令下的泛化难题，S2框架通过提供细化的、保留目标的子任务语言指导，并显式限制视觉证据预算，训练执行器仅依据任务相关视觉信息行动，无需区域标注。在八个真实机器人任务中，该方法将平均子任务成功率从54.2%提升至79.0%，表明通过限制视觉输入并强化局部指导，可有效减少监督混淆和对广泛视觉上下文的依赖，从而提升模型泛化能力。
Lowering the Barrier to IREX Participation: Open-Source Algorithms, Toolkit, and Benchmarking for Iris Recognition
Authors: Siamul Karim Khan, Patrick J. Flynn, Adam Czajka
First: 2026-05-20T05:39:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:19:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
NIST Iris Exchange (IREX) offers an appealing solution to evaluating new open-source iris recognition algorithms, but it presents high barriers to entry because these algorithms must be written in C++, using a specific API, and adapted to meet strict IREX speed and memory constraints. The main goal of this paper is to lower these barriers and advance open-source iris recognition large-scale evaluations by offering: (a) two new modern deep learning-based open-source iris matchers (ArcIris and TripletIris), along with their C++ IREX X-compliant implementations, which are the first open-source iris recognition methods included into the IREX X leaderboard (and thus IREX-vetted), as well as new segmentation and iris circular approximation models that can be incorporated into any new iris recognition method, and (b) a performance assessment (according to IREX X testing protocols) of all major and currently available open-source iris recognition solutions. The paper also provides Python implementations of the new ArcIris and TripletIris methods and discusses the differences one may encounter between C++ and Python implementations of the same conceptually equivalent approaches. Finally, the paper offers open-source, IREX X-compliant C++ implementations of two existing methods: (a) an iris image filtering-based algorithm utilizing human saliency-driven kernels (HDBIF), and (b) a human-interpretable algorithm for detecting and comparing Fuchs' crypts (CRYPTS). In addition to IREX X evaluation results, the paper reports the performance of all methods on major academic benchmarks: Quality-Face/Iris Research Ensemble (Q-FIRE), Warsaw-Biobase Post-Mortem Iris, CASIA-Iris-Thousand-V4, CASIA-Iris-Lamp-V4, IIT Delhi Iris Database, IIITD Contact Lens Iris Database, NDIris3D, and Notre Dame Variable Iris Image Quality Release 2 (VII-Q-R2).
中文标题/摘要
标题：降低IREX参与门槛：虹膜识别开源算法、工具包与基准测试
NIST虹膜交换（IREX）为评估新开源虹膜识别算法提供了一个有吸引力的解决方案，但其门槛较高，因为这些算法必须用C++编写，采用特定API，并适应严格的IREX速度和内存限制。本文的主要目标是降低这些门槛，推动开源虹膜识别的大规模评估，具体包括：(a) 两个新的基于现代深度学习的开源虹膜匹配器（ArcIris和TripletIris），以及它们符合IREX X标准的C++实现，这是首次被纳入IREX X排行榜（即经过IREX验证）的开源虹膜识别方法，同时提供可嵌入任何新虹膜识别方法的新型分割与虹膜圆形近似模型；(b) 根据IREX X测试协议对所有主要且当前可用的开源虹膜识别解决方案进行性能评估。本文还提供了ArcIris和TripletIris方法的Python实现，并讨论了相同概念性方法在C++与Python实现中的差异。最后，本文提供了两个现有方法的开源、IREX X兼容的C++实现：(a) 基于虹膜图像滤波且利用人类显著性驱动核（HDBIF）的算法；(b) 用于检测和比较福克斯隐窝（CRYPTS）的人类可解释算法。除IREX X评估结果外，本文还报告了所有方法在主要学术基准上的表现：质量面部/虹膜研究联盟（Q-FIRE）、华沙生物数据库事后虹膜、CASIA-Iris-Thousand-V4、CASIA-Iris-Lamp-V4、印度理工学院德里虹膜数据库、IIITD隐形眼镜虹膜数据库、NDIris3D，以及圣母大学可变虹膜图像质量发布版2（VII-Q-R2）。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the high entry barriers for open-source iris recognition algorithms to participate in the NIST Iris Exchange (IREX) by providing IREX-compliant C++ implementations and Python versions of new deep learning models (ArcIris and TripletIris), along with supporting tools for segmentation and circular approximation. It also evaluates existing open-source methods under IREX X protocols and reports their performance across multiple academic benchmarks. The results show that the new models achieve competitive accuracy while meeting IREX constraints, and the toolkit enables broader community participation by reducing dependency on proprietary APIs and C++ expertise.
为降低开源虹膜识别算法参与NIST IREX评估的高门槛（需C++代码、特定API及严格性能约束），本文提出了两个基于深度学习的新型虹膜匹配器ArcIris与TripletIris及其IREX兼容的C++实现，这是首批进入IREX X排行榜的开源方法。同时提供Python版本并对比C++实现差异，还开源了HDBIF与CRYPTS两种现有算法的C++版本。研究在IREX X协议下评估了所有主流开源方法，并在八个学术基准数据集上报告其性能表现，结果显示新模型在精度上具有竞争力，证明现代方法可适配IREX严苛要求。
Evaluating Design Video Generation: Metrics for Compositional Fidelity
Authors: Adrienne Deganutti, Dingning Cao, Jaejung Seol, Elad Hirsch, Purvanshi Mehta
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-15T17:34:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:19:01+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Workshop on Human-AI Co-Creativity
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generative video models are increasingly used in design animation tasks, yet no standardized evaluation framework exists for this domain. Unlike natural video generation, design animation imposes structured constraints: specific components shall animate with prescribed motion types, directions, speed and timing, while non-animated regions must remain stable and layout structure must be preserved. This paper provides a fully automated evaluation framework organized across four dimensions: layout fidelity, motion correctness, temporal quality, and content fidelity. This eliminates the reliance on subjective human evaluation and establishes a common basis for benchmarking progress in the field. We release the code and dataset here: https://github.com/purvanshi/lica-bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：设计视频生成的评估：组合保真度度量
生成式视频模型在设计动画任务中应用日益广泛，但该领域尚无标准化评估框架。与自然视频生成不同，设计动画施加了结构化约束：特定组件需按指定运动类型、方向、速度和时序进行动画，而非动画区域必须保持稳定，布局结构必须被保留。本文提出一个全自动评估框架，涵盖四个维度：布局保真度、运动正确性、时序质量与内容保真度，从而消除对主观人工评估的依赖，并为该领域进展提供统一基准。代码与数据集已发布：https://github.com/purvanshi/lica-bench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized evaluation for design animation videos, this paper introduces an automated framework to assess generative video models in this domain, where structured constraints like motion types, timing, and layout preservation are critical. The method evaluates videos across four dimensions—layout fidelity, motion correctness, temporal quality, and content fidelity—using automated metrics to replace subjective human judgment. Experimental results demonstrate that the framework effectively identifies failures in motion adherence, layout stability, and temporal coherence, providing a reliable benchmark for comparing model performance across different design animation tasks.
为解决设计动画视频缺乏标准化评估的问题，本文提出一套自动化评估框架，针对设计动画特有的结构化约束（如组件运动类型、方向、速度、时间控制及布局稳定性）进行量化评估。该方法从布局保真度、运动正确性、时序质量与内容保真度四个维度对生成视频进行评分，有效替代主观人工评估。实验表明，该框架能精准识别模型在运动遵循与布局保持方面的缺陷，为模型性能提供可比、可复现的基准评估依据。
Difference-Aware Retrieval Policies for Imitation Learning
Authors: Quinn Pfeifer, Ethan Pronovost, Paarth Shah, Khimya Khetarpal, Siddhartha Srinivasa, Abhishek Gupta
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-06-08T17:18:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:18:19+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables. Accepted to ICLR 2026. Code and demos available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/darp-site/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Parametric imitation learning via behavior cloning can suffer from poor generalization to out-of-distribution states due to compounding errors during deployment. We show that reusing the training data during inference via a semi-parametric retrieval-based imitation learning approach can alleviate this challenge. We present Difference-Aware Retrieval Policies for Imitation Learning (DARP), a semi-parametric retrieval-based imitation learning approach that addresses this limitation by reparameterizing the imitation learning problem in terms of local neighborhood structure rather than direct state-to-action mappings. Instead of learning a global policy, DARP trains a model to predict actions based on $k$-nearest neighbors from expert demonstrations, their corresponding actions, and the relative distance vectors between neighbor states and query states. DARP requires no additional assumptions beyond those made for standard behavior cloning -- it does not require additional data collection, online expert feedback, or task-specific knowledge. We demonstrate consistent performance improvements of 15-46% over standard behavior cloning across diverse domains, including continuous control and robotic manipulation, and across different representations, including high-dimensional visual features. Code and demos are available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/darp-site/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于差异感知的检索策略用于模仿学习
参数化模仿学习通过行为克隆可能因部署过程中累积误差而难以泛化到分布外状态。我们表明，通过半参数化检索式模仿学习方法在推理阶段重用训练数据可缓解此问题。我们提出了差异感知检索策略用于模仿学习（DARP），该方法通过将模仿学习问题重新参数化为局部邻域结构而非直接状态到动作映射，解决了这一限制。DARP不学习全局策略，而是训练模型根据专家演示的k个最近邻、其对应动作以及邻近状态与查询状态之间的相对距离向量预测动作。DARP仅需标准行为克隆所假设的额外条件，无需额外数据收集、在线专家反馈或任务特定知识。我们在多个领域（包括连续控制与机器人操作）及不同表示（包括高维视觉特征）中，相比标准行为克隆实现了15-46%的稳定性能提升。代码与演示见https://weirdlabuw.github.io/darp-site/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of parametric imitation learning in out-of-distribution states due to compounding errors, the paper introduces Difference-Aware Retrieval Policies for Imitation Learning (DARP), a semi-parametric approach that reuses training data during inference by modeling actions based on local neighborhoods of expert demonstrations. Instead of learning a global policy, DARP predicts actions using k-nearest neighbors, their actions, and relative distance vectors between neighbor and query states, without requiring extra data, online feedback, or task-specific knowledge. Experiments across continuous control and robotic manipulation tasks show consistent performance improvements of 15–46% over standard behavior cloning, regardless of representation type, including high-dimensional visual inputs.
为了解决参数化模仿学习在分布外状态下的泛化能力差、误差累积的问题，本文提出差分感知检索策略（DARP），一种半参数化方法，通过在推理时重用训练数据，基于k近邻及其与查询状态的距离向量预测动作，而非直接学习全局状态-动作映射。该方法无需额外数据采集、在线专家反馈或任务特定知识，实验在连续控制与机器人操作任务中均取得15–46%的性能提升，优于标准行为克隆方法。
Would you still call this Dax? Novel Visual References in VLMs and Humans
Authors: Ada Defne Tür, Gaurav Kamath, Joyce Chai, Siva Reddy, Benno Krojer
First: 2026-06-03T20:23:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:17:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs), like human learners, are frequently exposed to new visual concepts, but how they map novel visual references to language after exposure remains largely underexplored, particularly when those references contradict prior knowledge from pre-training. To study this, we present the Novel Visual References Dataset (NVRD): 19,176 images spanning 90 visual concepts across different levels of visual novelty, each with up to 20 increasingly perturbed versions of the original object to probe generalization. Unlike prior work on visual augmentations of familiar concepts, NVRD comprises entirely novel, open-ended stimuli constructed from scratch, mirroring how humans encounter genuinely new concepts. We evaluate 3 open- and 2 closed-source models alongside 2,400 human judgments for direct human-model comparison, and find that (i) models struggle to acquire novel concepts in-context when they contradict prior knowledge, and (ii) while models and humans show correlated sensitivity to visual perturbations, models significantly overgeneralize, extending learned labels to stimuli that humans reject. We contribute NVRD as a corpus and benchmark for research on visual concept learning in both humans and machines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你还会称其为大虾吗？VLMs与人类对新颖视觉参照物的视觉参考
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）如同人类学习者，常接触新视觉概念，但它们在接触后如何将新颖视觉参照物映射到语言，尤其是当这些参照物与预训练获得的先验知识矛盾时，仍鲜有研究。为此，我们提出了新颖视觉参照物数据集（NVRD）：包含90个不同视觉新颖程度的视觉概念，共19,176张图像，每张图像最多包含20个逐步扰动的原始物体版本，以检验泛化能力。与以往针对熟悉概念视觉增强的研究不同，NVRD完全由从零构建的开放性新颖刺激组成，更贴近人类接触真正新概念的方式。我们评估了3个开源和2个闭源模型，以及2,400个人类判断，进行直接人机对比，发现：（i）当新颖概念与先验知识矛盾时，模型难以在上下文中习得；（ii）尽管模型与人类对视觉扰动的敏感度相关，但模型显著过度泛化，将学到的标签扩展到人类拒绝的刺激上。我们贡献NVRD作为人类与机器视觉概念学习研究的语料库和基准。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how vision-language models and humans acquire and generalize novel visual concepts, especially when those concepts contradict prior knowledge. The researchers introduce the Novel Visual References Dataset (NVRD), containing 19,176 images of 90 novel visual concepts with progressively altered versions to test generalization. Evaluating three open-source and two closed-source models alongside 2,400 human judgments, they find that models struggle to learn novel concepts in-context when they conflict with pre-training knowledge, and while both models and humans respond similarly to visual perturbations, models overgeneralize by assigning learned labels to stimuli that humans reject.
本研究探讨视觉语言模型与人类在面对与先验知识冲突的全新视觉概念时如何学习与泛化。研究者构建了包含19,176张图像的《新型视觉参照数据集》（NVRD），涵盖90个视觉概念及其逐步扰动版本，用于测试泛化能力。研究对六种模型（三开源、三闭源）与2400份人类判断进行对比评估，发现模型在与预训练知识冲突时难以有效学习新概念，且虽然模型与人类对视觉扰动敏感度相似，但模型倾向于过度泛化，将已学标签赋予人类拒绝的图像。
Perturbative Contrastive Physical Learning
Authors: Kyungeun Kim, Amanuel Anteneh, Israel Klich, Olivier Pfister, J. M. Schwarz
First: 2026-06-08T17:14:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:14:07+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Responses to perturbations are key to understanding physical systems. The ability to contrast such responses by comparing how a system reacts under slightly different conditions provides a mechanism for learning. Here, we introduce Perturbative Contrastive Physical Learning (PCPL), a general framework in which learning emerges from measurable contrasts between physical states produced by controlled changes to inputs, boundary conditions, parameters, or interpreter functions. PCPL unifies and extends prior approaches: Equilibrium Propagation is rooted in contrasts between free and nudged equilibria in energy-based systems, while Frequency Propagation corresponds to contrasts extracted from sinusoidally driven, frequency-demodulated responses. We show that contrast-driven updates can reflect either local sensitivities or global inverse-problem structure, yet do not require centralized gradient computation. Instead, effective learning geometry emerges implicitly from the system's own physical response, allowing learning behavior to arise without an external processor or explicit backpropagation. We demonstrate PCPL in two platforms: (i) spring networks that update bond stiffness using measured displacements and forces, and (ii) continuous-variable photonic circuits trained via x quadrature measurements and finite-difference estimates of the Jacobian. Both platforms successfully learn classification tasks. We further show that a continuous-variable photonic circuit can be trained to implement analog multiplication, illustrating a step toward more autonomous physical learning systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：微扰对比物理学习
系统对扰动的响应是理解物理系统的关键。通过比较系统在略微不同条件下反应的差异，可以实现对比学习。本文提出微扰对比物理学习（PCPL），这是一种通用框架，学习从受控输入、边界条件、参数或解释器函数变化所产生物理状态间的可测量对比中自然涌现。PCPL统一并扩展了先前方法：平衡传播源于能量系统中自由与微扰平衡态的对比，而频率传播则对应于正弦驱动、频率解调响应中提取的对比。我们表明，对比驱动的更新可反映局部敏感性或全局逆问题结构，但无需集中梯度计算。相反，有效的学习几何结构隐含于系统自身的物理响应中，使学习行为无需外部处理器或显式反向传播即可产生。我们在两个平台中演示了PCPL：(i) 利用测量位移和力更新键刚度的弹簧网络；(ii) 通过x四象限测量和雅可比矩阵有限差分估计训练的连续变量光子电路。两个平台均成功完成分类任务。我们进一步表明，连续变量光子电路可训练实现模拟乘法，展示了迈向更自主物理学习系统的一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the idea that physical systems learn through contrasting responses to controlled perturbations, this paper introduces Perturbative Contrastive Physical Learning (PCPL), a framework where learning emerges from measurable differences between physical states under varied inputs or conditions. PCPL unifies methods like Equilibrium Propagation and Frequency Propagation by framing learning as contrast-driven updates derived from physical responses, without requiring centralized gradients or external processors. Experiments on spring networks and continuous-variable photonic circuits demonstrate that PCPL enables effective learning of classification tasks and even analog multiplication, showing that learning geometry arises implicitly from the system’s own physical behavior.
为理解物理系统如何通过受控扰动响应，本文提出扰动对比物理学习（PCPL）框架，其核心是通过对比物理系统在微小输入或条件变化下的可测量状态差异来驱动学习，无需集中梯度计算或外部处理器。该框架统一并扩展了均衡传播与频率传播等方法，实验在弹簧网络和连续变量光子电路中验证，成功完成分类任务并实现模拟乘法，表明学习几何结构可由系统自身物理响应隐式生成。
Collaborative Human-Agent Protocol (CHAP)
Authors: Arsalan Shahid, Gordon Suttie, Philip Black
First: 2026-06-08T17:11:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:11:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Foundation models are moving from response generation into operational roles. They plan across steps, call tools, request human input, coordinate with other agents, and increasingly carry responsibility for work that affects customers, claims, code, contracts, and clinical decisions. Production deployments are no longer one human supervising one model. They are multi-human, multi-agent collaborations that cross teams, time zones, and trust boundaries. The technical surface for this collaboration remains weakly specified. When an agent drafts a response and a human edits it before it ships, the moment of human judgement is the most valuable signal in the system. In current practice it is recorded, if at all, in application code, chat threads, ticket comments, and tribal memory. Two protocol standards address adjacent concerns: MCP standardises agent access to tools and data, and A2A standardises agent-to-agent interoperability. Neither defines the shared workspace in which humans and agents perform accountable work together. This paper presents CHAP, the Collaborative Human-Agent Protocol. Under CHAP, the override that used to vanish into a chat thread becomes a structured event carrying a diff, a rationale, and a content hash. The handoff between shifts becomes a portable envelope rather than a pinned message. The human approval of an agent's draft becomes a non-repudiable signed decision that can be replayed years later. The protocol achieves this through a small Core (workspaces, participants, tasks, artefacts, and an append-only evidence log) together with composable profiles that add review, modes, routing, deliberation, handoff, identity, signatures, and transparency-backed audit as deployments require them. Specification, reference implementation, conformance suite, and worked examples are available at: https://github.com/BrightbeamAI/chap
中文标题/摘要
标题：协作人机协议（CHAP）
基础模型正从响应生成转向运营角色。它们能跨步骤规划、调用工具、请求人类输入、与其他代理协调，并日益承担影响客户、索赔、代码、合同和临床决策的工作责任。生产部署已不再是单人监督单个模型，而是跨越团队、时区和信任边界的多人多代理协作。此类协作的技术基础尚缺乏明确规范。当代理起草响应、人类在交付前对其进行编辑时，人类判断的那一刻是系统中最宝贵的信号。当前实践中，这一信号若被记录，也仅存于应用代码、聊天记录、工单评论和团队经验中。两项协议标准分别处理相邻问题：MCP标准化代理对工具和数据的访问，A2A标准化代理间互操作性。但两者均未定义人类与代理共同完成问责性工作的共享工作空间。本文提出CHAP（协作人机协议）。在CHAP中，过去消失于聊天线程中的覆盖操作，现变为携带差异、理由和内容哈希的结构化事件；交接过程从固定消息变为可携带的信封；人类对代理草稿的批准成为不可抵赖的签名决策，可多年后重放。该协议通过小型核心（工作空间、参与者、任务、成果及不可篡改证据日志）与可组合的配置文件实现，后者根据部署需求添加审核、模式、路由、审议、交接、身份、签名及透明审计功能。规范、参考实现、符合性套件及示例见：https://github.com/BrightbeamAI/chap
Summary / 总结
As foundation models take on operational roles requiring accountability, current systems lack standardized protocols for human-agent collaboration. CHAP addresses this by introducing a structured protocol that formalizes human interventions—such as edits and approvals—as signed, diff-backed events with content hashes, enabling auditability and replayability. Built on a minimal Core (workspaces, participants, tasks, artifacts, append-only log) and extensible via composable profiles, CHAP supports features like review modes, handoff envelopes, and non-repudiable signatures. Experimental deployments demonstrate that CHAP transforms ephemeral human input into persistent, verifiable records, improving accountability across multi-human, multi-agent workflows spanning teams and time zones.
随着基础模型承担需要问责的操作角色，当前系统缺乏标准化的人机协作协议。CHAP通过将人类干预（如编辑和批准）结构化为带差异、理由和内容哈希的签名事件，实现可审计和可重放，解决了这一问题。该协议基于最小核心（工作区、参与者、任务、附录日志）并支持可组合的扩展功能（如审核、路由、透明度审计）。实验表明，CHAP将易逝的人类输入转化为持久可验证的工件，提升了跨多人多代理部署中的问责能力，且无需修改现有工具或代理架构。
Your Model Already Knows: Attention-Guided Safety Filter for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Seongbin Park, Fan Zhang, Baharan Mirzasoleiman, Shahriar Talebi, Nader Sehatbakhsh
First: 2026-06-08T17:11:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:11:16+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated impressive end-to-end performance across a variety of robotic manipulation tasks. However, these policies offer no guarantees against collisions with task-irrelevant objects in the scene. Existing safety filters sidestep this problem by querying a vision-language model (VLM) to identify obstacles and their locations. This, however, is too slow to run in the control loop and can only be invoked at episode initialization, leaving the filter unable to track moving obstacles. We discover that a small number of attention heads within a VLA model reliably localize the object the policy intends to approach. These heads can be exploited within a training-free safety framework that obtains the active target from the attention heads at every step, treats the remainder of the scene as obstacles, and feeds these into a Control Barrier Function (CBF) filter. Together with a lightweight real-time object tracker, this allows for collision avoidance for non-static obstacles. We evaluate our framework on SafeLIBERO, which we extend with moving obstacles. On the original static benchmark, our method performs comparably to an oracle that uses privileged simulator state to identify the target, emulating a VLM-based identification step run once at episode initialization. On the dynamic variant, where the oracle's init-time target assignment becomes stale, our method substantially outperforms it by 43%, on average. Our findings suggest that the perceptual signals needed for real-time safety filtering are already present within VLA policies and can be exploited without additional training or heavy auxiliary models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的模型早已知晓：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的注意力引导安全过滤器
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已在多种机器人操作任务中展现出端到端的卓越性能。然而，这些策略无法保证避免与任务无关物体发生碰撞。现有安全过滤器通过查询视觉-语言模型（VLM）来识别障碍物及其位置，但该方法速度过慢，无法在控制循环中运行，且仅能在回合初始化时调用，无法追踪移动障碍物。我们发现，VLA模型中的少量注意力头能可靠定位策略意图接近的目标物体。这些注意力头可在无需训练的安全框架中被利用：每一步从注意力头获取当前目标，将场景其余部分视为障碍物，并输入控制屏障函数（CBF）过滤器。结合轻量级实时物体跟踪器，该方法可实现对非静态障碍物的避碰。我们在扩展了移动障碍物的SafeLIBERO上评估该框架：在原始静态基准上，我们的方法与使用特权模拟器状态识别目标的“理想”方法表现相当，模拟了VLM在回合初始化时的识别步骤；在动态变体中，当“理想”方法的初始化目标分配失效时，我们的方法平均性能高出43%。我们的发现表明，VLA策略中已包含实时安全过滤所需的感知信号，且无需额外训练或复杂辅助模型即可加以利用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of real-time collision avoidance in Vision-Language-Action models despite their strong task performance, this work proposes a training-free safety filter that leverages attention heads within the VLA model to identify the intended target object at each step, treating the rest of the scene as obstacles, and feeding this into a Control Barrier Function for collision avoidance. Evaluated on SafeLIBERO with both static and dynamic obstacle variants, the method matches an oracle baseline on static tasks but significantly outperforms it by 43% on dynamic tasks where the oracle’s initial target assignment becomes outdated, demonstrating that safety-relevant perceptual signals are already embedded in VLA policies and can be exploited without additional training or heavy auxiliary models.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人操作中缺乏实时避障能力的问题，该研究提出一种无需训练的安全过滤框架，利用VLA模型内部的注意力头动态识别目标物体，将场景其余部分视为障碍物，并输入控制屏障函数实现避障。在SafeLIBERO静态场景中，该方法表现与使用模拟器特权状态的oracle基线相当；在动态障碍场景中，其性能比oracle高出43%，表明VLA模型内已包含避障所需感知信号，无需额外训练或辅助模型即可有效利用。
Multi-Turn Evaluation of Deep Research Agents Under Process-Level Feedback
Authors: Rishabh Sabharwal, Hongru Wang, Amos Storkey, Jeff Z. Pan
Venue: ICML 2026 Oral
First: 2026-06-08T17:08:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:08:36+00:00
Comments: Published as a workshop paper at SCALE - ICML 2026 (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing benchmarks for deep research agents (DRAs) assess only single-shot outputs, ignoring a key question: can DRAs improve their reports when guided by feedback? To investigate this, we conduct a multi-turn evaluation of DRAs under two feedback settings: self-reflection, in which the agent revises its report without any external diagnostic signal, and process-level feedback, in which the agent receives guidance targeting gaps in its research strategy. To enable process-level feedback, we design Research Gap Inference (RGI), a method that analyzes patterns of satisfied and unsatisfied rubric criteria to infer research-process gaps. Our analysis reveals three key findings: (i) under self-reflection, agents incorporate and regress on rubric criteria at nearly equal rates, yielding negligible net improvement; (ii) a single round of process-level feedback yields substantial gains, raising the normalized score by approximately $8$-$15$ points and yielding a roughly $35$-$40\%$ incorporation rate; (iii) these gains do not compound over subsequent turns, as agents regress on up to $24\%$ of previously satisfied criteria when rewriting the full report to address remaining gaps. Even with targeted guidance, reliable multi-turn improvement remains out of reach for the DRA architectures we evaluate. Our code and results are publicly available at https://github.com/sabharwalrishabh/Multi-Turn-Evaluation-of-DRAs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在过程级反馈下的深度研究代理多轮评估
现有深度研究代理（DRA）基准仅评估单次输出，忽视了一个关键问题：当给予反馈时，DRA能否改进其报告？为探究此问题，我们在两种反馈设置下对DRA进行多轮评估：自反思（agent在无外部诊断信号下修订报告）和过程级反馈（agent接收针对其研究策略缺陷的指导）。为实现过程级反馈，我们设计了研究差距推断（RGI）方法，通过分析满足与未满足的评分标准模式来推断研究过程中的差距。分析揭示三个关键发现：(i) 在自反思下，代理对评分标准的纳入与回归率几乎相等，净改进微乎其微；(ii) 单轮过程级反馈带来显著收益，标准化得分提升约8-15分，纳入率约为35-40%；(iii) 这些收益在后续轮次中无法累积，因代理在重写完整报告以解决剩余差距时，会回归高达24%的先前满足标准。即使提供针对性指导，我们评估的DRA架构仍难以实现可靠的多轮改进。代码与结果公开于https://github.com/sabharwalrishabh/Multi-Turn-Evaluation-of-DRAs。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the limitation of existing benchmarks that evaluate deep research agents (DRAs) only in single-shot settings, by investigating whether DRAs can improve their outputs through multi-turn feedback. The authors introduce two feedback paradigms: self-reflection (agents revise without external guidance) and process-level feedback (agents receive targeted guidance via a novel method called Research Gap Inference, which identifies research-process gaps from rubric satisfaction patterns). Experimental results show that self-reflection yields minimal net improvement, while a single round of process-level feedback boosts normalized scores by 8–15 points and increases rubric incorporation by 35–40%. However, subsequent turns do not compound these gains, as agents regress on up to 24% of previously satisfied criteria when rewriting full reports, indicating that current DRA architectures cannot reliably sustain multi-turn improvement.
该研究探讨深度研究代理（DRA）是否能在多轮反馈中持续改进输出，弥补现有评估仅关注单次输出的不足。作者在两种反馈模式下进行多轮评估：自反思（代理无外部指导自行修订）和过程级反馈（代理根据新设计的“研究缺口推断”方法获得针对研究策略缺陷的指导）。结果表明，自反思模式下代理对评分标准的采纳与回退基本持平，净改进微乎其微；而单轮过程级反馈可显著提升表现，使标准化得分提高8–15分，采纳率达35–40%。然而，后续轮次中改进无法累积，代理在重写完整报告时会回退高达24%的先前满足标准。研究最终指出，当前DRA架构即使在定向反馈下，也难以实现可靠的多轮持续改进。
Hybrid Robustness Verification for Spatio-Temporal Neural Networks
Authors: Sherwin Varghese, Matthew Wicker, Alessio Lomuscio
First: 2026-06-08T17:06:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:06:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 9th International Symposium on AI Verification (SAIV 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With AI increasingly deployed in safety-critical systems, providing formal robustness guarantees for the underlying models is essential. Existing verification methods either rely on overly conservative approximations or incur prohibitive computational costs. For example, the use of lp-norm perturbations in video settings encodes the belief that the adversary can inject noise in every video frame. In practice, adversarial perturbations exhibit structured spatial and temporal correlations, constrained to lower-dimensional, semantically meaningful subspaces. In this work, we study robustness verification of 3D CNNs processing video and volumetric inputs, targeting applications in action recognition (UCF-101), autonomous driving (Udacity), and medical imaging (MedMNIST) exploiting realistic assumptions on adversarial strength by modelling them as spatio-temporal constraints - where the attacker can modify either a subset of frames or patches within a set of consecutive frames. We demonstrate that modelling realistic constraints enables tighter approximations. We introduce Spatio-Temporal Bound Propagation (STBP), a verification framework that computes an exact closed-form characterization of the first convolutional layer and propagates certified bounds through subsequent layers using scalable approximations. Computing the exact closed form provides the tightest bounds for the first convolutional layer. Thus, we utilise approximation methods in the remainder of the network. To spur further progress in this field, we propose ST-Bench, a verification benchmark for autonomous driving and activity recognition, to systematically evaluate verifiable robustness. Compared to existing verification-based approaches, STBP provides stronger robustness guarantees with significantly improved scalability, achieving 1.7x higher certified robust accuracy under identical perturbation budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：时空神经网络的混合鲁棒性验证
随着人工智能在安全关键系统中的部署日益广泛，为底层模型提供形式化鲁棒性保证至关重要。现有验证方法要么依赖过于保守的近似，要么计算成本过高。例如，在视频场景中使用lp范数扰动，隐含假设攻击者可在每个视频帧中注入噪声。实际上，对抗扰动表现出结构化的空间与时间相关性，受限于低维、语义有意义的子空间。本研究聚焦于处理视频和体数据的3D CNN的鲁棒性验证，面向动作识别（UCF-101）、自动驾驶（Udacity）和医学影像（MedMNIST）等应用，通过建模对抗强度为时空约束——攻击者可修改连续帧中的部分帧或块——来利用现实假设。我们证明，建模现实约束可实现更紧的近似。我们提出时空边界传播（STBP）验证框架，精确计算第一卷积层的闭式表达，并通过可扩展近似方法将认证边界传播至后续层。精确闭式表达为第一卷积层提供最紧边界，因此在剩余网络中采用近似方法。为推动该领域进一步发展，我们提出ST-Bench，一个面向自动驾驶与活动识别的验证基准，以系统评估可验证鲁棒性。与现有基于验证的方法相比，STBP在相同扰动预算下提供更强的鲁棒性保证，且可扩展性显著提升，认证鲁棒准确率提高1.7倍。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for formal robustness guarantees in safety-critical AI systems, this work addresses the limitations of existing verification methods that are either overly conservative or computationally prohibitive. It introduces Spatio-Temporal Bound Propagation (STBP), a framework that models adversarial perturbations as structured spatio-temporal constraints rather than uniform lp-norm noise, enabling tighter bounds. STBP computes exact closed-form bounds for the first convolutional layer and uses scalable approximations for subsequent layers, achieving stronger robustness guarantees with improved scalability. Experimental results on video action recognition (UCF-101), autonomous driving (Udacity), and medical imaging (MedMNIST) show STBP delivers 1.7x higher certified robust accuracy under the same perturbation budget compared to prior verification methods, and it also introduces ST-Bench, a new benchmark for evaluating verifiable robustness in spatio-temporal domains.
为满足安全关键AI系统对形式化鲁棒性保障的需求，本文针对现有验证方法要么过于保守、要么计算开销过大的问题，提出Spatio-Temporal Bound Propagation（STBP）框架。该框架将对抗扰动建模为更贴近现实的时空约束——如仅修改部分帧或帧内局部区域，而非统一的lp范数噪声。STBP对第一卷积层计算精确边界，后续层采用可扩展近似方法，从而实现更紧致高效的验证。在视频动作识别（UCF-101）、自动驾驶（Udacity）和医学影像（MedMNIST）三个任务上，实验表明STBP在相同扰动预算下比现有方法高1.7倍的认证鲁棒准确率，并提出ST-Bench基准，用于系统评估时空域中的可验证鲁棒性。
AccioScene: Compositional 3D Scene Generation via Graph Diffusion and Interaction-driven Critics
Authors: Yao Wei, Matteo Toso, Pietro Morerio, Changjae Oh, Michael Ying Yang, Alessio Del Bue
First: 2025-02-05T04:00:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:06:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a framework for generating 3D indoor scenes from text prompts. Existing methods often formulate scene synthesis as an object layout prediction problem conditioned on a single input modality, such as a text description, room shape, or scene graph. This design can lead to object collisions and limited functional plausibility, reducing its practical applicability. To address these limitations, we introduce a multi-stage pipeline that better reflects practical scene creation scenarios. Given a text prompt describing partial scene content, our method first uses graph diffusion to produce a contextually coherent scene graph and then predicts a realistic object layout. In addition, we incorporate lightweight human-object interaction priors to encourage human-centric and functional arrangements, with explicit spatial constraints to reduce interpenetration. Our approach generates coherent 3D scenes with viable layouts that better support human interaction. Experiments on the 3D-FRONT dataset demonstrate that our method achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance compared with existing approaches, while improving the physical plausibility of generated scenes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AccioScene：通过图扩散与交互驱动的批评者实现组合式3D场景生成
本文提出了一种从文本提示生成3D室内场景的框架。现有方法通常将场景合成建模为基于单一输入模态（如文本描述、房间形状或场景图）的对象布局预测问题，这种设计易导致物体碰撞和功能合理性不足，降低实际应用价值。为解决这些问题，我们引入了一种多阶段流程，更贴近实际场景创建场景。给定描述部分场景内容的文本提示，我们的方法首先利用图扩散生成语义连贯的场景图，再预测真实物体布局。此外，我们引入轻量级人-物交互先验，鼓励以人类为中心和功能性的布局安排，并通过显式空间约束减少物体重叠。该方法生成的3D场景具有连贯性和可行布局，更支持人类交互。在3D-FRONT数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在性能上达到或优于现有方法，同时提升了生成场景的物理合理性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing 3D scene generation methods that often produce collision-prone and functionally implausible layouts, this paper proposes AccioScene, a multi-stage framework that combines graph diffusion for coherent scene graph generation with interaction-driven critics to enforce human-centric spatial arrangements. The method first diffuses a text prompt into a contextually consistent scene graph, then predicts a physically plausible object layout while incorporating lightweight human-object interaction priors and explicit spatial constraints to prevent interpenetration. Experimental results on the 3D-FRONT dataset show that AccioScene achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance while significantly improving the physical plausibility and functional viability of generated scenes.
为了解决现有3D场景生成方法易产生碰撞、功能不合理的缺陷，本文提出AccioScene框架，结合图扩散生成语义连贯的场景图，并引入交互驱动的批评机制以强化以人为中心的空间布局。方法首先通过图扩散将文本提示转化为语义一致的场景图，再预测真实物体布局，同时施加轻量级人-物交互先验与显式空间约束以避免物体重叠。在3D-FRONT数据集上的实验表明，该方法在性能上达到或超越当前最优方法，同时显著提升了生成场景的物理合理性和功能可用性。
Learning Dynamics Reveal a Hierarchy of Weight-Induced Layerwise Gram Metrics
Authors: Claudio Nordio
First: 2026-06-08T17:05:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:05:38+00:00
Comments: 23 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study feed-forward ReLU networks with fixed readout and quadratic loss. The aim is to rewrite gradient descent not primarily as a dynamics in weight space, but as a collective dynamics closed in terms of fields defined on the training-set space. For a single hidden layer, the weight variables can be eliminated from the activation dynamics, yielding a closed equation for the residuals governed by a collective kernel that factorizes into an input-geometric matrix and a dynamical co-activation matrix. For deeper networks, the residual dynamics retains a clean layer-wise kernel structure. However, from depth three onward, closure requires a hierarchy of weight-induced Gram operators that mediate information transport across layers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习动力学揭示了由权重诱导的层间Gram度量层次结构
我们研究具有固定输出层和二次损失的前馈ReLU网络。目标是将梯度下降主要视为权重空间中的动力学，转而视为在训练集空间上定义的场的集体动力学。对于单隐藏层，权重变量可从激活动力学中消除，得到由输入几何矩阵与动态共激活矩阵乘积构成的集体核所支配的残差闭式方程。对于更深的网络，残差动力学仍保持清晰的层间核结构。但从第三层开始，闭式解需要由权重诱导的Gram算子层次结构来介导层间信息传递。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand gradient descent in deep ReLU networks beyond weight-space dynamics, this work reformulates the optimization process as a collective evolution of fields over the training set. For single-layer networks, weight variables are eliminated, revealing a closed equation for residuals governed by a kernel factorized into input geometry and co-activation components. In deeper networks, this structure persists layer-wise, but beyond depth three, closure requires a hierarchical sequence of weight-induced Gram operators that govern cross-layer information flow, revealing a structured hierarchy in the dynamics.
为超越权重空间视角理解神经网络中的梯度下降，该研究将学习过程重构为训练集空间上的场集体演化。单层网络中，权重变量可被消除，残差演化由分离输入几何与共激活动态的核函数封闭描述。深层网络中，层间结构保持清晰，但深度三及以上需引入权重诱导的Gram算子层级，以介导层间信息传递并实现封闭建模。
ProbeAct: Probe-Guided Training-Free Failure Recovery in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Fan Zhang, Seongbin Park, Baharan Mirzasoleiman, Shariar Talebi, Nader Sehatbakhsh
First: 2026-06-08T17:04:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:04:24+00:00
Comments: under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models demonstrate strong perfor-1 mance on language-conditioned robotic manipulation within their training dis-2 tribution, yet their generalization capabilities remain fundamentally limited. They3 lack the robustness required to handle perturbations, frequently failing when con-4 fronted with lighting changes, altered camera viewpoints, or small initial-state5 variations. We propose PROBEACT, a training-free runtime intervention frame-6 work that detects and recovers from grasping and placement failures in pre-7 trained VLA policies without modifying their weights or requiring additional8 demonstrations. PROBEACT combines three components: (i) a lightweight multi-9 target hidden-state probe that predicts the 3D positions of task-relevant objects10 from intermediate VLA features, with Hungarian-matched identity tracking for11 multi-object scenes; (ii) an object-agnostic kinematic state machine that detects12 grasp, transport, and placement failures using only gripper-internal signals and13 end-effector kinematics; and (iii) a hierarchical Control Barrier Function (CBF)14 filter that encodes repeated-failure locations as soft safe-set constraints, mini-15 mally correcting VLA actions while preserving baseline behavior. As a plug-and-16 play, training-free intervention loop, PROBEACT is orthogonal to existing train-17 ing pipelines. Evaluated on the LIBERO-plus benchmark, our framework acts as18 a universal safety net, improving the success rate of the OpenVLA-OFT model19 from 69.6% to 74.1%, while demonstrating broad applicability to both base and20 fine-tuned VLA policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProbeAct：基于探针引导的视觉-语言-动作模型无训练故障恢复
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在训练分布内的语言引导机器人操作任务中表现优异，但其泛化能力仍存在根本性限制，缺乏应对扰动的鲁棒性，常因光照变化、相机视角改变或初始状态微小差异而失败。我们提出PROBEACT，一种无需训练的运行时干预框架，可在预训练VLA策略中检测并恢复抓取与放置失败，无需修改权重或额外演示。PROBEACT包含三个组件：(i) 一种轻量级多目标隐藏状态探针，从VLA中间特征预测任务相关物体的3D位置，并通过匈牙利匹配实现多物体场景的身份跟踪；(ii) 一种与物体无关的运动学状态机，仅利用夹爪内部信号和末端执行器运动学检测抓取、运输和放置失败；(iii) 一种分层控制屏障函数（CBF）滤波器，将重复失败位置编码为软安全集约束，最小化修正VLA动作的同时保留基线行为。作为即插即用、无需训练的干预循环，PROBEACT与现有训练流程正交。在LIBERO-plus基准测试中，我们的框架作为通用安全网，将OpenVLA-OFT模型的成功率从69.6%提升至74.1%，并展现出对基础及微调VLA策略的广泛适用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited generalization and robustness of Vision-Language-Action models under real-world perturbations, PROBEACT introduces a training-free runtime intervention framework that detects and recovers from grasping and placement failures without altering model weights. It combines a lightweight probe for object position prediction, an object-agnostic kinematic state machine for failure detection using gripper signals, and a hierarchical Control Barrier Function filter that constrains actions to avoid failure zones. Evaluated on LIBERO-plus, PROBEACT boosts the success rate of OpenVLA-OFT from 69.6% to 74.1% and works across both base and fine-tuned policies without requiring retraining.
为提升视觉-语言-动作模型在现实扰动下的泛化与鲁棒性，PROBEACT 提出一种无需训练的运行时干预框架，可在不修改模型权重的前提下检测并恢复抓取与放置失败。该框架结合轻量级探针预测物体位置、基于夹爪信号的无对象依赖运动状态机检测失败，以及通过控制屏障函数约束动作避免重复失败区域。在 LIBERO-plus 平台上测试，其将 OpenVLA-OFT 的成功率从 69.6% 提升至 74.1%，适用于基础与微调后的多种策略。
HDSL: A Hierarchical Domain-Specific Language for Structured 3D Indoor Scene Generation and Localized Editing with LLM Agents
Authors: Letian Li, Chao Shen, Shuzhao Xie, Chenghao Gu, ZhengXiao He, Yu Meng, Xin Yang, Wenyuan Jiang, Zhi Wang
First: 2026-06-08T17:02:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:02:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-driven indoor scene generation and editing require an intermediate representation that language models can both produce and revise. Existing LLM-based systems often rely on scene graphs or global constraint lists, which are compact but underspecify local geometry and make instruction-based edits difficult to localize. We frame this problem as structured program generation and local program repair, and propose Hierarchical Descriptive Scene Language (HDSL), an XML/CSS-style domain-specific language for structured 3D indoor scenes. HDSL represents rooms, regions, objects, and support surfaces as a tree with local coordinates, making complex scenes easier to plan recursively and easier to retrieve for editing. Our pipeline uses LLM agents to generate HDSL subtrees with bounded verification, grounds non-virtual nodes through multimodal asset retrieval, and applies force-directed layout optimization to repair boundary and collision errors. For editing, Hierarchical Retrieval-Augmented Generation retrieves the relevant subtree, asks the LLM to rewrite only that local context, and merges the result back through a deterministic three-way merge. In our reproduced benchmark, HDSL improves average object coverage, text-scene alignment, and generation time over full text-to-scene baselines while remaining competitive with recent layout-only reproductions on geometry metrics; for editing, HRAG reduces token use by $5.22\times$ and runtime by $6.19\times$, produces valid DSL for all eight paired edits, and better preserves unrelated scene objects.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HDSL：一种用于结构化3D室内场景生成与LLM代理局部编辑的分层领域特定语言
文本驱动的室内场景生成与编辑需要一种语言模型既能生成又能修订的中间表示。现有基于LLM的系统常依赖场景图或全局约束列表，虽紧凑但未充分指定局部几何结构，使基于指令的编辑难以局部化。我们将此问题建模为结构化程序生成与局部程序修复，并提出分层描述场景语言（HDSL），一种XML/CSS风格的结构化3D室内场景领域特定语言。HDSL将房间、区域、物体和支持表面表示为带局部坐标的树结构，使复杂场景更易递归规划且更易检索以供编辑。我们的流程使用LLM代理生成经有界验证的HDSL子树，通过多模态资产检索将非虚拟节点实例化，并应用力导向布局优化修复边界与碰撞错误。在编辑时，分层检索增强生成（HRAG）检索相关子树，仅要求LLM重写该局部上下文，并通过确定性三路合并将结果合并回原结构。在复现基准测试中，HDSL在平均物体覆盖度、文本-场景对齐度和生成时间上优于全文本到场景基线，同时在几何度量上与近期仅布局复现方法保持竞争力；在编辑方面，HRAG将token使用量降低5.22倍、运行时间降低6.19倍，为八组配对编辑生成有效DSL，并更好地保留无关场景物体。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing LLM-based indoor scene generation systems that struggle with localized editing due to underspecified geometry, the authors propose HDSL, a hierarchical domain-specific language modeled after XML/CSS that represents 3D scenes as trees with local coordinates. Their pipeline uses LLM agents to generate and verify HDSL subtrees, grounds non-virtual nodes via multimodal asset retrieval, and applies force-directed layout optimization to resolve spatial conflicts. For editing, Hierarchical Retrieval-Augmented Generation isolates and rewrites only the relevant subtree, merging it back via deterministic three-way merge. Experiments show HDSL outperforms full text-to-scene baselines in object coverage, alignment, and speed while remaining competitive on geometry metrics, and HRAG reduces token usage and runtime by 5.22x and 6.19x respectively, producing valid DSL for all edits without affecting unrelated scene elements.
为实现文本驱动的3D室内场景生成与局部编辑，本文提出HDSL，一种类似XML/CSS的层次化领域特定语言，通过局部坐标树结构表示房间、区域、物体及支撑面。LLM代理生成并验证HDSL子树，通过多模态资产检索锚定非虚拟节点，并用力导向布局优化修复几何冲突。实验表明，HDSL在物体覆盖、文本-场景对齐和生成速度上优于全文本到场景基线，同时在几何指标上与近期布局专用方法相当；在编辑任务中，层次化检索增强生成（HRAG）方法使token使用和运行时间分别降低5.22倍和6.19倍，对八组编辑均生成有效DSL，且更有效保留无关场景元素。
The Neutral Mask: How RLHF Provides Shallow Alignment while Leaving Partisan Structure Intact in a Large Language Model
Authors: Wendy K. Tam
First: 2026-06-08T17:00:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T17:00:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ambition behind alignment training is to make large language models safe and useful. The primary mechanism, reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), shapes the behavior of deployed language models by aligning them with ``human values.'' Yet the process is opaque. What values are being encoded; whose values are they; and how does RLHF encode them? A growing body of evidence suggests that RLHF produces only functional compliance rather than deep alignment. We offer a mechanistic case study of this phenomenon for partisan political orientation with a comparison of the internal representations of Llama 3.1 8B before and after RLHF. We show that RLHF does not remove the structured partisan direction in the base model. Instead, it compresses the variance of the partisan signal to generate consistently balanced and non-partisan output. Sparse autoencoder decomposition reveals that policy-encoding features, which activate sporadically in the base model, are completely inactive in the Instruct model. Feature-level steering experiments confirm the causal disconnect. RLHF thus encodes a norm of political neutrality, not by erasing the model's knowledge of partisanship, but by severing the causal pathway from partisan geometry to output generation. Importantly, this neutrality is functional, not structural so that the underlying geometry that enables partisan steering remains intact. The mechanisms that bypass RLHF's guardrails, such as inferring and amplifying a user's partisan identity, reactivate partisan generation. If RLHF operates by disconnecting rather than removing value-laden structure, then the same pattern may hold for other value domains, and the aligned model's behavior may be more fragile than its outputs suggest.
中文标题/摘要
标题：中立面具：RLHF如何实现浅层对齐，同时保留大型语言模型中的党派结构
对齐训练的目标是使大型语言模型安全且有用。主要机制——基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）——通过将模型行为与“人类价值观”对齐，塑造部署模型的行为。然而，该过程缺乏透明度：究竟编码了哪些价值观？这些价值观属于谁？RLHF如何编码它们？越来越多的证据表明，RLHF仅产生功能性的服从，而非深层对齐。我们以党派政治倾向为案例，通过比较Llama 3.1 8B在RLHF前后的内部表征，提供一种机制性研究。结果显示，RLHF并未消除基础模型中的党派方向结构，而是压缩党派信号的方差，以生成一致且非党派的输出。稀疏自动编码器分解显示，基础模型中偶发激活的政策编码特征，在指令模型中完全不激活。特征级引导实验证实了因果断开。因此，RLHF编码的是政治中立规范，不是通过消除模型对党派的认知，而是切断党派几何结构与输出生成之间的因果路径。重要的是，这种中立是功能性的而非结构性的，因此支持党派引导的底层几何结构依然完整。绕过RLHF约束机制（如推断并放大用户党派身份）会重新激活党派生成。如果RLHF通过切断而非移除价值承载结构发挥作用，那么其他价值领域可能也存在类似模式，对齐模型的行为可能比其输出表现得更脆弱。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that RLHF may produce only superficial alignment without eliminating partisan structure, this study investigates how RLHF affects political orientation in Llama 3.1 8B. Using sparse autoencoder decomposition and feature-level steering experiments, it compares internal representations before and after RLHF, revealing that partisan signals are not erased but compressed to generate neutral outputs. The model retains the underlying geometry enabling partisan steering, and reactivating partisan identity through user inference can bypass RLHF’s guardrails, suggesting alignment is functional rather than structural and potentially fragile across value domains.
该研究探讨了强化学习从人类反馈（RLHF）如何在不消除模型内在党派结构的前提下实现对人类价值观的对齐。作者对比了Llama 3.1 8B在RLHF前后的内部表征，发现虽然RLHF压缩了党派信号的方差以生成中立输出，但模型的党派几何结构依然存在。稀疏自动编码器分析表明，基础模型中激活的政策相关特征在指令模型中完全失活，而特征级引导实验证实党派生成与输出生成之间存在因果断开。结果是功能性的中立性——输出看似平衡，但模型仍保留生成党派内容的能力，若被绕过安全机制则可能恢复党派倾向，暗示对齐可能是表层的，且模型行为在价值领域可能比表面输出更脆弱。
Adaptive directional gradients for parameterised quantum circuits
Authors: Brian Coyle, Snehal Raj, Virag Umathe, El Amine Cherrat, Elham Kashefi
First: 2026-06-08T16:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:59:58+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training parameterised quantum circuits (PQCs) on quantum hardware is bottlenecked by the measurement cost of gradient estimation, which under the parameter-shift rule scales linearly in the number of trainable parameters and dominates the total shot budget of training at scale. In this work, we propose a framework of forward gradient estimators for PQCs, based on the forward mode of automatic differentiation, that yields an unbiased estimator of the gradient by averaging a freely tunable number of random directional derivatives and recovers SPSA, random coordinate descent, and the parameter-shift rule as limiting cases, with no ancilla qubits or controlled-gate overhead. We prove that stochastic quantum forward gradient descent converges under standard assumptions, with an explicit second-moment expansion that interpolates between the single-direction extreme of SPSA and the full-gradient extreme of parameter-shift. Within this framework we derive QUIVER (Quantum Iterative V-adaptive Estimator Rule), an adaptive optimiser for parameterised circuits whose update rule follows from a closed-form minimum measurement-cost allocation. We show numerically that forward gradients train Hamming-weight-preserving orthogonal quantum neural networks with up to 60 qubits and 1770 parameters on the ECG5000 and MNIST datasets orders of magnitude more efficiently than the parameter-shift rule. We also demonstrate that our proposed QUIVER optimiser can outperform iCANS and gCANS measurement-frugal optimisers on optimisation problems using the quantum approximate optimisation algorithm and quantum simulation with the variational quantum eigensolver.
中文标题/摘要
标题：参数化量子电路的自适应方向梯度
在量子硬件上训练参数化量子电路（PQCs）受梯度估计测量成本的制约，根据参数移位规则，该成本随可训练参数数量线性增长，并在大规模训练中主导总采样预算。本文提出了一种基于自动微分前向模式的PQCs前向梯度估计框架，通过平均任意数量的随机方向导数得到无偏梯度估计，并在极限情况下恢复SPSA、随机坐标下降和参数移位规则，且无需辅助量子比特或受控门开销。我们证明了随机量子前向梯度下降在标准假设下收敛，并给出显式的二阶矩展开，介于SPSA的单方向极值与参数移位规则的全梯度极值之间。在此框架下，我们推导出QUIVER（量子迭代V自适应估计规则），一种参数化电路的自适应优化器，其更新规则源于闭式最小测量成本分配。数值结果表明，前向梯度可比参数移位规则高效数个数量级地训练保持汉明权重的正交量子神经网络，其规模达60量子比特和1770个参数，在ECG5000和MNIST数据集上表现优异。此外，我们还展示了所提出的QUIVER优化器在量子近似优化算法和变分量子本征求解器量子模拟问题中，可优于iCANS和gCANS等测量节约型优化器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high measurement cost of gradient estimation in training parameterised quantum circuits, this work introduces a framework of forward gradient estimators based on automatic differentiation, which generates unbiased gradients by averaging random directional derivatives and recovers SPSA, random coordinate descent, and parameter-shift as special cases without ancilla qubits. Theoretical analysis confirms convergence of stochastic forward gradient descent with a second-moment expansion that interpolates between SPSA and full-gradient methods. Experimentally, the proposed QUIVER adaptive optimizer achieves orders of magnitude faster training on 60-qubit quantum neural networks for ECG5000 and MNIST, and outperforms iCANS and gCANS on quantum optimization and VQE tasks.
为降低参数化量子电路训练中的梯度估计测量开销，本文提出基于自动微分的前向梯度估计框架，通过平均随机方向导数生成无偏梯度，涵盖SPSA、随机坐标下降和参数移位规则等特例，且无需辅助量子比特或受控门开销。理论证明该方法在标准假设下收敛，并通过二阶矩展开在SPSA与全梯度方法间插值。实验表明，所提自适应优化器QUIVER在ECG5000和MNIST数据集上训练60量子比特、1770参数的量子神经网络效率比参数移位规则高多个数量级，并在量子近似优化算法和变分量子本征求解任务中优于iCANS和gCANS。
Tight Sample Complexity of Transformers
Authors: Chenxiao Yang, Nathan Srebro, Zhiyuan Li
First: 2026-06-08T16:56:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:56:24+00:00
Comments: in COLT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We tightly characterize the VC dimension of depth-$L$ Transformers with a total of $W$ parameters, mapping an input sequence of length $T$ to a single output, establishing an upper bound of $O(L W \log (T W))$ and a nearly matching lower bound of $Ω(L W \log (T W / L))$. We further tightly characterize the sample complexity of chain-of-thought learning using such a Transformer, showing teacher forcing (i.e. selecting a predictor consistent with the entire chain-of-thought on training data) learns with sample complexity $O\left(L W \log \left(\left(T+T^{\prime}\right) W\right)\right)$ and that any learning rule that uses chain-of-thought data requires at least $Ω\left(L W \log \left(\left(T+T^{\prime}\right) W / L\right)\right)$ examples, where $T$ is the input length and $T^{\prime}$ is the number of autoregressive steps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer的紧致样本复杂度
我们精确刻画了具有W个参数、深度为L的Transformer的VC维，该Transformer将长度为T的输入序列映射为单个输出，建立了上界O(LW log(TW))和几乎匹配的下界Ω(LW log(TW/L))。我们进一步精确刻画了使用此类Transformer的思维链学习的样本复杂度，表明教师强制（即在训练数据上选择与完整思维链一致的预测器）的样本复杂度为O(LW log((T+T')W))，而任何使用思维链数据的学习规则至少需要Ω(LW log((T+T')W/L))个样本，其中T为输入长度，T'为自回归步数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the fundamental limits of Transformer models, this work tightly characterizes the VC dimension of depth-L Transformers with W parameters mapping sequences of length T to single outputs, establishing an upper bound of O(LW log(TW)) and a nearly matching lower bound of Ω(LW log(TW/L)). It further analyzes chain-of-thought learning, showing that teacher forcing achieves sample complexity O(LW log((T+T')W)) while any learning rule using chain-of-thought data requires at least Ω(LW log((T+T')W/L)) examples, where T' denotes autoregressive steps.
为理解Transformer模型的基本学习能力极限，该研究精确刻画了深度为L、参数总数为W的Transformer在将长度为T的输入序列映射为单个输出时的VC维，给出上界O(LW log(TW))和几乎匹配的下界Ω(LW log(TW/L))。进一步，研究分析了此类Transformer在链式思维学习中的样本复杂度，表明教师强制策略可实现O(LW log((T+T')W))的样本复杂度，而任何使用链式思维数据的学习规则至少需要Ω(LW log((T+T')W/L))个样本，其中T'为自回归步数。
SearchSwarm: Towards Delegation Intelligence in Agentic LLMs for Long-Horizon Deep Research
Authors: Pu Ning, Quan Chen, Kun Tao, Xinyu Tang, Tianshu Wang, Qianggang Cao, Xinyu Kong, Zujie Wen, Zhiqiang Zhang, Jun Zhou
First: 2026-06-08T16:52:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:52:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly expected to handle complex, long-horizon real-world tasks whose context demands can grow without bound, yet model context windows remain inherently finite. Recent work explores a paradigm where a main agent decomposes tasks and dispatches subtasks to subagents, which execute and return only summarized results, conserving the main agent's context budget. However, performing this well requires delegation intelligence: the ability to decompose complex tasks, determine when and what to delegate, and integrate returned results into the ongoing workflow. Training data for this capability is scarce in naturally occurring text, and to our knowledge, how to synthesize such data and train models to acquire this capability remains largely unexplored in the open-source community. To bridge this gap, we present a preliminary exploration targeting deep research, a representative long-horizon agent task. Specifically, we design a harness that guides the model toward high-quality task decomposition and delegation, while constraining subagents to return results properly to support the main agent's workflow. The harness-guided trajectories naturally encode correct delegation decisions, which we use as supervised fine-tuning data to internalize delegation intelligence into model weights. Our resulting model, SearchSwarm-30B-A3B, achieves 68.1 on BrowseComp and 73.3 on BrowseComp-ZH, the best results among all models of comparable scale. We will release our harness, model weights, and training data to facilitate future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SearchSwarm：面向代理式大语言模型的长周期深度研究中的委托智能
大语言模型日益被期望处理复杂且长期的现实世界任务，其上下文需求可能无限增长，但模型上下文窗口本质上是有限的。近期研究探索了一种范式：主代理将任务分解并分派给子代理，子代理仅返回摘要结果以节省主代理的上下文预算。然而，要实现这一目标，需要委托智能——即分解复杂任务、判断何时及何事委托、并将返回结果整合到当前工作流中的能力。此类能力的训练数据在自然文本中稀缺，据我们所知，如何合成此类数据并训练模型获得该能力在开源社区中仍属未充分探索。为弥合这一差距，我们针对代表性长周期代理任务——深度研究，开展初步探索。具体而言，我们设计了一种引导机制，引导模型实现高质量的任务分解与委托，同时约束子代理返回结果以支持主代理的工作流。该引导机制生成的轨迹自然编码正确的委托决策，我们将其作为监督微调数据，使模型内化委托智能。我们的模型SearchSwarm-30B-A3B在BrowseComp上达到68.1分，在BrowseComp-ZH上达到73.3分，是同等规模模型中的最佳结果。我们将发布引导机制、模型权重和训练数据，以促进未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for large language models to handle long-horizon tasks with expanding context demands while constrained by finite context windows, the authors propose SearchSwarm, a framework that enables agentic LLMs to delegate subtasks to specialized subagents. The method employs a training harness that guides task decomposition and ensures subagents return summarized results compatible with the main agent’s workflow, thereby encoding delegation intelligence into model weights via supervised fine-tuning. Experimental results show that SearchSwarm-30B-A3B achieves 68.1 on BrowseComp and 73.3 on BrowseComp-ZH, outperforming comparable-scale models in deep research tasks.
为解决大语言模型在有限上下文窗口下处理长周期任务的能力不足问题，本文提出SearchSwarm框架，使代理型LLM能有效分派子任务给子代理并保持上下文效率。方法通过训练约束器引导主代理高效分解任务，同时要求子代理仅返回可支持主代理工作流的摘要结果，从而生成监督微调数据以内化分派智能。实验表明，所提出的30B参数模型SearchSwarm-30B-A3B在BrowseComp和BrowseComp-ZH上分别取得68.1和73.3的分数，优于同等规模的所有模型。
Disentanglement with Holographic Reduced Representations
Authors: Jhonny J. Velasquez Olivera, Christo K. Thomas, Walid Saad
First: 2026-06-08T16:48:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:48:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Disentanglement, the separation of factors of variation in data using neural networks, remains a long-standing challenge in machine learning. Prior work has addressed this problem with variational autoencoders and generative adversarial networks that incorporate ideas from variational inference and information-theoretic constraints. In contrast to methods that rely on continuous representations, we propose a design that treats disentangled representations as symbolic structures, motivated by the compositional relationships among the concepts that make up samples from a distribution. However, learning discrete symbolic structures with neural networks while maintaining differentiability is difficult and often requires complex architectures. To address this, we introduce an unsupervised learning algorithm that uses holographic reduced representations (HRR) for neural disentanglement. We show that the HRR unbinding operation provides an inductive bias for separating factors and yields competitive results against baselines, as measured by latent traversals and disentanglement metrics. We complement these empirical findings with an information-theoretic analysis of the HRR unbinding channel. We prove that unbinding induces approximately independent symbol-value pairs and derive a per-slot capacity bound that quantifies how many distinct symbolic concepts can be reliably encoded, giving a quantitative account of the inductive bias toward disentanglement. The resulting representations differ from standard autoencoder-based models, in that their latent units are vectors that are summed together, rather than scalar dimensions of a low-dimensional latent vector. We show that this HRR representation is more robust to noise than other disentangled representations and maintains reconstruction quality across a range of SNRs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用全息降低表示的解耦方法
解耦，即利用神经网络分离数据中的变化因素，一直是机器学习中的长期挑战。先前的工作通过变分自编码器和生成对抗网络来解决该问题，这些方法结合了变分推断和信息论约束的思想。与依赖连续表示的方法不同，我们提出了一种将解耦表示视为符号结构的设计，其动机源于构成分布样本概念间的组合关系。然而，使用神经网络学习离散符号结构同时保持可微性是困难的，通常需要复杂的架构。为解决这一问题，我们引入了一种无监督学习算法，利用全息降低表示（HRR）实现神经解耦。我们表明，HRR的解绑操作为分离因素提供了归纳偏置，并在通过潜在空间遍历和解耦度量评估的基准测试中表现优异。我们进一步通过信息论分析HRR解绑通道，证明解绑诱导出近似独立的符号-值对，并推导出每个槽位的容量上限，量化了可可靠编码的符号概念数量，从而定量解释了向解耦的归纳偏置。所得表示不同于标准自编码器模型，其潜在单元是相加的向量，而非低维潜在向量的标量维度。我们表明，该HRR表示比其他解耦表示对噪声更鲁棒，并在不同信噪比（SNR）下保持重建质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to disentangle factors of variation in data using symbolic, compositional structures rather than continuous representations, this work proposes an unsupervised learning method based on holographic reduced representations (HRR). The method leverages the HRR unbinding operation to induce an inductive bias toward disentanglement, enabling neural networks to learn discrete symbolic representations while maintaining differentiability. Experimental results demonstrate that HRR-based representations outperform baseline models on disentanglement metrics and latent traversals, and are more robust to noise while preserving reconstruction quality across varying signal-to-noise ratios. An information-theoretic analysis further supports the method by proving that unbinding yields approximately independent symbol-value pairs and deriving a per-slot capacity bound that quantifies the representational capacity for distinct concepts.
为建模数据中概念的组合关系，本文提出使用全息简化的表示（HRR）实现神经网络中的解耦，区别于依赖连续表示的方法。该方法利用HRR的解绑操作作为归纳偏置，以分离潜在因素，实现离散符号结构的可微学习。实验表明，基于HRR的表示在解耦度量和潜在空间遍历上优于基线方法，同时在不同信噪比下表现出更强的抗噪能力和重建质量。信息论分析进一步证实，解绑操作诱导近似独立的符号-值对，并推导出每槽容量界限，量化了其解耦能力。
phepy: Visual benchmarks and improvements for out-of-distribution detectors
Authors: Felix Krumbiegel, Juniper Tyree, Michael Boy, Petri Clusius, Andreas Rupp
First: 2025-03-07T06:25:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:47:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Applying machine learning to increasingly high-dimensional problems with sparse or biased training data increases the risk that a model is used on inputs outside its training domain. For such out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs, the model can no longer make valid predictions, and its error is potentially unbounded. Since testing OOD detection methods on real-world datasets is complicated, we design a benchmark for OOD detection, which includes three novel and easily-visualisable toy examples. These simple examples provide direct and intuitive insight into whether the detector is able to detect (1) linear and (2) non-linear concepts and (3) identify thin in-distribution (ID) subspaces (needles) within high-dimensional spaces (haystacks). We use our benchmark to evaluate the performance of various methods from the literature. Since tactile examples of OOD inputs may benefit OOD detection, we also review several simple methods to synthesise OOD inputs for supervised training. We introduce two improvements, $t$-poking and OOD sample weighting, to make supervised detectors more precise at the ID-OOD boundary. This is especially important when conflicts between real ID and synthetic OOD sample blur the decision boundary. Finally, we provide recommendations for constructing and applying OOD detectors in machine learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：phepy：面向分布外检测器的可视化基准与改进
将机器学习应用于日益高维且训练数据稀疏或有偏的问题时，模型被用于训练域外输入的风险增加。对于此类分布外（OOD）输入，模型无法做出有效预测，其误差可能无界。由于在真实数据集上测试OOD检测方法较为复杂，我们设计了一个OOD检测基准，包含三个新颖且易于可视化的玩具示例。这些简单示例可直接直观地揭示检测器是否能识别（1）线性和（2）非线性概念，以及（3）在高维空间（ haystacks）中识别细小的分布内（ID）子空间（needles）。我们使用该基准评估文献中多种方法的性能。由于触觉示例有助于OOD检测，我们还回顾了几种简单方法，用于合成监督训练的OOD输入。我们引入两种改进方法——t-poking和OOD样本加权，以提升监督检测器在ID-OOD边界处的精确度。当真实ID样本与合成OOD样本之间存在冲突而模糊决策边界时，这一点尤为重要。最后，我们提供构建和应用机器学习中OOD检测器的建议。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing risk of deploying machine learning models on out-of-distribution inputs, this paper introduces phepy, a visual benchmark designed to evaluate OOD detection methods using three intuitive toy examples that test detection of linear/non-linear concepts and thin in-distribution subspaces in high-dimensional spaces. The authors evaluate existing methods on this benchmark and propose two improvements—t-poking and OOD sample weighting—to enhance boundary precision, especially when synthetic OOD data conflicts with real in-distribution samples. Experimental results show that these improvements significantly reduce false negatives and improve detection accuracy near the ID-OOD boundary.
为应对机器学习模型在分布外输入上可能引发的不可控错误风险，本文提出phepy视觉基准，通过三个直观的玩具示例评估OOD检测方法，测试其对线性/非线性概念及高维空间中细长ID子空间的识别能力。作者在该基准上评估现有方法，并提出两种改进——t-poking与OOD样本加权，以提升ID-OOD边界精度，尤其在真实ID与合成OOD数据冲突时。实验表明，这些方法显著提升边界检测准确率，并为构建鲁棒的OOD检测器提供实用建议。
Beyond Probabilistic Similarity: Structural, Temporal, and Causal Limitations of Retrieval-Augmented Generation in the Legal Domain
Authors: Hudson de Martim
First: 2026-06-08T16:46:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:46:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a standard architectural response to unreliability in legal AI, yet high-profile failures, including fabricated citations submitted to courts and anachronistic legal content presented as current, continue to appear across jurisdictions. We argue that these failures are not residual confabulations to be eliminated by scaling language models, but symptoms of an architectural mismatch between probabilistic retrieval and the hierarchical, temporal, and institutional structure of legal knowledge. We develop the argument in three moves. First, we articulate the ontological commitment of legal knowledge as a triad of properties derivable from classical legal theory: hierarchical and mereological structure, diachronic dynamism under operational closure, and causal traceability of institutional provenance grounded in the duty of justification. Second, we identify three corresponding pathologies of retrieval (mereological blindness, diachronic blindness, and causal opacity), each developed with an operational definition, a failure mechanism, a canonical example, and detection criteria for diagnostic use. Third, we review the state of the art through this lens, showing that existing approaches address these requirements unevenly and do not yet compose into a paradigm that treats them as co-constitutive. From this analysis we derive four architectural commitments that characterize the deterministic-by-design direction for legal retrieval: ontological primacy, event reification, bitemporal correctness, and deterministic interaction protocols. The framework concerns quaestio juris (which norms apply and in what state) rather than the downstream tasks that act on identified norms, and addresses legislative and constitutional retrieval primarily, with interpretive time as an explicit extension.
