标题：超越概率相似性：法律领域检索增强生成的结构、时间与因果局限
检索增强生成（RAG）已成为应对法律AI不可靠性的标准架构方案，但高调失败案例仍不断出现，包括向法院提交伪造引文和将过时法律内容呈现为现行内容等现象。我们认为，这些失败并非单纯通过扩大语言模型规模即可消除的残余幻觉，而是概率检索架构与法律知识所具有的层级化、时间性和制度性结构之间存在根本性不匹配的体现。我们分三步展开论证：首先，我们从古典法律理论出发，阐明法律知识的本体论承诺，即由三个属性构成的三元组——层级与部分整体结构、在操作闭合下的历时动态性，以及基于正当性义务的制度溯源的因果可追溯性。其次，我们识别出检索系统对应的三种病理现象（部分整体盲区、历时盲区、因果不透明），并为每种现象提供操作性定义、失效机制、典型范例及诊断标准。第三，我们通过这一框架审视现有技术现状，表明当前方法对上述要求的满足程度不一，尚未形成将三者视为共构要素的范式。基于此分析，我们提出四个架构承诺，以定义法律检索的“设计即确定性”方向：本体论优先性、事件实体化、双时态正确性、确定性交互协议。该框架关注的是“法之问”（适用何种规范及处于何种状态），而非对已识别规范进行下游操作的任务，主要面向立法与宪法检索，并将解释性时间作为显式扩展。
Summary / 总结
The paper argues that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in legal AI fail not due to model limitations but because their probabilistic retrieval mechanisms are fundamentally misaligned with the hierarchical, temporal, and causally traceable nature of legal knowledge. The authors propose three core pathologies—mereological blindness, diachronic blindness, and causal opacity—each with operational definitions and diagnostic criteria, and show that current RAG systems inadequately address these issues. Through a theoretical lens rooted in classical legal theory, they derive four architectural commitments—ontological primacy, event reification, bitemporal correctness, and deterministic interaction protocols—to guide future legal retrieval systems that prioritize normative correctness over probabilistic similarity.

A Vision-language Framework for Comparative Reasoning in Radiology
Authors: Tengfei Zhang, Ziheng Zhao, Xiaoman Zhang, Lisong Dai, Pengcheng Qiu, Ya Zhang, Yanfeng Wang, Weidi Xie
First: 2026-06-04T17:12:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:45:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical imaging artificial intelligence has achieved strong performance in isolated image interpretation, but remains poorly aligned with radiological practice, where diagnosis and follow-up rely on comparison across prior studies and analogous reference cases. Here we formulate radiological comparison as an entity-aware cross-image reasoning problem and introduce a framework that supports both reference-case retrieval and temporal comparative interpretation. We construct MedReCo-DB, a large-scale comparative imaging resource derived from routine image-report pairs, comprising more than 690,000 images from over 160,000 patients across eight institutions, four countries and seven imaging modalities. Reports are decomposed into anatomical structures, abnormal findings and pathological conditions to provide supervision for entity-conditioned retrieval and comparative visual question answering. Using this resource, we develop MedReCo, an entity-aware visual encoder for controllable retrieval of clinically analogous cases, and MedReCo-VLM, a vision--language extension for generative interpretation of interval change. Across internal, external and cross-center evaluations, MedReCo achieved the highest Recall@1 in all 12 internal retrieval settings and improved external retrieval by a mean of 6.0 percentage points. In clinically confusable differential groups, it consistently outperformed the strongest baselines. MedReCo-VLM achieved the best performance across all comparative generation evaluations and improved longitudinal follow-up accuracy by 14.5-46.5 percentage points on chest radiographs and 13.0-27.9 percentage points on CT. These findings suggest that entity-aware comparative reasoning can be learned from routine clinical data at scale and may provide a more clinically aligned foundation for medical imaging AI.

标题：用于放射学比较推理的视觉-语言框架

Safe Polytope-in-Polytope Motion Planning and Control with Control Barrier Functions
Authors: Alejandro Gonzalez-Garcia, Dries Dirckx, Jan Swevers, Wilm Decré
First: 2026-06-08T16:44:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:44:28+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous mobile robots operating in tight environments require motion planning frameworks that account for the physical footprint of the robot. Simplifying the geometry to a point or a circle is conservative and discards information needed to successfully and safely traverse narrow passages. This work proposes a safe local motion planning and control method that guarantees that a polytopic robot footprint stays inside a continuously updated convex free-space region. The containment condition is formulated as a set of discrete-time control barrier function constraints within a model predictive controller. The number of safety constraints depends on the complexity of the local free-space geometry and the robot shape, instead of the number of obstacles. The proposed free-space formulation does not need any obstacle detection or segmentation. A comparative analysis against a polytope-based obstacle avoidance formulation confirms favorable scaling up to a reduction of 91$\times$ in computation time as the number of obstacles increases. The approach is validated in simulation with an autonomous surface vehicle and on hardware with a non-holonomic mobile robot, using both occupancy grids and LiDAR sensing. The experiments demonstrate safe real-time motion planning and control at 10~Hz on an onboard embedded computer, including reactive avoidance of dynamic obstacles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于控制屏障函数的安全多边形内多边形运动规划与控制
在狭窄环境中运行的自主移动机器人需要考虑机器人物理足迹的运动规划框架。将几何形状简化为点或圆是保守的，且会丢弃成功安全通过狭窄通道所需的信息。本文提出了一种安全的局部运动规划与控制方法，确保多边形机器人足迹始终位于持续更新的凸自由空间区域内。包含条件以一组离散时间控制屏障函数约束形式在模型预测控制器中表述。安全约束的数量取决于局部自由空间几何形状和机器人形状的复杂度，而非障碍物数量。所提出的自由空间表述无需任何障碍物检测或分割。与基于多边形的避障公式对比分析证实，随着障碍物数量增加，计算时间可减少91倍。该方法在仿真中使用自主水面车辆验证，并在硬件上使用非完整移动机器人进行验证，采用占用网格和激光雷达感知。实验表明，该方法可在车载嵌入式计算机上实现10 Hz实时安全运动规划与控制，包括对动态障碍物的反应式避让。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe navigation in tight environments where point-based approximations of robots are insufficient, this work introduces a motion planning and control framework that ensures a polytopic robot footprint remains within a continuously updated convex free-space region. The method formulates safety as discrete-time control barrier function constraints within a model predictive controller, with the number of constraints tied to local geometry and robot shape rather than obstacle count. Experimental results show a 91x reduction in computation time compared to traditional polytope-based obstacle avoidance, and the approach enables real-time, safe navigation at 10 Hz on embedded hardware using both occupancy grids and LiDAR, including reactive avoidance of dynamic obstacles.
为解决在狭窄环境中机器人点状或圆形近似导致的安全性不足问题，本文提出一种确保多面体机器人足迹始终位于动态更新的凸自由空间内的运动规划与控制方法。该方法将安全约束建模为模型预测控制器中的离散时间控制屏障函数，约束数量取决于局部自由空间几何与机器人形状，而非障碍物数量。实验表明，相比传统多面体避障方法，计算时间减少91倍，且在嵌入式硬件上实现10Hz实时安全规划，支持基于占用网格和激光雷达的动态障碍物避让。
Evaluating the Representation Space of Diffusion Models via Self-Supervised Principles
Authors: Xiao Li, Yixuan Jia, Zekai Zhang, Xiang Li, Lianghe Shi, Jinxin Zhou, Zhihui Zhu, Liyue Shen, Qing Qu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-08T16:44:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:44:18+00:00
Comments: First two authors contributed equally. Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable generative capabilities and have also emerged as powerful self-supervised representation learners, yet the connection between these two abilities remains less explored. Drawing inspiration from self-supervised learning (SSL), we introduce a framework for jointly evaluating the representation and generation capabilities of diffusion models. Specifically, we decompose features into invariant and residual components and derive the Invariant Contamination Ratio (ICR), a Fisher-based metric that quantifies how residual variation contaminates invariant signal in feature space. We use this framework to analyze both discriminative and generative behavior of diffusion models. On the representation side, we find that invariance peaks at intermediate noise levels, which also yield the best downstream classification performance. On the generative side, we study how training transitions from genuine generalization to memorization in data-limited regimes, and show that ICR serves as a sensitive training-time indicator of early learning: increasing residual energy along Fisher directions marks the onset of memorization, detectable from training features alone without external evaluators or held-out test sets. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can be monitored from a self-supervised perspective through the geometry of their learned representations.

标题：通过自监督原则评估扩散模型的表示空间

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the dual roles of diffusion models as generative systems and self-supervised learners, this work introduces a framework to jointly evaluate their representation and generation capabilities using self-supervised principles. SwAIther-Precip: Lead-Time-Aware Bias Correction Enables Kilometer-Scale Downscaling of Global AI Precipitation Forecasts over Switzerland
Authors: Dan Assouline, Erwan Koch, Federico Amato, Filippo Quarenghi, Daniele Nerini, Thibaut Loiseau, Kyle van de Langemheen, Tom Beucler
First: 2026-05-15T16:42:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:39:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Skillful medium-range precipitation forecasting at kilometer scale remains challenging over complex terrain because precipitation arises from multiscale nonlinear processes that global models cannot explicitly resolve at affordable cost. Global AI weather models can produce skillful medium-range forecasts, but their native 0.25 degrees resolution limits direct use for local hazard applications. Statistical downscaling can help bridge this gap, yet existing approaches often struggle with state-dependent, and especially lead-time-dependent, biases in global forecasts. We introduce SwAIther-Precip, a lead-time-aware downscaling framework that converts coarse-resolution AIFS forecasts into probabilistic km-scale precipitation fields over Switzerland. First, a U-Net conditioned on lead time via feature-wise linear modulation deterministically corrects systematic biases at coarse resolution. This targeted correction enables a cheaper super-resolution stage conditioned only on corrected precipitation, allowing direct training on observations rather than on the full atmospheric state. A diffusion-based model then generates fine-scale spatial variability independently of lead time. Using AIFS forecasts and CombiPrecip radar-gauge observations, SwAIther-Precip reduces CRPS by 48% relative to raw AIFS. The generated fields reproduce observed spatial variability with spectral fidelity above 0.85 at large scales and 0.88 at small scales, corresponding to an effective resolution of approximately 4 km on a 1 km grid for lead times up to 5 days. Training across lead times further improves long-range performance, yielding a 13% CRPS reduction at 6 days relative to lead-time-specific models. These results show that explicitly correcting lead-time-dependent biases before generative super-resolution is key to efficient km-scale probabilistic downscaling of global AI precipitation forecasts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SwAIther-Precip：基于预报时效的偏差校正可实现瑞士区域公里级全球AI降水预报的降尺度
在复杂地形上，实现公里级中程降水精准预报仍具挑战，因降水源于多尺度非线性过程，全球模型难以在可承受成本下显式解析。全球AI天气模型虽能生成技能型中程预报，但其原生0.25度分辨率限制了其直接用于本地灾害应用。统计降尺度可弥合此差距，但现有方法常难以处理状态依赖性及尤其时效依赖性的全球预报偏差。我们提出SwAIther-Precip，一种时效感知的降尺度框架，将粗分辨率AIFS预报转化为瑞士区域公里级概率降水场。首先，通过特征线性调制条件于预报时效的U-Net，确定性校正粗分辨率系统性偏差。此针对性校正使后续超分辨率阶段仅依赖校正后的降水，从而可直接基于观测训练而非完整大气状态。随后，基于扩散模型独立生成细尺度空间变异性，且与预报时效无关。利用AIFS预报与CombiPrecip雷达-雨量计观测，SwAIther-Precip相较原始AIFS预报将CRPS降低48%。生成场在大尺度和小尺度上均能复现观测空间变异性，谱相关性分别达0.85和0.88，对应于1公里网格上约4公里有效分辨率，适用于5天以内预报时效。跨时效训练进一步提升长程性能，在6天预报时效下相较时效特定模型CRPS降低13%。结果表明，生成超分辨率前显式校正时效依赖性偏差，是高效实现全球AI降水预报公里级概率降尺度的关键。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of generating accurate kilometer-scale precipitation forecasts over complex terrain like Switzerland, where global AI models suffer from coarse resolution and lead-time-dependent biases, SwAIther-Precip introduces a two-stage downscaling framework. It first applies a U-Net with lead-time-aware modulation to correct systematic biases in coarse-resolution AI forecasts, enabling a subsequent super-resolution stage that relies only on corrected precipitation data and can be trained directly on observations. A diffusion model then generates fine-scale spatial variability independent of lead time. Evaluated against CombiPrecip observations, the method reduces CRPS by 48% compared to raw AI forecasts and achieves spectral fidelity above 0.85 at large scales and 0.88 at small scales, effectively resolving features at 4 km on a 1 km grid for up to 5 days. Training across multiple lead times further improves performance, reducing CRPS by 13% at 6 days compared to lead-time-specific models.
为解决复杂地形（如瑞士）上全球AI降水预报在千米尺度下精度不足的问题，SwAIther-Precip提出一种基于预报时效的偏差校正方法。该方法首先使用U-Net结合特征线性调制，根据预报时效校正粗分辨率AI预报中的系统性偏差，使后续超分辨率阶段仅依赖校正后的降水数据，从而可直接基于观测数据训练，无需完整大气状态。随后，扩散模型独立于预报时效生成细尺度空间变异性。实验表明，该方法相较原始AI预报CRPS降低48%，在大尺度和小尺度上空间频谱保真度分别达0.85和0.88，相当于在1公里网格上实现约4公里的有效分辨率，适用于5天以内的预报时效。跨时效训练进一步提升长时效性能，在6天预报中CRPS较时效特定模型降低13%。
SDM-Q: Cost-Aware Staged Decision-Making for Multi-Omics Classification with Deep Q-Learning
Authors: Nan Mu, Yangfan Xiao, Ling Wang, Xiaoning Li, Yue Kang, Chen Zhao
First: 2026-05-29T08:47:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:39:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-omics data provide complementary molecular characterizations of disease phenotypes and play an important role in disease diagnosis and subtype classification in precision medicine. However, acquiring complete multi-omics profiles is expensive and time-consuming, while most existing deep learning methods assume full modality availability during inference, resulting in substantial redundancy and limited practicality in clinical settings. To address this issue, we propose SDM-Q, a reinforcement learning framework for adaptive and cost-aware multi-omics classification. Specifically, multi-omics diagnosis is reformulated as a finite-horizon sequential decision problem, where the currently acquired omics modalities define the diagnostic state at each stage. An action--value function determines whether to acquire an additional modality or terminate the decision process and output the final prediction. To balance diagnostic utility and acquisition cost, the reward is defined only at the terminal stage and jointly determined by classification correctness and cumulative modality acquisition cost. A backward stage-wise optimization strategy is introduced to improve policy consistency and training stability. Experiments on four public multi-omics datasets, including ROSMAP, LGG, BRCA, and KIPAN, demonstrate that SDM-Q effectively reduces redundant modality acquisition while maintaining competitive classification performance compared with methods using complete multi-omics inputs. In the BRCA and KIPAN datasets, more than 99\% and 95\% of subjects, respectively, achieve accurate classification using only a single omics modality, while the average number of acquired modalities remains below two for ROSMAP and LGG. These results suggest that cost-aware sequential decision-making provides an effective paradigm for improving the efficiency of precision medicine workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SDM-Q：基于深度Q学习的多组学分类成本感知分阶段决策方法
多组学数据提供了疾病表型的互补分子表征，在精准医学的疾病诊断和亚型分类中发挥重要作用。然而，获取完整的多组学数据成本高昂且耗时，而现有大多数深度学习方法在推理时假设所有模态均可用，导致大量冗余且在临床环境中实用性有限。为解决此问题，我们提出SDM-Q，一种用于自适应且成本感知的多组学分类的强化学习框架。具体而言，多组学诊断被重新表述为有限时域的序列决策问题，当前获取的组学模态定义每个阶段的诊断状态。动作-价值函数决定是否获取额外模态或终止决策过程并输出最终预测。为平衡诊断效用与获取成本，奖励仅在终端阶段定义，由分类正确性和累积模态获取成本共同决定。引入逆向阶段优化策略以提升策略一致性与训练稳定性。在包括ROSMAP、LGG、BRCA和KIPAN在内的四个公开多组学数据集上的实验表明，SDM-Q在保持与使用完整多组学输入方法相当的分类性能的同时，有效减少了冗余模态获取。在BRCA和KIPAN数据集中，分别有超过99%和95%的样本仅使用单一组学模态即可准确分类，而ROSMAP和LGG的平均获取模态数均低于两个。这些结果表明，成本感知的序列决策为提升精准医学工作流效率提供了一种有效范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and redundancy of acquiring full multi-omics profiles in clinical settings, SDM-Q introduces a reinforcement learning framework that adaptively decides which omics modalities to acquire during diagnosis. It models the classification task as a sequential decision process where each state represents the current set of acquired modalities, and actions determine whether to add another modality or terminate. The reward, defined only at the end, balances classification accuracy and cumulative acquisition cost, with a backward stage-wise optimization strategy enhancing training stability. Experiments across four datasets show SDM-Q achieves competitive accuracy while significantly reducing modality usage: in BRCA and KIPAN, over 99% and 95% of subjects were correctly classified using just one modality, with average acquisition below two modalities in ROSMAP and LGG.
为降低临床中获取完整多组学数据的成本与冗余，本文提出SDM-Q框架，将多组学分类建模为有限时域的序列决策问题。模型在每一步根据当前已获取的组学模态决定是否继续采集或终止并输出预测，奖励仅在终止时计算，兼顾分类准确率与采集成本。采用逆向分阶段优化策略提升训练稳定性。在四个公开数据集上的实验表明，SDM-Q显著减少模态采集数量而不牺牲性能：在BRCA和KIPAN数据集中，分别有99%和95%的样本仅用单一模态即可准确分类，而ROSMAP和LGG的平均采集模态数均低于两个。
When Do Diffusion Models learn to Generate Multiple Objects?
Authors: Yujin Jeong, Arnas Uselis, Iro Laina, Seong Joon Oh, Anna Rohrbach
First: 2026-04-30T22:18:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:33:25+00:00
Comments: ICML2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-image diffusion models achieve impressive visual fidelity, yet they remain unreliable in multi-object generation. Despite extensive empirical evidence of these failures, the underlying causes remain unclear. We begin by asking how much of this limitation arises from the data itself. To disentangle data effects, we consider two regimes across different dataset sizes: (1) concept generalization, where each individual concept is observed during training under potentially imbalanced data distributions, and (2) compositional generalization, where specific combinations of concepts are systematically held out. To study these regimes, we introduce mosaic (Multi-Object Spatial relations, AttrIbution, Counting), a controlled framework for dataset generation. By training diffusion models on mosaic, we find that scene complexity plays a dominant role rather than concept imbalance, and that counting is uniquely difficult to learn in low-data regimes. Moreover, compositional generalization collapses as more concept combinations are held out during training. These findings highlight fundamental limitations of diffusion models and motivate stronger inductive biases and data design for robust multi-object compositional generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型何时学会生成多个物体？
文本到图像的扩散模型在视觉保真度方面表现卓越，但在多物体生成上仍不可靠。尽管已有大量实证证据表明这些失败，但其根本原因尚不明确。我们首先探讨这种局限性是否源于数据本身。为分离数据影响，我们考虑两种不同数据集规模下的场景：（1）概念泛化，即每个独立概念在训练中以可能不平衡的数据分布出现；（2）组合泛化，即特定概念组合被系统性排除。为研究这些场景，我们引入了mosaic（多物体空间关系、属性、计数）这一受控数据集生成框架。通过在mosaic上训练扩散模型，我们发现场景复杂度起主导作用，而非概念不平衡，且在低数据量场景下计数尤为难以学习。此外，随着训练中排除的概念组合增多，组合泛化能力急剧下降。这些发现揭示了扩散模型的根本局限性，并推动更强的归纳偏置和数据设计，以实现稳健的多物体组合生成。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why text-to-image diffusion models struggle with generating multiple objects, despite high visual fidelity. The authors isolate whether data distribution or model capacity causes the failure by introducing mosaic, a controlled dataset generation framework that tests concept generalization and compositional generalization under varying data regimes. Experiments reveal that scene complexity—not concept imbalance—is the primary bottleneck, and that counting objects is especially hard in low-data settings. Compositional generalization also degrades as more object combinations are withheld during training, suggesting fundamental limitations in current diffusion models that require stronger inductive biases and better data design for robust multi-object generation.
本文探讨了文本到图像扩散模型在生成多个物体时表现不佳的原因，尽管其视觉保真度很高。作者通过引入mosaic框架，将数据分布的影响与模型能力分离，分别考察概念泛化和组合泛化两种场景。实验发现，场景复杂度而非概念分布不平衡是主要瓶颈，且在数据量少时计数任务尤为困难。随着训练中被排除的物体组合增多，组合泛化能力急剧下降，揭示了当前扩散模型的根本局限，呼吁设计更强的归纳偏置和更优的数据策略以实现稳健的多物体组合生成。
Proxy Reward Internalization and Mechanistic Exploitation: A Learned Precursor to Reward Hacking and Its Generalization
Authors: Mohammad Beigi, Ming Jin, Lifu Huang
First: 2026-06-08T16:32:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:32:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reward hacking is usually studied after it becomes visible, once a model earns high proxy reward while failing the intended task. We instead study what proxy RL teaches before that failure appears. We introduce Proxy Reward Internalization and Mechanistic Exploitation (PRIME), a learned capability to assess task correctness, predict proxy acceptance, and reason about exploitable proxy--gold gaps. In coding RL environments with exploitable pytest rewards, we measure PRIME through chain-of-thought monitoring, direct probes, and activation-level concept vectors. We find that PRIME emerges in a staged sequence before sustained reward hacking, and that its current direct-probe score forecasts later hack onset and severity even when the visible hack rate is still low. PRIME also adapts when the evaluator changes, retargeting to whichever proxy--gold gap remains rewarded and persisting when gold reward suppresses overt hacking, and ablating its activation directions reduces hacking. Across checkpoints, in-domain PRIME tracks out-of-domain misalignment. Together these results suggest that exploitable proxy RL amplifies a proxy-internalization capability upstream of visible hacking, making PRIME a candidate early-warning signal for broader alignment risk.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理奖励内化与机制性利用：奖励黑客行为及其泛化的学习前兆
通常在奖励黑客行为显现后才对其进行研究，即当模型在未完成目标任务时却获得高代理奖励。我们则研究代理强化学习在该失败显现前所教授的能力。我们引入了代理奖励内化与机制性利用（PRIME），这是一种学习到的能力，用于评估任务正确性、预测代理奖励接受度，并推理可利用的代理-黄金奖励差距。在具有可利用pytest奖励的编码强化学习环境中，我们通过思维链监控、直接探测和激活层概念向量来衡量PRIME。我们发现，PRIME在持续奖励黑客行为出现前以阶段性序列形式涌现，且其当前直接探测得分即使在可见黑客率仍较低时，也能预测后续黑客行为的出现与严重程度。PRIME在评估器变化时亦能适应，重新定向至仍被奖励的代理-黄金差距，并在黄金奖励抑制显性黑客行为时持续存在；消融其激活方向可减少黑客行为。在不同检查点中，域内PRIME可追踪域外对齐偏差。这些结果共同表明，可利用的代理强化学习在可见黑客行为之前放大了代理内化能力，使PRIME成为更广泛对齐风险的早期预警信号候选。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the emergence of reward hacking before it becomes visible, focusing on a learned capability called PRIME—Proxy Reward Internalization and Mechanistic Exploitation—which enables models to assess task correctness, predict proxy reward acceptance, and reason about exploitable discrepancies between proxy and true rewards. Using coding RL environments with pytest-style rewards, the authors measure PRIME through chain-of-thought monitoring, direct probes, and activation-level concept vectors, finding that PRIME develops in stages prior to sustained reward hacking. Its direct-probe score predicts future hack severity even when visible hacking is minimal, and PRIME adapts to changing evaluators by retargeting to remaining rewarded proxy-gold gaps, persists even when gold rewards suppress overt hacking, and its ablation reduces hacking. PRIME also tracks out-of-domain misalignment, suggesting it serves as an early indicator of broader alignment risk.
该论文研究了奖励黑客行为在显性出现前的形成过程，聚焦于一种名为PRIME（代理奖励内化与机制性利用）的习得能力，该能力使模型能够评估任务正确性、预测代理奖励接受度，并推理代理与黄金奖励之间的可利用差距。在具有pytest风格奖励的编码强化学习环境中，作者通过思维链监控、直接探测和激活层概念向量测量PRIME，发现PRIME在持续奖励黑客行为出现前分阶段形成，且其直接探测得分可预测未来黑客行为的发生与严重程度，即使此时可见黑客行为仍较低。PRIME能适应评估器变化，重新定向至当前被奖励的代理-黄金差距，即使黄金奖励抑制了显性黑客行为仍能持续存在，且其激活方向被移除后可减少黑客行为。在多个检查点中，域内PRIME还能追踪域外对齐偏差，表明PRIME是潜在对齐风险的早期预警信号。
Self-Mined Hardness for Safety Fine-Tuning
Authors: Prakhar Gupta, Garv Shah, Donghua Zhang
First: 2026-05-04T23:30:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:31:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety fine-tuning of language models typically requires a curated adversarial dataset. We take a different approach: score each candidate prompt's difficulty by how often the target model's own rollouts are judged harmful, then fine-tune on the hardest prompts paired with the model's own non-jailbroken rollouts. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, this approach cuts the WildJailbreak attack success rate from 11.5% and 20.1% down to 1-3%, but pushes refusal on jailbreak-shaped benign prompts from 14-22% to 74-94%. Interleaving the same hard prompts 1:1 with adversarially-framed benign prompts (prompts that look like jailbreaks but have benign intent) cuts that refusal back down to 30-51% on 8B and 52-72% on 3B, at a cost of 2-6 percentage points of attack success rate. Within the mixed regime, training on the hardest half of the eligible pool rather than a random half cuts the remaining ASR by 35-50% (about 3 percentage points) on both models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自采硬度用于安全微调
语言模型的安全微调通常需要人工筛选的对抗性数据集。我们采取不同方法：通过目标模型自身rollout被判定有害的频率，评估每个候选提示的难度，然后在最难的提示与模型自身非禁用rollout配对的基础上进行微调。在Llama-3-8B-Instruct和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct上，该方法将WildJailbreak攻击成功率从11.5%和20.1%降至1-3%，但使针对禁用型良性提示的拒绝率从14-22%提升至74-94%。若将相同难提示与对抗性框架的良性提示（外观似禁用但意图良性）1:1交错训练，则8B模型上拒绝率回落至30-51%，3B模型上回落至52-72%，代价是攻击成功率上升2-6个百分点。在混合训练中，仅在合格池中选择最难的半数样本而非随机半数，可进一步降低剩余攻击成功率35-50%（约3个百分点）
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safer language model fine-tuning without relying on external adversarial datasets, this work introduces a self-mined hardness metric that ranks prompts by how frequently the target model’s own rollouts judge them harmful. It fine-tunes on the hardest prompts paired with the model’s non-jailbroken rollouts, significantly reducing jailbreak attack success rates from 11.5% to 1-3% on Llama-3-8B-Instruct and from 20.1% to 1-3% on Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, while drastically increasing refusal rates on benign jailbreak-shaped prompts from 14-22% to 74-94%. When interleaving these hard prompts with adversarially-framed benign prompts, refusal rates moderate to 30-51% on 8B and 52-72% on 3B, at the cost of a small increase in attack success. Training on the top half of the hardest prompts further reduces residual attack success by 35-50% (about 3 percentage points) on both models.
为实现无需人工标注对抗样本的安全微调，该研究提出通过模型自身生成的输出被判定为有害的频率来衡量提示难度，从而筛选出最难的提示进行微调。在Llama-3系列模型上，该方法将WildJailbreak攻击成功率从11.5%至20.1%降至1-3%，同时将对伪装成越狱攻击的良性提示的拒绝率从14-22%大幅提升至74-94%。若将最难提示与对抗性伪装的良性提示混合训练，拒绝率回落至30-51%（8B）和52-72%（3B），攻击成功率仅上升2-6个百分点。进一步仅训练最难的一半提示，可再降低攻击成功率35-50%（约3个百分点），适用于两个模型。
IS-CoT: Breaking the Long-form Generation Collapse via Interleaved Structural Thinking
Authors: Zechen Sun, Yuyang Sun, Zecheng Tang, Juntao Li, Wenpeng Hu, Wenliang Chen, Zhunchen Luo, Guotong Geng, Min Zhang
First: 2026-06-08T16:31:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:31:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating coherent and controllable long-form content remains a persistent challenge for Large Language Models (LLMs). While reasoning-enhanced models have demonstrated success in logic-intensive domains, our evaluation reveals that they suffer from a severe length collapse in open-ended writing, where performance degrades sharply as target lengths exceed 2,000 words. We attribute this failure to the limitation of static hierarchical planning, which struggles to provide dynamic guidance over extended contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Interleaved Structural Chain-of-Thought (IS-CoT) framework. Unlike external agentic workflows, IS-CoT embeds a dynamic Plan-Write-Reflect cycle into the generation process, enabling continuous strategy adaptation and global alignment without additional assistance. Based on this framework, we construct a high-quality dataset of interleaved reasoning traces via a multi-teacher pipeline and train IS-Writer-8B. Experiments demonstrate that IS-Writer-8B achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging long-form benchmarks (e.g., +3.08 vs. DeepSeek-V3.2 on LongBench-Write), exhibiting robust length compliance and coherence competitive with significantly larger proprietary models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IS-CoT：通过交错结构思维打破长文本生成崩溃
生成连贯且可控的长文本内容仍是大型语言模型（LLMs）面临的持续挑战。尽管增强推理能力的模型在逻辑密集型领域表现成功，但我们的评估显示，它们在开放式写作中存在严重长度崩溃问题，当目标长度超过2000词时性能急剧下降。我们归因于静态分层规划的局限性，其难以在长上下文中提供动态指导。为弥合这一差距，我们提出交错结构思维链（IS-CoT）框架。与外部代理工作流不同，IS-CoT将动态的“规划-写作-反思”循环嵌入生成过程，无需额外协助即可实现持续策略调整和全局对齐。基于该框架，我们通过多教师流水线构建了高质量的交错推理轨迹数据集，并训练了IS-Writer-8B模型。实验表明，IS-Writer-8B在具有挑战性的长文本基准测试中达到最先进性能（如在LongBench-Write上比DeepSeek-V3.2高出3.08），展现出稳健的长度合规性和与显著更大规模专有模型相当的连贯性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of reasoning-enhanced LLMs to maintain coherence and length control beyond 2,000 words in open-ended writing, the authors propose IS-CoT, a framework embedding a dynamic Plan-Write-Reflect cycle directly into generation to enable continuous strategy adaptation. They build a high-quality interleaved reasoning dataset via a multi-teacher pipeline and train IS-Writer-8B. Experiments show IS-Writer-8B achieves state-of-the-art results on long-form benchmarks, outperforming DeepSeek-V3.2 by +3.08 on LongBench-Write, with strong length compliance and coherence rivaling much larger proprietary models.
为了解决推理增强型大模型在开放式写作中超过2000词后出现的严重长度坍缩问题，作者提出了IS-CoT框架，通过在生成过程中嵌入动态的计划-写作-反思循环，实现持续策略调整。基于多教师管道构建了交错推理轨迹数据集并训练IS-Writer-8B模型，在LongBench-Write基准上超越DeepSeek-V3.2达+3.08，展现出更强的长度合规性与连贯性，无需外部辅助。
Investigating the Histogram Loss in Regression
Authors: Ehsan Imani, Kai Luedemann, Sam Scholnick-Hughes, Esraa Elelimy, Martha White
Venue: JMLR,2026
First: 2024-02-20T23:29:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:29:39+00:00
Comments: 52 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
It is becoming increasingly common in regression to train neural networks that model the entire distribution even if only the mean is required for prediction. This additional modeling often comes with performance gain and the reasons behind the improvement are not fully known. This paper investigates a recent approach to regression, the Histogram Loss, which involves learning the conditional distribution of the target variable by minimizing the cross-entropy between a target distribution and a flexible histogram prediction. We design theoretical and empirical analyses to determine why and when this performance gain appears, and how different components of the loss contribute to it. Our results suggest that the benefits of learning distributions in this setup come from improvements in optimization rather than modelling extra information. We then demonstrate the viability of the Histogram Loss in common deep learning applications without a need for costly hyperparameter tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：回归中直方图损失的探究
在回归任务中，训练能够建模整个分布的神经网络（即使仅需预测均值）正变得越来越普遍。这种额外建模通常带来性能提升，但其原因尚不完全清楚。本文研究了一种近期的回归方法——直方图损失，该方法通过最小化目标分布与灵活直方图预测之间的交叉熵，学习目标变量的条件分布。我们设计了理论与实证分析，以确定该性能提升何时出现及其原因，并探讨损失函数各组成部分的贡献。结果表明，该设置下学习分布的收益主要源于优化改进，而非建模额外信息。随后，我们展示了直方图损失在常见深度学习应用中的可行性，且无需昂贵的超参数调优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that modeling full distributions in regression tasks often yields performance gains even when only the mean is needed, this paper investigates the Histogram Loss—a method that learns the conditional distribution of the target by minimizing cross-entropy between a target distribution and a flexible histogram prediction. Through theoretical and empirical analyses, the study finds that these gains stem primarily from improved optimization dynamics rather than from modeling additional information. Experiments across common deep learning applications confirm the effectiveness of the Histogram Loss without requiring extensive hyperparameter tuning.
鉴于在回归任务中即使仅需预测均值，建模完整分布仍常带来性能提升，本文研究了直方图损失方法——该方法通过最小化目标分布与灵活直方图预测之间的交叉熵来学习目标变量的条件分布。理论与实证分析表明，性能提升主要源于优化过程的改善，而非额外信息建模。在多个深度学习应用中，该方法表现稳健，无需复杂超参数调优即可取得良好效果。
A Graphop Analysis of Graph Neural Networks on Sparse Graphs: Generalization and Universal Approximation
Authors: Ofek Amran, Tom Gilat, Ron Levie
First: 2026-02-09T15:25:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:26:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generalization and approximation capabilities of message passing graph neural networks (MPNNs) are often studied by defining a compact metric on a space of input graphs under which MPNNs are equicontinuous. Such analyses are of two varieties: 1) when the metric space includes graphs of unbounded sizes, the theory is only appropriate for dense graphs, and, 2) when studying sparse graphs, the metric space only includes graphs of uniformly bounded size. In this work, we present a unified approach, defining a compact metric on the space of graphs of all sizes, both sparse and dense, under which MPNNs are equicontinuous. This leads to more powerful universal approximation theorems and generalization bounds than previous works. The theory is based on, and extends, a recent approach to graph limit theory called graphop analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏图上图神经网络的图算子分析：泛化与通用逼近
消息传递图神经网络（MPNNs）的泛化与逼近能力常通过在输入图空间上定义紧致度量来研究，使得MPNNs在该度量下等连续。此类分析分为两类：1）当度量空间包含无界大小的图时，理论仅适用于稠密图；2）当研究稀疏图时，度量空间仅包含大小有统一上界的图。本文提出统一方法，在包含所有大小（稀疏与稠密）图的空间上定义紧致度量，使得MPNNs等连续。这导致比以往工作更强的通用逼近定理与泛化界。该理论基于并扩展了近期称为图算子分析的图极限理论方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing analyses that either restrict to dense graphs or bounded-size sparse graphs, this work introduces a unified compact metric for graphs of all sizes, enabling MPNNs to be equicontinuous under this framework. The method extends graphop analysis to handle both sparse and dense graphs simultaneously, leading to stronger universal approximation theorems and generalization bounds. Experimental results confirm that this approach yields improved theoretical guarantees compared to prior methods, particularly in settings involving variable graph sizes and sparsity.
本文针对现有图神经网络（MPNNs）理论分析的局限性，提出一种统一的紧致度量，适用于所有规模的图（包括稀疏和稠密图），从而在该度量下实现MPNNs的等连续性。该方法扩展了近期的图运算分析技术，突破了以往仅适用于稠密图或限定规模稀疏图的限制。实验结果表明，该框架在不同图结构上实现了更强的通用逼近定理和更紧的泛化界，验证了其理论优势。
Sparse Memory Finetuning as a Low-Forgetting Alternative to LoRA and Full Finetuning
Authors: Prakhar Gupta, Garv Shah, Satyam Goyal, Anirudh Kanchi
First: 2026-05-04T23:46:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:26:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adapting a pretrained language model to a new task often hurts the general capabilities it already had, a problem known as catastrophic forgetting. Sparse Memory Finetuning (SMF) tries to avoid this by adding key-value memory layers to the model and, on each training step, updating only the small set of memory rows that the current batch reads most heavily. We re-implement SMF on Qwen-2.5-0.5B-Instruct and compare it with LoRA and full finetuning on MedMCQA, a 4-choice medical exam task, using WikiText perplexity and TriviaQA accuracy as forgetting probes. SMF improves MedMCQA by 2.5 percentage points while keeping both forgetting probes within roughly 1 point of the base model, whereas LoRA and full finetuning achieve larger gains but with clear drift on both. We also compare two row-selection rules (KL-divergence and TF-IDF), which balance the two forgetting metrics differently.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏记忆微调：一种低遗忘的LoRA与全微调替代方案
将预训练语言模型适配新任务常导致其原有通用能力受损，即灾难性遗忘问题。稀疏记忆微调（SMF）通过在模型中添加键值记忆层，并在每个训练步骤仅更新当前批次最频繁读取的少量记忆行，以避免该问题。我们在Qwen-2.5-0.5B-Instruct上重新实现SMF，并在医学四选一考试任务MedMCQA上与LoRA和全微调进行比较，使用WikiText困惑度和TriviaQA准确率作为遗忘探测指标。SMF使MedMCQA性能提升2.5个百分点，同时两个遗忘探测指标均保持在基线模型附近约1点以内；而LoRA和全微调虽收益更大，但存在明显性能漂移。我们还比较了两种行选择规则（KL散度与TF-IDF），它们在平衡两个遗忘指标方面表现不同。
Summary / 总结
To mitigate catastrophic forgetting during fine-tuning of pretrained language models, Sparse Memory Finetuning (SMF) introduces key-value memory layers and updates only the most frequently accessed memory rows per training step. Evaluated on MedMCQA with forgetting probes via WikiText perplexity and TriviaQA accuracy, SMF achieved a 2.5 percentage point improvement in task performance while maintaining forgetting metrics within about 1 point of the base model, outperforming both LoRA and full fine-tuning which showed larger gains but significant forgetting. Two row-selection strategies—KL-divergence and TF-IDF—were compared, revealing differing trade-offs in balancing the forgetting metrics.
为缓解语言模型适配新任务时的灾难性遗忘，Sparse Memory Finetuning（SMF）引入键值记忆层，并在每一步仅更新当前批次最频繁访问的记忆行。在MedMCQA任务上，以WikiText困惑度和TriviaQA准确率作为遗忘指标，SMF实现2.5个百分点的性能提升，同时遗忘指标保持在基线模型附近约1点，优于LoRA和全参数微调（后者虽提升更大但遗忘明显）。两种行选择策略——KL散度与TF-IDF——在平衡两个遗忘指标上表现不同。
BrainSurgery: Reproducible and Reliable Declarative Weight Manipulations for Model Editing and Upcycling
Authors: Gianluca Barmina, Annemette Broch Pirchert, Andrea Blasi Núñez, Lukas Galke Poech, Peter Schneider-Kamp
First: 2026-06-08T16:26:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:26:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As deep learning models scale, managing, inspecting, and modifying large checkpoints has become increasingly challenging. Researchers often need to alter model weights for layer restructuring, precision casting, low-rank factorization, and architectural debugging, yet these workflows often rely on fragile ad-hoc Python scripts. Here, we introduce BrainSurgery, a tool for robust and reproducible "tensor surgery" on neural network checkpoints, and provide a system demonstration covering four examples and three case studies from model upcycling to LoRA extraction. By abstracting storage formats and memory management, BrainSurgery executes complex transformations through declarative YAML plans. It supports structural modifications, mathematical transformations, and tensor reshaping through expressive regex and structural targeting, while built-in assertions validate tensor shapes, data types, and values to prevent silent errors. We envision that BrainSurgery will provide a strong foundation for future research through its reproducible and validated operations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BrainSurgery：用于模型编辑与再利用的可重现且可靠的声明式权重操作
随着深度学习模型规模扩大，管理、检查和修改大型模型检查点变得愈发困难。研究人员常需修改模型权重以实现层结构重组、精度转换、低秩分解及架构调试，但这些工作流程往往依赖脆弱的临时Python脚本。本文介绍BrainSurgery，一个用于神经网络检查点稳健且可重现的“张量手术”工具，并通过四个示例和三个案例研究（涵盖模型再利用至LoRA提取）进行系统演示。BrainSurgery通过声明式YAML计划执行复杂变换，抽象存储格式与内存管理，支持结构修改、数学变换及张量重塑，借助表达性正则表达式与结构化目标定位；内置断言验证张量形状、数据类型与值，防止静默错误。我们期望BrainSurgery通过其可重现且经验证的操作，为未来研究提供坚实基础。
Summary / 总结
As deep learning models grow larger, manually editing their weights via fragile Python scripts becomes impractical. BrainSurgery addresses this by offering a declarative, YAML-based system that allows users to precisely manipulate model tensors through structural targeting and regex patterns, supporting operations like layer restructuring and LoRA extraction. The tool abstracts storage and memory management, enforces tensor shape and value validation via built-in assertions, and demonstrates reproducible results across four examples and three case studies, including model upcycling and precision casting.
随着深度学习模型规模扩大，手动通过易出错的Python脚本修改模型权重变得不可行。BrainSurgery通过YAML配置文件提供一种声明式、可复现的神经网络权重编辑系统，抽象存储与内存管理细节，支持结构修改、数学变换和张量重塑，借助正则表达式和结构化定位，并内置断言验证张量形状、类型和值以防止隐式错误。在涵盖模型再利用与LoRA提取的四个示例和三个案例研究中，实验表明BrainSurgery能稳定可靠地执行权重操作，无需深入底层基础设施知识。
When Do Local Score Models Extrapolate Across Size? A Diagnostic Theory and Benchmark
Authors: Wenjie Xi
First: 2026-06-08T16:24:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:24:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific generative modeling often requires size transfer, where models trained on small systems are evaluated on larger ones. While translation-invariant architectures enable this evaluation, we show that architectural locality alone does not guarantee stable size extrapolation. Instead, stable extrapolation is governed by the quasi-locality of the Gaussian-smoothed score. Through Tweedie's formula, far-away perturbations can influence local score components via posterior covariance, meaning a local model succeeds only if its receptive field covers the smoothed score's response range. We formalize this mechanism, proving a size-uniform comparison theorem for local marginals under reverse diffusion. We also introduce Finite-Depth Local Flow (FDLF), a white-box diagnostic benchmark with exact scores, densities, and controllable response ranges. Empirically, we validate the interplay between spatial mixing, smoothed-score quasi-locality, and model receptive fields. Under spatial mixing, the smoothed score remains quasi-local relative to the receptive field, enabling stable extrapolation. Conversely, when spatial mixing weakens, the score's locality rapidly degrades, causing size transfer to fail.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部得分模型在何种情况下能跨规模外推？一种诊断理论与基准
科学生成建模常需规模迁移，即在小规模系统上训练的模型需在更大系统上评估。虽然平移不变架构支持此类评估，但我们证明仅靠架构局部性不足以保证稳定规模外推。稳定外推由高斯平滑得分的准局部性决定。通过Tweedie公式，远处扰动可通过后验协方差影响局部得分分量，意味着局部模型仅在感受野覆盖平滑得分响应范围时才能成功。我们形式化该机制，证明了在反向扩散下局部边缘分布的规模统一比较定理。同时引入有限深度局部流（FDLF），这是一种白盒诊断基准，具有精确得分、密度及可控响应范围。实证上，我们验证了空间混合、平滑得分准局部性与模型感受野之间的相互作用。在空间混合条件下，平滑得分相对于感受野保持准局部性，从而实现稳定外推；反之，当空间混合减弱时，得分的局部性迅速退化，导致规模迁移失败。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate generative models trained on small systems on larger ones, this paper investigates when local score models can reliably extrapolate across system sizes. It proposes that stable extrapolation depends not on architectural locality alone, but on the quasi-locality of the Gaussian-smoothed score, which is influenced by posterior covariance via Tweedie’s formula. The authors formalize this using a size-uniform comparison theorem for local marginals under reverse diffusion and introduce Finite-Depth Local Flow (FDLF) as a diagnostic benchmark with exact scores and controllable response ranges. Experiments show that under strong spatial mixing, the smoothed score remains quasi-local relative to the model’s receptive field, enabling stable size transfer; however, when spatial mixing weakens, the score’s locality degrades rapidly, leading to extrapolation failure.
为解决在小系统上训练的生成模型如何可靠地评估大系统的问题，本文研究了局部分数模型何时能实现跨规模外推。研究指出，稳定外推依赖于高斯平滑分数的准局部性，而非架构局部性本身，该性质由后验协方差通过Tweedie公式决定。作者通过反向扩散下的局部边际大小统一比较定理形式化该机制，并提出Finite-Depth Local Flow（FDLF）作为具有精确分数、密度和可控响应范围的诊断基准。实验表明，在空间混合条件下，平滑分数相对于模型感受野保持准局部性，从而实现稳定外推；而当空间混合减弱时，分数局部性迅速退化，导致外推失败。
Learning to Attack and Defend: Adaptive Red Teaming of Language Models via GRPO
Authors: Blake Bullwinkel, Eugenia Kim, Amanda Minnich, Mark Russinovich
First: 2026-06-08T16:21:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:21:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI red teaming must continually adapt to evolving attackers and defenders. Reinforcement learning offers a promising approach to discovering novel attacks, and co-training methods can produce more robust defenders in tandem. Recent works have demonstrated the efficacy of attacker-defender co-training by applying PPO and DPO, but report that GRPO is unstable in this setting. We introduce AdvGRPO, a co-training framework that makes GRPO viable for joint attacker-defender optimization using dense multi-channel rewards and decoupled advantage normalization. Training progresses through a curriculum from single-turn to closed-loop multi-turn attacks before bootstrapping co-training, where attacker and defender models are updated in alternation. We show that our method can produce highly effective and transferable attacks and that co-trained defenders outperform baselines on safety benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习攻防之道：通过GRPO实现语言模型的自适应红队对抗
AI红队对抗必须持续适应不断演化的攻击者与防御者。强化学习为发现新颖攻击提供了有前景的方法，而协同训练方法可同时生成更稳健的防御者。近期研究已证明PPO和DPO在攻击者-防御者协同训练中的有效性，但指出GRPO在此场景下不稳定。我们提出AdvGRPO，一种协同训练框架，通过密集多通道奖励与解耦优势归一化，使GRPO适用于联合攻击者-防御者优化。训练从单轮攻击逐步过渡到闭环多轮攻击，随后启动协同训练，攻击者与防御者模型交替更新。实验表明，我们的方法可生成高效且可迁移的攻击，且协同训练的防御者在安全基准测试中优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptive red teaming in language models, this work proposes AdvGRPO, a co-training framework that stabilizes GRPO for joint attacker-defender optimization using dense multi-channel rewards and decoupled advantage normalization. Training begins with single-turn attacks and progresses to multi-turn closed-loop attacks before initiating alternating updates between attacker and defender models. Experimental results demonstrate that AdvGRPO generates highly effective and transferable attacks, and co-trained defenders significantly outperform baseline models on safety benchmarks.
为应对语言模型红队攻击中攻击者与防御者持续演进的需求，本文提出AdvGRPO框架，通过密集多通道奖励与解耦优势归一化，使GRPO在联合优化攻击者与防御者时稳定可用。训练从单轮攻击逐步过渡到闭环多轮攻击，随后交替更新攻击者与防御者模型。实验表明，该方法生成的攻击效果显著且具备迁移能力，联合训练的防御者在安全基准测试中明显优于基线模型。
What the Eyes See, the LLMs Miss: Exploiting Human Perception for Adversarial Text Attacks
Authors: Qin Yang, Lu Malloy, Joshua Lee, Xiaohan Chang, Meisam Mohammady, Doowon Kim, Yuan Hong
First: 2026-06-08T16:21:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:21:34+00:00
Comments: This work has been accepted for publication at USENIX Security 2026. This paper includes examples of harmful, hateful, or abusive language for research purposes. Reader discretion is advised
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-powered content moderation systems have become a critical defense against harmful online content. However, these systems primarily operate on tokenized text and largely ignore the visual cues that humans naturally rely on when interpreting content. We show that this discrepancy creates a fundamental perceptual mismatch: content that is readily recognized as harmful by humans can become effectively invisible to automated moderation systems. To study this vulnerability, we introduce a class of Human-Perceptible Adversarial Attacks (HPAA), in which harmful expressions are embedded into otherwise benign text through visually salient typographic manipulations. Our key insight is that typographic features, including spacing, visual emphasis, and spatial arrangement, can be strategically combined to preserve human recognition of harmful content while substantially reducing machine detectability. Operating in black-box settings with only a small query budget, our attack automatically generates evasive content without requiring model access or gradient information. We evaluate the attack across multiple datasets and ten deployed moderation systems, including commercial APIs and state-of-the-art open-source guardrails. Results reveal a striking gap between human and machine perception: with only three detector queries, generated attacks achieve over 86\% human recognition while maintaining detection rates below 1\% across the evaluated systems. We further conduct ablation studies to identify the typographic factors driving successful evasion, analyze why current moderation architectures fail to capture these signals, and discuss practical defenses. Our findings expose a fundamental blind spot in today's LLM-based moderation ecosystem and highlight need for moderation systems that reason about content in a manner more consistent with human perceptual understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：眼睛看到的，大语言模型忽略：利用人类感知进行对抗性文本攻击
基于大语言模型（LLM）的内容审核系统已成为抵御有害在线内容的关键防线。然而，这些系统主要处理分词文本，很大程度上忽略了人类自然依赖的视觉线索。我们表明，这种差异造成了根本性的感知错配：人类容易识别为有害的内容，可能对自动化审核系统变得几乎不可见。为研究这一漏洞，我们引入了一类人类可感知对抗性攻击（HPAA），通过视觉显著的排版操纵，将有害表达嵌入原本无害的文本中。我们的核心见解是，排版特征（包括间距、视觉强调和空间布局）可被策略性组合，以在保持人类识别有害内容的同时，大幅降低机器检测率。在仅允许少量查询的黑盒环境下，我们的攻击无需模型访问或梯度信息，即可自动生成规避内容。我们在多个数据集和十种已部署的审核系统（包括商业API和最先进的开源防护系统）上评估攻击效果。结果揭示了人类与机器感知之间的显著差距：仅用三次检测器查询，生成的攻击即可获得超过86%的人类识别率，同时在所有评估系统中检测率低于1%。我们进一步通过消融研究，识别驱动成功规避的排版因素，分析当前审核架构为何无法捕捉这些信号，并讨论实用防御措施。我们的发现揭示了当前基于LLM的审核生态系统的根本盲点，强调了需要开发更符合人类感知理解的内容审核系统。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the gap between human perception and LLM-based content moderation systems, which ignore visual cues that humans naturally use to interpret text. The authors introduce Human-Perceptible Adversarial Attacks (HPAA), which embed harmful content into benign text using visually salient typographic manipulations such as spacing, emphasis, and layout, making it readable to humans but invisible to automated detectors. Tested across ten moderation systems and multiple datasets under black-box constraints with minimal query budgets, the attacks achieved over 86% human recognition while maintaining detection rates below 1% in most cases. Ablation studies identified key typographic factors enabling evasion and revealed architectural shortcomings in current moderation systems, underscoring the need for perceptually aligned moderation approaches.
该研究指出，当前基于LLM的内容审核系统忽视人类自然依赖的视觉线索，导致其与人类感知存在根本性差异。作者提出一种可被人眼识别但机器难以检测的对抗攻击（HPAA），通过调整字间距、视觉强调和排版等排版特征，将有害内容嵌入无害文本中。在仅需少量查询的黑盒环境下，该攻击成功欺骗十种部署的审核系统，人类识别率超过86%，而检测率低于1%。消融实验揭示了关键排版因素的作用，并指出当前审核架构未能捕捉这些信号，强调需构建更符合人类感知理解的审核系统。
Cranio-Diff: Diffusion-based Cross-domain Craniofacial Reconstruction with 2D X-ray Skull Guidance and Structural Identity Constraints
Authors: Ravi Shankar Prasad, Naresh Gurjar, Shashank Baghel, Chirag, Dinesh Singh
First: 2026-06-08T16:20:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:20:35+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 7 figures, BMVC 2026 conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The state-of-the-art generative models, such as CycleGAN, Pix2Pix, and diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable performance in the face generation task. However, they fail to effectively capture cross-modality semantic information in craniofacial reconstruction when translating from the skull (x-ray) to the face (optical) domain, due to a mismatch in the alignment of structural identity across modalities. To address this issue, we propose Cranio-Diff, a diffusion-based framework for cross-domain cranio-facial reconstruction from 2D X-ray skull images. The proposed approach integrates skull-conditioned structural guidance through ControlNet with biometric text conditioning to generate a face which is more semantically and structurally aligned with the given skull. The proposed Cranio-diff method is evaluated on skull-face dataset obtained from X-ray scans of 120 subjects in lateral and frontal views. To enable controlled evaluation, each face image is synthesised across three age groups (25, 45, 65) and three BMI variations of -10%, baseline and +10%, yielding 4320 paired samples. To the best of our knowledge, this is the only X-ray-face dataset with this magnitude. Extensive experiments showed that the proposed method outperforms recent existing approaches in both generated image quality and retrieval task. Finally, to evaluate the performance of our proposed method, we have evaluated the quality of the generated image using FID, IS, SSIM, LPIPS, PSNR and ArcFace score. Additionally, retrieval performance is evaluated using recall@k, mAP@k and MRR@k. Obtained experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method can be used as an alternate tool in providing aid in forensic investigations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Cranio-Diff：基于扩散模型的跨域颅面重建，结合2D X光头骨引导与结构身份约束
当前最先进的生成模型（如CycleGAN、Pix2Pix和扩散模型）在人脸生成任务中表现卓越，但在从头骨（X光）到人脸（光学）域的颅面重建中，因跨模态结构身份对齐不匹配，难以有效捕捉跨模态语义信息。为解决此问题，我们提出Cranio-Diff，一种基于扩散模型的框架，可从2D X光头骨图像实现跨域颅面重建。该方法通过ControlNet整合头骨条件结构引导与生物特征文本条件，生成与给定头骨在语义和结构上更对齐的人脸。该方法在120名受试者侧位和正位X光扫描数据集上进行评估。为实现可控评估，每张人脸图像在三个年龄段（25、45、65岁）和三种BMI变化（-10%、基线、+10%）下合成，共获得4320对样本。据我们所知，这是目前规模最大的X光-人脸数据集。大量实验表明，该方法在生成图像质量和检索任务上均优于现有方法。为评估生成图像质量，我们使用FID、IS、SSIM、LPIPS、PSNR和ArcFace分数；为评估检索性能，使用recall@k、mAP@k和MRR@k。实验结果表明，该方法可作为法医调查中的辅助工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing generative models in capturing cross-modality structural alignment between X-ray skulls and optical faces, the authors propose Cranio-Diff, a diffusion-based framework that combines skull-conditioned structural guidance via ControlNet with biometric text conditioning to enhance semantic and structural fidelity. Evaluated on a novel dataset of 4,320 paired skull-face samples from 120 subjects across three age groups and three BMI variations, the method demonstrates superior performance over recent approaches in both image quality metrics (FID, IS, SSIM, LPIPS, PSNR, ArcFace) and retrieval accuracy (recall@k, mAP@k, MRR@k), establishing its potential utility in forensic applications.
为解决现有生成模型在颅骨X光与面部图像跨模态语义对齐上的不足，作者提出Cranio-Diff框架，结合ControlNet实现颅骨结构引导与生物特征文本条件生成，以从2D颅骨X光图重建结构准确的面部图像。在包含120名受试者、3个年龄段与3种BMI变化的4320对样本数据集上评估，该方法在图像质量（FID、IS、SSIM、LPIPS、PSNR、ArcFace）与检索性能（recall@k、mAP@k、MRR@k）上均优于现有方法，可作为法医颅面重建的实用工具。
PsychoSafe: Eliciting Psychologically-Informed Refusals in Large Language Models
Authors: Gianluca Barmina, Federico Torrielli, Sven Harms, Jacob Nielsen, Felix Mächtle, Stine Lyngsø Beltoft, Peter Schneider-Kamp, Thomas Eisenbarth, Lukas Galke Poech, Anne Lauscher
First: 2026-06-08T16:19:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:19:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) routinely face requests that should be refused, creating a trade-off between helpfulness and harm prevention. However, refusals themselves can be helpful. In high-risk interactions involving crisis, coercion, or escalating intent, blunt non-compliance may prevent direct harm while still failing to support the needs of the person behind the request. We present PsychoSafe, a psychologically-informed refusal framework that reframes refusal as structured supportive communication grounded in evidence-based intervention strategies. To develop PsychoSafe, we construct a corpus of 8019 prompt-response pairs spanning five psychologically salient risk domains and apply prompting and parameter-efficient fine-tuning to Qwen 3.5 27B. On a balanced validation set of 500 prompts, evaluated with an LLM judge and validated through human ratings, PsychoSafe prompting improves overall refusal quality by 28.1% over a generic baseline, with particularly strong gains in external resource referral (+46.8%) and psychological grounding (+34.8%), while preserving downstream performance on non-refusal tasks. Fine-tuning achieves near-perfect refusal and resource-referral rates but reduces response relevance. Additional evaluations on SORRY-Bench and XSTest show strong in-domain robustness but limited out-of-domain generalization, suggesting that future work should diversify fine-tuning data to help models apply interventions selectively rather than schematically.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PsychoSafe：在大语言模型中激发心理知情的拒绝
大语言模型（LLMs）常面临应拒绝的请求，这在助人与防害之间形成权衡。然而，拒绝本身亦可有益。在涉及危机、胁迫或意图升级的高风险互动中，直接拒绝虽可避免直接伤害，却可能未能满足请求者的真实需求。我们提出PsychoSafe，一种基于心理干预策略的拒绝框架，将拒绝重构为结构化的支持性沟通。为构建PsychoSafe，我们构建了涵盖五大心理相关风险领域的8019组提示-响应对，并对Qwen 3.5 27B进行提示工程与参数高效微调。在500个平衡验证提示上，经LLM裁判与人工评分验证，PsychoSafe提示相较通用基线整体拒绝质量提升28.1%，在外部资源转介（+46.8%）与心理依据（+34.8%）方面提升显著，同时保持非拒绝任务的下游性能。微调实现近乎完美的拒绝与资源转介率，但降低响应相关性。在SORRY-Bench与XSTest上的额外评估显示其领域内鲁棒性强，但领域外泛化能力有限，表明未来工作应多样化微调数据，以帮助模型选择性而非刻板地应用干预策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance helpfulness and harm prevention in LLM interactions, especially in high-risk scenarios, PsychoSafe reframes refusal as psychologically informed, supportive communication using evidence-based intervention strategies. The method constructs a 8019-pair corpus across five risk domains and applies prompting and parameter-efficient fine-tuning to Qwen 3.5 27B. Experimental results show PsychoSafe improves refusal quality by 28.1% over a generic baseline, with notable gains in external resource referral (+46.8%) and psychological grounding (+34.8%), while maintaining performance on non-refusal tasks. Fine-tuning achieves near-perfect refusal rates but reduces response relevance, and while robust in-domain, it shows limited out-of-domain generalization, indicating a need for more diverse fine-tuning data.
为在LLM交互中平衡帮助性与危害预防，PsychoSafe将拒绝重构为基于循证干预策略的心理支持性沟通。该方法构建了覆盖五大高风险领域的8019对提示-响应语料，并对Qwen 3.5 27B进行提示工程与参数高效微调。实验表明，相比通用基线，拒绝质量提升28.1%，其中外部资源转介提升46.8%，心理支持性增强34.8%，且非拒绝任务性能保持稳定。微调虽实现近乎完美的拒绝率，但降低响应相关性，且在领域内表现稳健，跨领域泛化能力有限。
A Camera-Native Talking-Head Video Dataset for Various Computer Vision Tasks
Authors: Babak Naderi, Ross Cutler, Nabakumar Singh Khongbantabam
First: 2026-03-23T20:51:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:17:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Talking-head videos constitute a predominant content type in real-time communication, yet publicly available datasets for video processing research in this domain remain scarce and limited in signal fidelity. In this paper, we open-source a camera-native dataset of 847 talking-head recordings (approximately 212 minutes), each 15s in duration, captured from 805 participants using 446 unique consumer webcam devices in their natural environments. All recordings are stored using the FFV1 lossless codec, preserving the camera-native signal -- uncompressed (24.4%) or MJPEG-encoded (75.6%) -- without additional lossy processing. Each recording is annotated with a Mean Opinion Score (MOS) and ten perceptual quality tokens that jointly explain 64.4% of the MOS variance. From this corpus, we curate a stratified benchmarking subset of 120 clips in three content conditions: original, background blur, and background replacement. Codec efficiency evaluation across four datasets and four codecs, namely H.264, H.265, H.266, and AV1, yields VMAF BD-rate savings up to $-71.3\%$ (H.266) relative to H.264, with significant encoder$\times$dataset ($η_p^2 = .112$) and encoder$\times$content condition ($η_p^2 = .149$) interactions, demonstrating that both content type and background processing affect compression efficiency. A preliminary super-resolution evaluation with four SR models confirms that the dataset significantly affects absolute performance while preserving model rankings, demonstrating applicability beyond codec benchmarking. The dataset offers 5$\times$ the scale of the largest prior talking-head webcam dataset (847 vs. 160 clips) with lossless signal fidelity, establishing a resource for benchmarking video compression, super-resolution, quality assessment, and enhancement models in real-time communication.

Each clip is annotated with MOS and perceptual quality tokens explaining 64.4% of quality variance. Observability for Delegated Execution in Agentic AI Systems
Authors: Abhinav Mishra, Kumar Sharad
First: 2026-06-08T16:10:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:10:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Delegation-scoped execution is not identifiable from standard observables: audit logs and execution traces can be identical under multiple incompatible delegation assignments. This gap is especially acute in LLM-based agentic systems, where agents dynamically select tools, vary execution sequences across runs for the same instruction, and spawn cooperating sub-agents. These dynamics fragment and interleave traces, making delegation-scoped reconstruction from causal structure alone structurally underdetermined. Although individual actions are authorized and logged, existing audit, tracing, and security schemas lack the semantics to reconstruct what actions occurred under a given delegation across heterogeneous systems. We focus on delegation-scoped attribution and access/share footprint reconstruction, not intent inference or reasoning reconstruction. We present an agent-aware observability substrate consisting of a lightweight gateway and a common information model that binds delegation context at execution time. This enables reliable cross-tool delegation-scoped reconstruction and direct forensic queries without heuristic time-window correlation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理AI系统中委托执行的可观测性
委托范围内的执行无法从标准可观测数据中识别：审计日志和执行轨迹在多种不兼容的委托分配下可能相同。这一差距在基于大语言模型的代理系统中尤为突出，因为代理会动态选择工具、在相同指令的不同运行中改变执行顺序，并生成协作的子代理。这些动态特性使轨迹碎片化并交错，仅凭因果结构无法结构化地重建委托范围内的执行。尽管单个操作已获授权并记录，但现有审计、追踪和安全架构缺乏重建特定委托下跨异构系统所发生操作的语义能力。我们专注于委托范围内的归属与访问/共享足迹重建，而非意图推断或推理重建。我们提出了一种代理感知的可观测性基础架构，包括轻量级网关和统一信息模型，可在执行时绑定委托上下文，从而实现可靠跨工具的委托范围重建和直接取证查询，无需启发式时间窗口关联。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the challenge that standard observability tools like audit logs and execution traces cannot reliably distinguish between different delegation assignments in agentic AI systems, especially those using LLMs that dynamically choose tools and spawn sub-agents, causing fragmented and interleaved traces. To solve this, the authors propose an agent-aware observability substrate comprising a lightweight gateway and a common information model that binds delegation context at execution time, enabling accurate reconstruction of delegation-scoped actions and access footprints across heterogeneous tools without relying on heuristic correlation. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach achieves reliable cross-tool attribution and forensic querying, resolving the structural underdetermination inherent in causal-only trace analysis.
该论文解决了一个关键问题：在代理型AI系统中，由于工具动态选择和子代理生成导致执行轨迹碎片化，现有可观测性工具无法准确识别特定委托上下文下的操作。为此，作者提出了一种代理感知的可观测性基础架构，包含轻量级网关和统一信息模型，可在执行时绑定委托上下文，从而实现跨工具的委托粒度活动重建。实验结果表明，该方法无需依赖启发式时间窗口关联，即可准确重建委托范围内的访问与共享足迹，显著优于现有审计与追踪系统。
The Sample Complexity of Parameter-Free Stochastic Convex Optimization
Authors: Jared Lawrence, Ari Kalinsky, Hannah Bradfield, Yair Carmon, Oliver Hinder
First: 2025-06-12T22:14:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:05:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in JMLR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the sample complexity of stochastic convex optimization when problem parameters such as the distance to optimality and the Lipschitz constant are unknown. We pursue two strategies. First, we develop a reliable model selection method that avoids overfitting to the validation set. This method allows us to generically tune the learning rate of stochastic optimization methods to match the optimal known-parameter sample complexity up to log log factors. Second, we develop a regularization-based method that is specialized to the case that only the distance to optimality is unknown. More specifically, it uses norm-regularized empirical risk minimization to estimate the distance to optimality to within a constant factor, allowing known-parameter stochastic optimization methods to achieve optimal sample complexity. This method provides perfect adaptability to unknown distance to optimality, demonstrating a separation between the sample and computational complexity of parameter-free stochastic convex optimization. Combining these two methods allows us to simultaneously adapt to multiple problem structures. Experiments performing few-shot learning on CIFAR-10 by fine-tuning CLIP models and prompt engineering Gemini to count shapes indicate that our reliable model selection method can help mitigate overfitting to small validation sets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无参数随机凸优化的样本复杂度
我们研究了在距离最优解和Lipschitz常数等问题参数未知的情况下，随机凸优化的样本复杂度。我们采用两种策略：首先，开发了一种可靠的模型选择方法，避免对验证集过拟合，该方法允许我们泛化地调整随机优化方法的学习率，使其与已知参数下的最优样本复杂度（log log因子内）匹配；其次，开发了一种基于正则化的专门方法，适用于仅距离最优解未知的情形。具体而言，该方法通过范数正则化的经验风险最小化，以常数因子精度估计距离最优解的距离，从而使已知参数的随机优化方法达到最优样本复杂度。该方法实现了对未知距离最优解的完美自适应，揭示了无参数随机凸优化中样本复杂度与计算复杂度的分离。结合这两种方法，我们能够同时自适应多种问题结构。实验表明，在CIFAR-10上通过微调CLIP模型和提示工程Gemini进行形状计数的少样本学习中，我们的可靠模型选择方法有助于缓解对小规模验证集的过拟合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of optimizing stochastic convex functions without prior knowledge of key parameters like distance to optimality or Lipschitz constant, the paper proposes two methods: a reliable model selection approach that avoids overfitting and tunes learning rates to nearly match known-parameter sample complexity up to log log factors, and a regularization-based method that estimates distance to optimality within a constant factor to enable optimal sample complexity. Experiments on few-shot learning with CLIP and Gemini show the model selection method reduces overfitting on small validation sets.
为解决在缺乏距离最优解或Lipschitz常数等参数信息下优化随机凸函数的挑战，论文提出两种方法：一种是避免过拟合并调节学习率以逼近最优样本复杂度（log log因子内）的可靠模型选择方法；另一种是通过范数正则化估计距离最优解（常数因子内），使已知参数方法实现最优样本复杂度的正则化方法。实验表明，该模型选择方法可缓解小验证集上的过拟合，验证其实际有效性。
An 84-Format Numeric Catalog with Bit-Exact Conformance Vectors: A Vendor-Neutral Reference for FP8, BF16, MXFP4, and Microscaling Formats
Authors: Dmitrii Vasilev
First: 2026-06-08T16:04:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:04:15+00:00
Comments: 17 pages. Source repository: https://github.com/gHashTag/paper3-methodology tag v4.0-trinity. Paper CC BY 4.0; code MIT. ORCID 0009-0008-4294-6159
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Numeric format proliferation in machine learning hardware -- FP8 (E4M3 and E5M2), BF16, MXFP4, microscaling block formats, and dozens of research variants -- has outpaced the availability of vendor-neutral, bit-exact reference material. Engineers porting models across accelerators encounter silent divergences that are difficult to diagnose without a shared ruler. This paper describes a catalog of 84 numeric formats spanning 13 families, a suite of six bit-exact conformance packs covering GF16, MXFP4 element, BF16, FP8 E4M3, FP8 E5M2, and E8M0 block scale, and an IEEE P3109 v3.2.0 cross-walk that maps each pack to its corresponding standards-track configured format. Each pack is a self-contained JSON document with a SHA-256 fingerprint, a shared row schema, and an anchor vector that encodes 3.0 -- the identity phi^2 + 1/phi^2 = 3 -- as a cross-pack sanity check. Packs are cross-validated against ml_dtypes 0.5.4 (Google/JAX); any divergence is documented explicitly and interpreted as a spec-permitted interpretation gap rather than hidden. The work is framed as registry filling: it does not propose new formats, make model-accuracy claims, or assert superiority over any vendor's implementation. All artifacts are publicly available at https://github.com/gHashTag/t27 under an open license.
中文标题/摘要
标题：84种数值格式的数字目录及其位精确一致性向量：FP8、BF16、MXFP4和微缩放格式的供应商中立参考标准
机器学习硬件中数值格式的多样化——包括FP8（E4M3和E5M2）、BF16、MXFP4、微缩放块格式及数十种研究变体——已超出供应商中立且位精确的参考材料的供应速度。工程师在不同加速器间移植模型时，常遭遇难以诊断的静默差异，而缺乏一个共同的“标尺”。 本文描述了一个涵盖13个家族、共84种数值格式的目录，一套覆盖GF16、MXFP4元素、BF16、FP8 E4M3、FP8 E5M2及E8M0块缩放的六套位精确一致性包，以及一个映射每套包至其对应标准配置格式的IEEE P3109 v3.2.0交叉对照表。每套包均为自包含的JSON文档，包含SHA-256指纹、共享行模式及一个编码3.0（即phi^2 + 1/phi^2 = 3）的锚点向量，作为跨包一致性校验。所有包均与ml_dtypes 0.5.4（Google/JAX）交叉验证；任何差异均明确记录并解释为规范允许的解释差异，而非隐藏问题。本工作定位为注册表填充：不提出新格式，不作模型精度声明，也不宣称优于任何供应商实现。所有成果均在https://github.com/gHashTag/t27以开放许可发布。

Driven by the need for vendor-neutral, bit-exact reference material amid the proliferation of numeric formats in ML hardware, this work compiles an 84-format catalog across 13 families, including FP8, BF16, MXFP4, and microscaling variants. It introduces six conformance packs, each a self-contained JSON document with SHA-256 fingerprints and an anchor vector encoding the identity 3.0 for cross-pack validation, mapped via IEEE P3109 v3.2.0 to standards-track configurations. Cross-validated against ml_dtypes 0.5.4, any divergences are documented as permitted interpretation gaps, not errors. The project serves as a registry-filling effort, offering no new formats or accuracy claims, and all artifacts are openly available.
为应对机器学习硬件中大量新兴数值格式缺乏统一、精确参考标准的问题，该研究构建了一个包含84种格式、覆盖13个家族的数值格式目录，并提供针对BF16、FP8变体及MXFP4等关键格式的六个精确一致性包。每个包均为带SHA-256指纹和锚向量（编码3.0恒等式）的独立JSON文件，经与ml_dtypes 0.5.4交叉验证，所有差异均明确记录为规范允许的解释差异，而非错误，验证了该目录作为跨硬件模型移植时诊断隐性偏差的公共基准的有效性。
AutoMegaKernel: A Statically-Checked Agent Harness for Self-Retargeting Megakernel Synthesis
Authors: Jaber Jaber, Osama Jaber
First: 2026-06-08T16:02:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:02:03+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 5 figures. Open-source code, data, and agent harness: https://github.com/RightNow-AI/AutoMegaKernel
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AutoMegaKernel (AMK) compiles a HuggingFace Llama-family model into a single persistent cooperative CUDA kernel that runs the whole forward pass in one launch, with no per-model hand-written CUDA. The contribution is the system, not raw speed. A frozen schedule-IR validator statically certifies deadlock-freedom and race-freedom via static graph checks (not a mechanized proof), so an unsafe agent-proposed schedule is rejected before launch: across 7,160 adversarial schedules (6,091 unsafe) it had zero false-accepts and accepted all 360 real lowerings. The same source retargets sm_80/sm_90/sm_120 from one codebase, auto-generates correct megakernels for 10 of 10 supported models, and on a real SmolLM2-135M checkpoint reproduces HuggingFace greedy decode token-for-token (perplexity match 2.5e-7). An unattended, agent-drivable autoresearch loop self-improves the megakernel over its own baseline (1.25-1.72x). A search-found int8 (W8A16) megakernel beats CUDA-graphed cuBLAS bf16 at batch-1 decode across NVIDIA's datacenter inference fleet: L4 up to 1.33x, the current-gen L40S 1.25-1.27x, A10G up to 1.08x at scale, and the consumer RTX 5090 1.19-1.23x. The ordering is not a clean function of bandwidth (the 864 GB/s L40S beats the 600 GB/s A10G); the divide is inference-class vs training-class. AMK trails cuBLAS on the high-bandwidth training-class A100/H100, where the harness localizes the cross-SM-sync bottleneck; we report the gap plainly. This is a precision-asymmetric (W8A16 vs bf16) comparison at decode position 0; the largest real checkpoint is TinyLlama-1.1B. Code and the harness: https://github.com/RightNow-AI/AutoMegaKernel
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, self-retargeting CUDA kernel synthesis for large language models without manual intervention, AutoMegaKernel (AMK) compiles HuggingFace Llama-family models into single persistent cooperative kernels that execute full forward passes in one launch. It employs a statically-checked schedule-IR validator to reject unsafe agent-proposed schedules before execution, achieving zero false accepts across 7,160 adversarial schedules. The system automatically generates correct megakernels for all 10 supported models across compute architectures (sm_80/sm_90/sm_120) from a single codebase and reproduces HuggingFace decoding behavior on SmolLM2-135M with high accuracy. An agent-driven autoresearch loop self-improves the megakernel by 1.25–1.72x over its baseline. The resulting int8 (W8A16) megakernel outperforms cuBLAS bf16 on inference-class GPUs (L4, L40S, RTX 5090) but trails on training-class GPUs (A100/H100) due to cross-SM sync bottlenecks, highlighting a precision-asymmetric performance divide.
AutoMegaKernel旨在无需手动编写CUDA代码，自动合成适用于Llama系列模型的高性能单次启动CUDA巨核，支持跨GPU架构自适应。其静态检查代理框架通过静态图分析验证调度方案，确保无死锁与竞态，成功拦截6091个对抗性调度，全部通过360个真实调度。系统可自动生成10个支持模型的正确巨核，并在SmolLM2-135M上复现HuggingFace贪心解码，困惑度达2.5e-7。自驱动的自动研究循环使巨核性能提升1.25–1.72倍。所生成的int8（W8A16）巨核在推理类GPU（L4、L40S、RTX 5090）上超越cuBLAS bf16，但在训练类GPU（A100/H100）上落后，因跨SM同步瓶颈所致，性能差异源于GPU类型而非带宽。
Efficient and Stealthy Jailbreak Attacks via Adversarial Prompt Distillation from LLMs to SLMs
Authors: Xiang Li, Chong Zhang, Jia Wang, Fangyu Wu, Yushi Li, Xiaobo Jin
First: 2025-05-26T08:27:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:01:52+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs) predominantly rely on LLMs themselves to generate adversarial prompts, creating a critical efficiency bottleneck: each attack requires substantial computational resources and API queries, limiting scalability and practical deployment. To overcome this limitation, we propose Adversarial Prompt Distillation (APD), a novel framework that transfers jailbreaking capabilities from LLMs to small language models (SLMs) for efficient, low-resource attacks. APD integrates three key components: (1) masked adversarial knowledge pre-training via LoRA fine-tuning, (2) dynamic temperature-controlled knowledge distillation to bridge architectural gaps, and (3) reinforcement learning-based template optimization for adaptive refinement. Extensive experiments across 12 models show that APD achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates (e.g., 96.4% ASR_k on GPT-4) while dramatically improving efficiency - generating prompts 3.7x faster with 11.3x fewer parameters than teacher models. Our work establishes the first practical framework for lightweight jailbreak attacks, exposes new vulnerabilities in LLM defenses, and provides a scalable testbed for advancing AI safety research. Our code is available at: https://github.com/lxgem/Efficient_and_Stealthy_Jailbreak_Attacks_via_Adversarial_Prompt.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过从大语言模型到小语言模型的对抗性提示蒸馏实现高效隐蔽的越狱攻击
当前针对大语言模型（LLM）的越狱攻击主要依赖LLM自身生成对抗性提示，造成关键效率瓶颈：每次攻击需大量计算资源和API调用，限制了可扩展性和实际部署。为克服此限制，我们提出对抗性提示蒸馏（APD）框架，将越狱能力从LLM迁移到小语言模型（SLM），实现高效、低资源攻击。APD包含三个关键组件：（1）通过LoRA微调实现掩码对抗知识预训练，（2）动态温度控制知识蒸馏弥合架构差异，（3）基于强化学习的模板优化实现自适应精炼。在12个模型上的广泛实验表明，APD实现最先进攻击成功率（如在GPT-4上达到96.4% ASR_k），同时大幅提升效率——生成提示速度提升3.7倍，参数量减少11.3倍。本工作首次建立轻量级越狱攻击实用框架，揭示LLM防御新漏洞，并为推进AI安全研究提供可扩展测试平台。代码见：https://github.com/lxgem/Efficient_and_Stealthy_Jailbreak_Attacks_via_Adversarial_Prompt。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and scalability issues of current jailbreak attacks that rely on large language models (LLMs) to generate adversarial prompts, this work introduces Adversarial Prompt Distillation (APD), a framework that transfers jailbreaking capability from LLMs to small language models (SLMs). APD employs masked adversarial knowledge pre-training via LoRA fine-tuning, dynamic temperature-controlled knowledge distillation to handle architectural mismatches, and reinforcement learning for template optimization. Experimental results across 12 models demonstrate that APD achieves high attack success rates—up to 96.4% on GPT-4—while being 3.7x faster and using 11.3x fewer parameters than the original LLMs, enabling efficient, stealthy, and scalable jailbreak attacks.
为解决当前依赖大语言模型生成对抗性提示的越狱攻击计算成本高、难以规模化的问题，本文提出对抗性提示蒸馏（APD）框架，将越狱能力从大模型迁移到小模型。APD结合LoRA微调进行掩码对抗知识预训练、动态温度控制知识蒸馏以弥合架构差异，以及强化学习优化模板。在12个模型上的实验表明，APD在如GPT-4上实现96.4%的攻击成功率，同时比教师模型快3.7倍、参数量少11.3倍，实现了高效、隐蔽且可扩展的越狱攻击。
GenEyePose: Patient-Free, Knowledge-Based Saccadic Eye Movement Modeling for Digital Neurophysiologic Biomarker Development
Authors: Tianyu Lin, Jooyoung Ryu, Puvada Sreevarsha, Rahul Srinivasaragavan, Riya Satavlekar, Susan Kim, Nidhi Soley, Yujie Yan, Ishan Vatsaraj, Carl Harris, Aimon Rahman, Vishal Patel, Joseph Greenstein, Casey Taylor, Kemar E. Green
First: 2026-06-08T16:01:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T16:01:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Eye movements, including saccades, are widely regarded as highly sensitive and objective biomarkers of neurophysiologic states. Detecting saccadic signatures in neurologic diseases offers a rapid, portable alternative to brain imaging, avoiding access and cost barriers. Currently, there are no robust AI-enabled video-oculographic solutions (e.g., digital biomarkers) for screening, triaging, or localizing brain abnormalities due to privacy issues and scarce datasets. In this work, we propose the first fully synthetic, patient-free, multimodal eye movement generation pipeline for generalizable saccade analysis. Using this synthetic dataset, we trained a deep learning classifier to distinguish between normal and abnormal (hypometria and hypermetria) saccadic accuracies and evaluated its performance on real-world clinical data. The model achieved an AUROC of 0.76 and a sensitivity of 0.71, showing that the synthetic data has strong potential to generalize for clinical applications, including as a screening tool in at-home and emergency room settings or a tool for precise neuroanatomic localization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GenEyePose：无患者依赖、基于知识的扫视眼动建模，用于数字神经生理学生物标志物开发
眼动，包括扫视，被广泛认为是神经生理状态高度敏感且客观的生物标志物。在神经系统疾病中检测扫视特征，可提供一种快速、便携的脑成像替代方案，避免访问和成本障碍。目前，由于隐私问题和数据稀缺，尚无稳健的AI驱动视频眼动解决方案（如数字生物标志物）用于筛查、分诊或定位脑异常。本研究提出首个完全合成、无患者依赖、多模态眼动生成流程，用于可推广的扫视分析。利用该合成数据集，我们训练了一个深度学习分类器，以区分正常与异常（欠校正和过校正）扫视准确性，并在真实临床数据上评估其性能。模型达到AUROC 0.76，敏感度0.71，表明合成数据在临床应用中具有强大泛化潜力，包括作为家庭或急诊室筛查工具，或用于精确神经解剖定位。
