Motivated by the need for privacy-preserving, cost-effective neurophysiologic biomarkers, this study introduces GenEyePose, a patient-free, knowledge-based synthetic pipeline for generating saccadic eye movement data. The method leverages multimodal simulation to create diverse, realistic eye movement patterns without real patient data, enabling training of a deep learning classifier to detect abnormal saccadic accuracy (hypometria and hypermetria) versus normal. Evaluated on real clinical data, the model achieved an AUROC of 0.76 and sensitivity of 0.71, demonstrating strong generalization potential for use in screening or neuroanatomic localization without requiring patient-specific datasets.
为解决隐私保护和数据稀缺问题，本研究提出GenEyePose，一种无需真实患者参与的合成多模态眼动数据生成管道，用于泛化性扫视运动分析。基于该合成数据训练深度学习分类器，区分正常与异常（欠校正和过校正）扫视精度，并在真实临床数据上测试。模型达到AUROC 0.76和敏感度0.71，表明合成数据可有效支持临床生物标志物开发，适用于居家筛查或急诊室中的神经解剖定位。
SoccerNet 2026 Player-Centric Ball-Action Spotting:Retraining and Post-Processing Extensions to the FOOTPASS Baselines
Authors: Parthsarthi Rawat
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-06-08T15:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:59:56+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 SoccerNet Player Centric Ball Action Spotting Challenge, Rank 7
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We describe our system for the SoccerNet 2026 Player-Centric Ball-Action Spotting Challenge, which requires predicting who performs which action and when, across eight classes in broadcast soccer. Building on the three FOOTPASS baselines [1] (TAAD, TAAD+GNN, and TAAD+DST), we contribute four extensions: (1) gradient check pointing to enable full-backbone fine-tuning on a single GPU; (2) fusion of GNN logits into the DST encoder, combining graph-based tactical context with per-player visual features; (3) square-root frequency class weighting to address the 213:1 pass-to-tackle imbalance in the training data; and (4) a post processing pipeline comprising per-class logit gating, temporal frame refinement, jersey re-assignment, and a two-model ensemble. Our system achieves 0.548 Macro F1 on the test set and 0.446 on the challenge set (server evaluation).
中文标题/摘要
标题：SoccerNet 2026 球员中心球行动识别：FOOTPASS 基线的再训练与后处理扩展
我们描述了针对 SoccerNet 2026 球员中心球行动识别挑战的系统，该挑战要求预测在广播足球比赛中，谁在何时执行何种动作（共八类）。基于三个 FOOTPASS 基线（TAAD、TAAD+GNN 和 TAAD+DST），我们贡献了四项扩展：（1）梯度检查点以支持单 GPU 上全骨干网络微调；（2）将 GNN 预测值融合进 DST 编码器，结合图结构战术上下文与每位球员的视觉特征；（3）平方根频率类别加权以应对训练数据中传球与抢断 213:1 的严重不平衡；（4）后处理流程包括按类别 logit 置门、时间帧精调、球衣重新分配及双模型集成。系统在测试集上达到 0.548 的宏 F1 分数，在挑战集（服务器评估）上达到 0.446。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately identify player-specific ball actions in broadcast soccer, this work extends the FOOTPASS baselines by introducing four key enhancements: gradient checkpointing for efficient full-backbone fine-tuning, fusion of GNN logits into the DST encoder to integrate tactical context with visual features, square-root frequency class weighting to mitigate severe class imbalance, and a post-processing pipeline involving logit gating, temporal refinement, jersey reassignment, and model ensembling. These methods collectively improve performance, achieving a Macro F1 score of 0.548 on the test set and 0.446 on the challenge set under server evaluation.
为提升广播足球中球员特定球动作的识别精度，该研究在FOOTPASS基线基础上提出四项改进：通过梯度检查点实现单GPU全骨干微调，将图神经网络预测值融入DST编码器以融合战术上下文与视觉特征，采用平方根频率类权重缓解训练数据中传球与铲球213:1的严重类别不平衡，以及包含类特定logit门控、时序帧优化、球衣重分配和双模型集成的后处理流程。这些方法共同提升了性能，在测试集上达到0.548的Macro F1，在挑战集服务器评估中达到0.446。
MeCo: One-Step MeanFlow-based Corrector for Multi-Channel Speech Separation
Authors: Dohwan Kim, Jung-Woo Choi
First: 2026-06-08T15:58:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:58:31+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, accepted to Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While discriminative models for multi-channel speech separation excel in reference-based metrics, they often exhibit suboptimal human listening quality. To address this, we propose a novel MeanFlow-based one-step generative corrector (MeCo). MeCo learns a conditional average velocity field to map discriminative estimates directly onto the clean speech manifold in a single step. To maximize one-step generation performance, we introduce Data-Space Optimization (DSO). DSO integrates an $\mathbf{x}_r$-loss, which penalizes prediction errors on longer displacement intervals to serve as a generative objective for human listening quality, with an Endpoint SI-SDR loss that directly optimizes terminal signal fidelity. Experiments demonstrate that MeCo achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance with minimal computational overhead, simultaneously achieving superior signal fidelity and human listening quality in both in-domain and out-of-domain scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MeCo：基于MeanFlow的一步生成式校正器，用于多通道语音分离
尽管用于多通道语音分离的判别式模型在参考指标上表现优异，但其人耳听感质量常不理想。为此，我们提出了一种基于MeanFlow的一步生成式校正器（MeCo）。MeCo学习一个条件平均速度场，将判别式估计直接映射到干净语音流形上，一步完成。为最大化一步生成性能，我们引入了数据空间优化（DSO），其结合了惩罚长位移区间预测误差的x_r损失（作为人耳听感质量的生成目标）与端点SI-SDR损失（直接优化终端信号保真度）。实验表明，MeCo在计算开销极小的情况下，同时在域内和域外场景中实现了信号保真度与人耳听感质量的最先进（SOTA）表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between high reference-based performance and poor human listening quality in discriminative multi-channel speech separation models, the authors propose MeCo, a one-step generative corrector based on MeanFlow that maps discriminative outputs directly onto the clean speech manifold. MeCo employs Data-Space Optimization (DSO), combining an x_r-loss to penalize long-displacement errors for perceptual fidelity and an Endpoint SI-SDR loss to optimize final signal quality. Experimental results show MeCo achieves state-of-the-art performance with minimal computational cost, outperforming existing methods in both signal fidelity and human listening quality across in-domain and out-of-domain conditions.
为解决判别式多通道语音分离模型在参考指标表现优异但人耳听感不佳的问题，作者提出MeCo，一种基于MeanFlow的一步生成校正器，直接将判别式输出映射至纯净语音流形。MeCo采用数据空间优化（DSO），结合x_r损失（惩罚长距离预测误差以提升听感）与端点SI-SDR损失（优化最终信号保真度）。实验表明，MeCo在计算开销极小的前提下，于域内与域外场景均取得当前最优性能，同时在信号保真度与人耳听感上显著优于现有方法。
(Auto)formalization is supposed to be easy: Trellis process semantics for spelling out rigorous proofs
Authors: Wesley Pegden
First: 2026-06-08T15:57:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:57:14+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 7 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Trellis: an autoformalization system that leverages LLM agents in a deterministically constrained workflow to enforce incremental progress in Lean autoformalization tasks through iterative refinement of natural language proofs. Our approach is motivated by the common mathematician's notion of what it means to have a rigorous proof in the first place: namely, that it would be routine to elaborate any part of the proof in further detail. The result is a system which aims to achieve reliable autoformalization on a modest budget and with generalist agents, with specialization to autoformalization coming not from any task-specific agent training but instead from a meaning-of-rigor inspired workflow enforced by process semantics. We link to an end-to-end Lean formalization of a recent Ramsey theory breakthrough produced by the process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：(自动)形式化本应简单：通过拼写严谨证明的Trellis过程语义
我们提出了Trellis：一个利用LLM代理在确定性约束工作流中进行自动形式化的系统，通过自然语言证明的迭代细化，强制在Lean自动形式化任务中实现渐进式进展。我们的方法源于数学家对“严谨证明”最初含义的普遍理解：即任何证明部分都应能常规地进一步展开细节。该系统旨在以有限预算和通用代理实现可靠的自动形式化，其对自动形式化的专业化并非来自特定任务的代理训练，而是源于受“严谨性含义”启发的工作流，由过程语义强制执行。我们链接了一个由该过程生成的近期Ramsey理论突破的端到端Lean形式化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mathematician’s intuitive belief that rigorous proofs should be easily elaborable, the authors introduce Trellis: an autoformalization system using LLM agents within a deterministic workflow that enforces incremental progress through iterative refinement of natural language proofs. The method avoids task-specific agent training by instead relying on a process semantics inspired by the concept of rigor, guiding agents to progressively formalize proofs in Lean. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach achieves reliable autoformalization with generalist agents and modest computational resources, culminating in the end-to-end formalization of a recent Ramsey theory breakthrough.
受数学家认为严谨证明应可逐步展开的启发，作者构建了Trellis系统，该系统通过确定性工作流引导LLM代理迭代细化自然语言证明，最终在Lean中实现形式化。其方法基于“严谨性”的语义流程，而非针对特定任务训练代理。实验表明，该系统仅用通用代理和有限资源即可实现可靠自动形式化，并成功完成了一项近期Ramsey理论突破的完整形式化。
PicoSAM3: Real-Time In-Sensor Region-of-Interest Segmentation
Authors: Pietro Bonazzi, Nicola Farronato, Stefan Zihlmann, Haotong Qin, Michele Magno
First: 2026-03-12T13:31:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:56:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time, on-device segmentation is critical for latency-sensitive and privacy-aware applications such as smart glasses and Internet-of-Things devices. We introduce PicoSAM3, a lightweight promptable visual segmentation model optimized for edge and in-sensor execution, including deployment on the Sony IMX500 vision sensor. PicoSAM3 has 1.3M parameters and combines a dense CNN architecture with region of interest prompt encoding, Efficient Channel Attention, and knowledge distillation from SAM2 and SAM3. On COCO and LVIS, PicoSAM3 achieves 65.45% and 64.01% mIoU, respectively, outperforming existing SAM-based and edge-oriented baselines at similar or lower complexity. The INT8 quantized model preserves accuracy with negligible degradation while enabling real-time in-sensor inference at 11.82ms latency on the IMX500, fully complying with its memory and operator constraints. Ablation studies show that distillation from large SAM models yields up to +14.5% mIoU improvement over supervised training and demonstrate that high-quality, spatially flexible promptable segmentation is feasible directly at the sensor level.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PicoSAM3：实时传感器内区域兴趣分割
实时设备端分割对延迟敏感且注重隐私的应用（如智能眼镜和物联网设备）至关重要。我们提出了PicoSAM3，一个专为边缘和传感器内执行优化的轻量级可提示视觉分割模型，包括在索尼IMX500视觉传感器上的部署。PicoSAM3拥有130万参数，结合密集CNN架构、区域兴趣提示编码、高效通道注意力机制，并从SAM2和SAM3进行知识蒸馏。在COCO和LVIS数据集上，PicoSAM3分别达到65.45%和64.01%的mIoU，优于现有基于SAM和边缘导向的基线模型，在相似或更低复杂度下表现更优。INT8量化模型在精度上几乎无损，同时在IMX500上实现11.82毫秒的实时传感器内推理，完全符合其内存和算子约束。消融研究表明，从大型SAM模型蒸馏可使mIoU提升最高达14.5%，并证明高质量、空间灵活的可提示分割可在传感器级别直接实现。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for low-latency, privacy-preserving segmentation on edge devices like smart glasses and IoT sensors, PicoSAM3 is a lightweight, promptable model optimized for in-sensor execution. It combines a dense CNN with region-of-interest prompt encoding, Efficient Channel Attention, and knowledge distillation from SAM2/SAM3, resulting in a 1.3M-parameter architecture. Evaluated on COCO and LVIS, it achieves 65.45% and 64.01% mIoU, surpassing existing edge and SAM-based baselines at comparable or lower complexity. The INT8 quantized version maintains accuracy with minimal loss, enabling real-time inference at 11.82ms on the Sony IMX500 while respecting its memory and operator limits. Ablation studies confirm that distillation from SAM models boosts mIoU by up to 14.5% over supervised training, proving high-quality promptable segmentation is viable directly at the sensor level.
为满足智能眼镜和物联网设备等对低延迟、隐私保护的实时分割需求，PicoSAM3 是一款专为传感器内执行优化的轻量级可提示分割模型，支持在索尼 IMX500 视觉传感器上部署。该模型融合密集卷积网络、区域兴趣提示编码、高效通道注意力机制，并通过从 SAM2/SAM3 进行知识蒸馏，参数量仅 130 万。在 COCO 和 LVIS 数据集上，其 mIoU 分别达 65.45% 和 64.01%，优于现有边缘设备和 SAM 基线模型，且复杂度相当或更低。INT8 量化版本保持精度，推理延迟仅 11.82ms，完全符合 IMX500 的内存与算子限制；消融实验表明，蒸馏可使 mIoU 相比监督训练提升最高 14.5%。
Correlation Is Not Enough: Embedding Human Metadata for Individual Causal Discovery
Authors: Suraj Biswas, Saurabh Gupta, Pritam Mukherjee
First: 2026-06-08T15:54:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:54:28+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 18 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ask a pretrained biomedical language model whether "cortisol 28 ug/dL" and "stock-market volatility" are related, and it returns a cosine similarity of 0.83 on a scale where 1.0 means identical. The two share no mechanism. This is not a corner case: every off-the-shelf biomedical encoder we tested (BioBERT, PubMedBERT, BioM-ELECTRA) scores unrelated cross-domain pairs between 0.76 and 0.92 when the answer should be near zero. Accuracy on cross-domain discrimination is 0%. Retrieval systems survive this, because a language model downstream filters the noise. A Large Behavioural Model (LBM), a foundation model whose subject is a person rather than a sentence, does not: it reasons over a graph of a user's life and treats embedding proximity as evidence that two events are causally linked. False proximity writes a false causal edge, and everything downstream inherits the error. Here, embedding geometry is not a tuning knob; it is correctness. We report the fix. A contrastive pass over 72,034 pairs raises PubMedBERT BIOSSES correlation from 0.633 to 0.828 and within-vs-across-domain separation from 1.05x to 1.63x. A second pass, BODHI, mines hard negatives from edges absent in a biomedical knowledge graph and lifts separation to 2.30x and the discrimination gap to +0.392, at a 4.5% BIOSSES cost. On an Intel Xeon 6737P with AMX, OpenVINO cuts single-query latency from 1367 ms to 10 ms (133x) and reaches 555 sentences/sec. One finding contradicts standard advice: FP16 beats INT8 on this silicon at every serving batch size, and we explain why. The same model on a no-AMX Ice Lake instance runs 13-27x slower. We release the benchmark suite, training corpora, the BODHI generator, and the OpenVINO scripts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相关性不足：为个体因果发现嵌入人类元数据
询问一个预训练的生物医学语言模型“皮质醇28 ug/dL”与“股市波动”是否相关，它返回0.83的余弦相似度（1.0表示完全相同）。两者并无共同机制。这不是特例：我们测试的每个现成生物医学编码器（BioBERT、PubMedBERT、BioM-ELECTRA）在跨领域无关对上得分介于0.76至0.92之间，而正确答案应接近零。跨域判别准确率仅为0%。 检索系统能应对这一问题，因为下游语言模型可过滤噪声。而大型行为模型（LBM）——以个体而非句子为研究对象的基础模型——无法做到这一点：它在用户生活图谱上进行推理，并将嵌入空间的邻近性视为因果关联的证据。虚假邻近性会生成虚假因果边，下游所有环节都将继承该错误。此处，嵌入几何结构并非调参旋钮，而是正确性本身。 我们报告了修复方案：对72,034对数据进行对比学习，可将PubMedBERT的BIOSSES相关性从0.633提升至0.828，跨域分离度从1.05x提升至1.63x。第二阶段BODHI方法从生物医学知识图谱中挖掘缺失边作为难负样本，将分离度提升至2.30x，判别差距扩大至+0.392，代价仅为4.5%的BIOSSES。在配备AMX的Intel Xeon 6737P上，OpenVINO将单查询延迟从1367毫秒降至10毫秒（133倍加速），达到555句/秒。一个发现与常规建议相悖：FP16在该芯片上所有服务批大小下均优于INT8，我们解释了原因。同一模型在无AMX的Ice Lake实例上运行速度慢13-27倍。我们发布基准套件、训练语料、BODHI生成器及OpenVINO脚本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of biomedical language models to distinguish unrelated cross-domain concepts—such as cortisol levels and stock-market volatility—the paper proposes embedding human metadata to correct causal inference errors. The method involves two contrastive training passes: first, a general pass that improves correlation and domain separation in PubMedBERT; second, BODHI, which mines hard negatives from biomedical knowledge graphs to further boost separation and discrimination. Experiments show correlation increases from 0.633 to 0.828, domain separation from 1.05x to 2.30x, and discrimination gap reaches +0.392 with minimal cost. Performance gains are also demonstrated via OpenVINO optimization, reducing latency 133x and achieving 555 sentences/sec on AMX hardware, while FP16 outperforms INT8 despite standard recommendations.
鉴于生物医学语言模型无法区分跨领域无关概念（如皮质醇水平与股市波动），本文提出通过嵌入人类元数据来修正因果推理错误。方法包含两个对比训练阶段：第一阶段提升PubMedBERT的语义相关性与领域分离能力；第二阶段BODHI从生物医学知识图谱中挖掘硬负样本，进一步增强分离度与判别能力。实验表明，BODHI使领域分离度提升2.30倍，判别准确度提升+0.392，代价仅为4.5%性能损失；在支持AMX的硬件上，通过OpenVINO推理加速133倍，单查询延迟从1367毫秒降至10毫秒。值得注意的是，FP16在该硬件上优于INT8，与常规建议相反。
Transition-Based Digital Twin Modelling for Alzheimer's Disease under Sparse Longitudinal Data
Authors: Yinyu Huang, Yilin Zhang, Sofia Michopoulou, Christopher Kipps, Rahman Attar
First: 2026-06-08T15:54:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:54:10+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables. Accepted as a full-length paper at the International Conference on AI in Healthcare (AIiH) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression is highly heterogeneous and is typically observed through sparse and irregular longitudinal data, posing challenges for prediction and personalised monitoring. Existing machine learning approaches have improved AD prediction using multimodal data, yet often focus on static classification or cohort-level risk estimation, providing limited support for subject-specific modelling and uncertainty-aware reasoning. To address these limitations, we present a personalised digital twin framework for AD prediction and scenario-based analysis using multimodal longitudinal data. The proposed approach integrates complementary modelling strategies to capture clinical transitions and temporal dependencies across visits. Using data from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), including cognitive assessments, clinical variables, and MRI-derived phenotypes, the framework predicts cognitive status and diagnostic categories while quantifying predictive uncertainty and enabling patient-specific what-if trajectory analysis. Evaluation on leak-free subject-level splits demonstrates strong performance in score forecasting and diagnosis classification. In this sparse and irregular ADNI setting, transition-based modelling of adjacent visits achieved higher predictive accuracy than the sequence-based branch, suggesting that local transition modelling may be more data-efficient. While sequence models remain valuable for uncertainty-aware trajectory forecasting, local transition modelling offers a more data-efficient and robust predictive strategy. These findings highlight the importance of aligning temporal modelling strategies with clinical data structure and suggest that transition-based digital twin formulations may provide a practical and interpretable approach for personalised disease forecasting in neurodegenerative disorders.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于转换的数字孪生建模用于在稀疏纵向数据下的阿尔茨海默病预测
阿尔茨海默病（AD）进展高度异质，通常通过稀疏且不规则的纵向数据观察，给预测和个性化监测带来挑战。现有机器学习方法虽利用多模态数据提升了AD预测，但多聚焦于静态分类或队列级风险估计，对个体化建模和不确定性推理支持有限。为解决此问题，我们提出一种基于多模态纵向数据的个性化数字孪生框架，用于AD预测与情景分析。该方法整合互补建模策略，捕捉临床转变及跨就诊时间依赖性。利用阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议（ADNI）数据（含认知评估、临床变量及MRI衍生表型），框架可预测认知状态与诊断类别，量化预测不确定性，并支持患者特异性“假设情景”轨迹分析。在无泄漏的个体级划分评估中，该框架在评分预测与诊断分类方面表现优异。在ADNI稀疏不规则数据环境下，相邻就诊的转换建模比序列分支模型具有更高预测精度，表明局部转换建模可能更数据高效。尽管序列模型在不确定性轨迹预测中仍具价值，局部转换建模提供更数据高效且稳健的预测策略。这些发现强调了时间建模策略需与临床数据结构对齐的重要性，并提示基于转换的数字孪生形式可能为神经退行性疾病个性化疾病预测提供实用且可解释的方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting Alzheimer's disease progression from sparse and irregular longitudinal data, this study proposes a transition-based digital twin framework that models subject-specific clinical transitions using multimodal data. The method integrates temporal modeling strategies to capture local transitions between adjacent visits, enabling personalized cognitive status prediction and uncertainty quantification. Evaluated on ADNI data with leak-free subject splits, the approach outperformed sequence-based models in predictive accuracy, demonstrating that local transition modeling is more data-efficient while still supporting scenario-based trajectory analysis. The results suggest that aligning temporal modeling with data sparsity improves practicality and interpretability for personalized neurodegenerative disease forecasting.
为应对阿尔茨海默病在稀疏不规则纵向数据下预测困难的问题，该研究提出一种基于转换的数字孪生框架，利用多模态数据建模个体临床状态的转变。方法通过捕捉相邻访视间的局部转换关系，实现个性化认知状态预测与不确定性量化。在ADNI数据集上进行的评估显示，该方法在预测精度上优于序列模型，表明局部转换建模更节省数据且更稳健，同时支持情景化轨迹分析。结果表明，将时间建模策略与数据稀疏性对齐可提升个性化神经退行性疾病预测的实用性和可解释性。
An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making
Authors: Yonchanok Khaokaew, Ruochen Kong, Andreas Zufle, Hao Xue, Taylor Anderson, Chandini Raina MacIntyre, Matthew Scotch, Flora D. Salim, David J Heslop
First: 2026-06-04T16:30:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:54:07+00:00
Comments: 12 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modelling individual decision-making during infectious disease outbreaks is crucial for understanding behavioural dynamics and informing effective public health interventions. Prior work has shown that large language models can simulate realistic human behaviour by generating agent decisions based on demographic prompts and situational context. We build on this foundation with a spatially grounded, agent-based simulation framework that integrates LLM-generated decisions about self-reported influenza-like illness into a census-based synthetic population of agents. Location is treated as a central feature: agents are assigned to spatial units within cities, capturing the spatial distributions of different demographic groups using real-world census data and enabling geographically diverse behavioural modelling. We implement and compare three decision scenarios, independent reasoning, household influence, and message framing, and simulate self-reporting outcomes in San Francisco and Atlanta. Results reveal that income and education are the dominant drivers of reporting rate variation, with smaller but consistent effects from geography, LLM model choice, and message framing. Our framework generates synthetic data that captures both social and geographic heterogeneity, supporting spatial epidemiological modelling and bias-aware behavioural analysis.

标题：基于大语言模型决策的传染病传播模拟
在传染病暴发期间模拟个体决策对于理解行为动态并指导有效的公共卫生干预至关重要。先前研究表明，大语言模型可通过结合人口统计学提示和情境背景生成代理决策，从而模拟真实的人类行为。我们在此基础上构建了一个空间锚定的基于代理的模拟框架，将大语言模型生成的自我报告流感样疾病决策整合进基于人口普查数据的合成人口中。位置被视为核心特征：代理被分配至城市内的空间单元，利用真实人口普查数据捕捉不同人口群体的空间分布，从而支持地理多样性的行为建模。我们实施并比较了三种决策场景：独立推理、家庭影响和信息框架，并在旧金山和亚特兰大模拟自我报告结果。结果表明，收入和教育是报告率差异的主要驱动因素，地理因素、大语言模型选择和信息框架则产生较小但稳定的影响。我们的框架生成的合成数据同时捕捉社会与地理异质性，支持空间流行病学建模和偏见感知的行为分析。

Three decision scenarios—individual reasoning, household influence, and message framing—are compared, revealing that income and education are the strongest predictors of reporting rates, while geography, LLM choice, and message framing have smaller but consistent effects. The framework produces synthetic data that captures social and geographic variation, supporting epidemiological modeling and bias-aware behavioral analysis.

Visual Prompting Meets Feature Reconstruction-Based Anomaly Detection with Dual-Teacher Supervision
Authors: Mateo Diaz-Bone, Daniel Caraballo, Florian Scheidegger, Thomas Frick, Mattia Rigotti, Andrea Bartezzaghi, Roy Assaf, Niccolo Avogaro, Yagmur G. Cinar, Brown Ebouky, Filip M. Janicki, Piotr S. Kluska, Cezary Skura, Cristiano Malossi
First: 2026-06-08T15:52:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:52:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent Anomaly Detection methods achieve perfect detection and segmentation scores on well-established datasets, such as MVTec. However, many of these methods face challenges when foundational assumptions - such as consistent object scale, viewpoint, background, illumination, and centered placement - are violated. Those variations that occur render anomaly detection methods unusable in many real-world scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce three key contributions: (1) a visual prompting pipeline that isolates objects using foreground-background masking; (2) a mechanism for unfreezing the teacher in student-teacher models to improve domain adaptability; and (3) a data augmentation strategy leveraging diffusion-generated synthetic images to enhance anomaly detection performance. We achieve a 3.5 percentage point improvement over the previous state-of-the-art on the challenging AeBAD dataset by using the Masked Multiscale Reconstruction (MMR) model as our backbone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉提示与基于特征重建的异常检测结合双教师监督
近期异常检测方法在MVTec等成熟数据集上实现了完美的检测与分割得分，但当基础假设（如物体尺度、视角、背景、光照和居中放置的一致性）被违反时，许多方法面临挑战。这些变化使异常检测方法在许多现实场景中无法使用。为解决这些局限性，我们提出三项关键贡献：（1）一种通过前景-背景掩码分离物体的视觉提示流程；（2）一种在学生-教师模型中解冻教师以提升领域适应性的机制；（3）一种利用扩散生成合成图像增强异常检测性能的数据增强策略。我们使用Masked Multiscale Reconstruction（MMR）模型作为骨干网络，在具有挑战性的AeBAD数据集上实现了比先前最先进方法高出3.5个百分点的性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of existing anomaly detection methods under real-world variations like scale, viewpoint, and illumination changes, this work proposes a visual prompting pipeline using foreground-background masking, a dynamic teacher unfreezing mechanism for improved domain adaptability, and diffusion-based data augmentation to enhance robustness. Evaluated on the challenging AeBAD dataset, the proposed Masked Multiscale Reconstruction (MMR) model, under dual-teacher supervision, achieves a 3.5 percentage point improvement over prior state-of-the-art performance.
为解决现有异常检测模型在真实场景中因尺度、视角、光照等变化导致泛化能力差的问题，本文提出基于视觉提示的前景-背景分割机制、动态解冻教师模型以提升域适应能力，以及扩散模型生成的增强数据策略。在具有挑战性的AeBAD数据集上，采用双教师监督的Masked Multiscale Reconstruction（MMR）模型，性能较当前最优方法提升3.5个百分点。
SpatialWorld: Benchmarking Interactive Spatial Reasoning of Multimodal Agents in Real-World Tasks
Authors: Hongcheng Gao, Hailong Qu, Jingyi Tang, Jiahao Wang, Zihao Huang, Hengkang Qiao, Shihong Huang, Junming Yang, Yi Li, Hongyixuan Yuan, Wenjie Li, Bohan Zeng, Wenbo Li, Bo Wang, Jianhui Liu, Olive Huang, Haoyang Huang, Wentao Zhang, Guoqing Huang, Nan Duan, Yinpeng Dong
First: 2026-06-08T15:51:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:51:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spatial reasoning is a foundational capability for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to perceive and operate within the physical world. However, existing benchmarks predominantly rely on passive evaluation (e.g., static VQA) or simulator-specific pipelines, failing to assess general interactive spatial understanding. We introduce SpatialWorld, a unified benchmark designed specifically for evaluating the interactive spatial understanding of multimodal agents in complex real-world tasks. Integrating eight heterogeneous simulation backends under a shared, simulator-agnostic protocol, SpatialWorld features 760 human-annotated tasks across diverse domains (e.g., household routines, travel, social collaboration). Agents must solve tasks under vision-only partial observability, actively gathering egocentric visual evidence and expressing decisions via a unified, text-based action interface native to MLLMs. For reliable evaluation, each task includes a human-validated initial state, a reference trajectory, and a terminal-state verifier. Evaluating 15 advanced agents reveals that robust spatial task solving remains challenging: the strongest model, GPT-5, achieves an average task success rate (TSR) of only 17.4%, while the leading open-source model, Qwen-3.5, reaches 14.1%. Further analysis exposes a clear mismatch between task success and execution efficiency, alongside substantial domain-specific performance variations. These bottlenecks in active exploration and long-horizon planning position SpatialWorld as a rigorous testbed for future spatial agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpatialWorld：评估多模态代理在现实任务中交互式空间推理的基准
空间推理是多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）感知并操作物理世界的基础能力。然而，现有基准主要依赖被动评估（如静态视觉问答）或特定模拟器管道，无法评估通用交互式空间理解能力。我们提出了SpatialWorld，一个专为评估多模态代理在复杂现实任务中交互式空间理解能力而设计的统一基准。该基准整合了八个异构模拟器后端，通过共享的、与模拟器无关的协议实现，涵盖760个由人类标注的任务，涉及多种领域（如家庭日常、旅行、社交协作）。代理需在仅视觉部分可观测条件下解决任务，主动收集以自我为中心的视觉证据，并通过统一的文本型动作接口（原生适配MLLMs）表达决策。为确保可靠评估，每个任务均包含经人类验证的初始状态、参考轨迹和终端状态验证器。对15个先进代理的评估表明，稳健的空间任务解决仍具挑战性：最强模型GPT-5平均任务成功率（TSR）仅为17.4%，领先开源模型Qwen-3.5为14.1%。进一步分析揭示任务成功率与执行效率之间存在明显不匹配，且各领域性能差异显著。这些在主动探索与长时规划方面的瓶颈，使SpatialWorld成为未来空间代理的严格测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models' (MLLMs) interactive spatial reasoning in real-world contexts, the authors introduce SpatialWorld, a unified benchmark that integrates eight heterogeneous simulation backends under a simulator-agnostic protocol. The benchmark features 760 human-annotated tasks across domains like household routines and travel, requiring agents to operate under vision-only partial observability while gathering egocentric visual evidence and executing actions via a text-based interface. Evaluation of 15 advanced agents shows that even the strongest model, GPT-5, achieves only 17.4% task success rate, while Qwen-3.5 scores 14.1%, highlighting persistent challenges in active exploration and long-horizon planning. The benchmark also reveals a disconnect between task success and efficiency, along with significant domain-specific performance gaps.
为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在真实世界任务中的交互式空间推理能力，作者提出了SpatialWorld基准，通过要求代理在部分视觉观测下主动收集第一人称视觉证据并执行文本动作来评估其解决复杂任务的能力。该基准整合了八个异构模拟器，涵盖760个由人类标注的任务，涉及家庭日常、旅行等多领域。对15个先进代理（包括GPT-5和Qwen-3.5）的评估显示，最强模型仅达成17.4%的任务成功率，表明主动探索与长时规划仍存在显著挑战。结果还揭示任务成功率与执行效率之间存在脱节，且各领域表现差异明显，凸显SpatialWorld作为严格评估平台的价值。
Algorithm for Contextual Queueing Bandits with Rate-Optimal Queue Length Regret
Authors: Seoungbin Bae, Dabeen Lee
First: 2026-06-08T15:51:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:51:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contextual queueing bandits provide a framework for learning to schedule heterogeneous jobs under unknown context-dependent service rates. Under stochastic contexts, existing algorithms achieve $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/4})$ queue length regret, defined as the expected difference between the learner's and oracle's queue lengths at horizon $T$. In this paper, we improve this rate to $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$. The key observation is that random exploration is needed only up to a carefully chosen cutoff round, rather than throughout the entire horizon. We propose CQB-$η$-2, a three-phase algorithm: (i) pure random exploration to construct an initial estimator, (ii) $η$-random exploration combined with a UCB rule to continue learning while maintaining negative drift, and (iii) pure UCB after the exploration cutoff. Our proof decomposes the queue length regret at the cutoff round. Before the cutoff, negative drift suppresses queue length differences caused by suboptimal choices. After the cutoff, the first two phases provide sufficient random exploration samples, ensuring that UCB decisions incur small departure-rate gaps. Combining these two bounds yields queue length regret of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$. We further prove a minimax lower bound of order $Ω(T^{-1/2})$. The proof constructs two hard instances that are statistically indistinguishable up to the final service decision, and uses a queue-specific coupling argument to convert the resulting testing error into queue length regret. Together, our upper and lower bounds characterize the minimax dependence on the horizon $T$ up to logarithmic factors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：速率最优队列长度遗憾的上下文排队多臂赌博机算法
上下文排队多臂赌博机提供了一个在未知上下文依赖服务速率下学习调度异构作业的框架。在随机上下文条件下，现有算法实现 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/4})$ 的队列长度遗憾（定义为学习者与最优策略在时间 $T$ 时队列长度的期望差）。本文将此速率提升至 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$。关键观察是：随机探索仅需在精心选择的截止轮次前进行，而非整个时间跨度。我们提出 CQB-$η$-2 算法，包含三个阶段：(i) 纯随机探索以构建初始估计器；(ii) $η$-随机探索结合 UCB 规则，在保持负漂移的同时继续学习；(iii) 探索截止后纯 UCB。证明将截止轮次的队列长度遗憾分解：截止前，负漂移抑制由次优选择导致的队列长度差异；截止后，前两个阶段提供足够随机探索样本，确保 UCB 决策产生小的离开率差距。结合这两个界，得到 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$ 的队列长度遗憾。我们进一步证明了 $Ω(T^{-1/2})$ 的最小最大下界。证明构造两个在最终服务决策前统计不可区分的困难实例，并使用队列特异性耦合论证将测试误差转化为队列长度遗憾。综上，我们的上界与下界刻画了对时间 $T$ 的最小最大依赖关系，至对数因子精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently schedule heterogeneous jobs under unknown context-dependent service rates, this paper addresses the contextual queueing bandit problem and improves the queue length regret rate from $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/4})$ to $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$. The method, called CQB-$η$-2, operates in three phases: initial pure random exploration to build an estimator, followed by $η$-random exploration with UCB to maintain negative drift, and finally pure UCB after a carefully chosen exploration cutoff. The analysis decomposes regret into pre- and post-cutoff components, showing that negative drift controls regret before the cutoff, while sufficient exploration after ensures small departure-rate gaps for UCB. The authors also establish a minimax lower bound of $Ω(T^{-1/2})$, proving that the improved rate is optimal up to logarithmic factors.
为解决在未知上下文依赖服务率下高效调度异构任务的问题，本文研究上下文队列化多臂赌博机问题，将队列长度遗憾率从 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/4})$ 提升至 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$。所提算法 CQB-$η$-2 分三阶段：初始纯随机探索构建估计器，随后结合 UCB 的 $η$-随机探索维持负漂移，最后在探索截止后纯用 UCB 决策。分析将遗憾率分解为截止前后的两部分，证明前期负漂移抑制了次优选择导致的队列长度偏差，后期充分探索确保 UCB 决策的离开率差距小。同时，作者通过构造两个统计上难以区分的硬实例并结合队列耦合论证，证明了 $Ω(T^{-1/2})$ 的最小最大下界，确认所获速率在对数因子内是最优的。
Cross-Modal Masking for Robust Silent Speech Synthesis Using sEMG and Lipreading
Authors: Eder del Blanco, David Gimeno-Gómez, Eva Navas, Carlos-D. Martínez-Hinarejos, Inma Hernáez
First: 2026-06-08T15:50:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:50:51+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 7 figures and 6 tables. Submitted to Transactions on Audio, Speech and Language Processing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speech restoration through silent speech interfaces (SSIs) has emerged as a promising assistive technology for individuals with impaired or absent laryngeal voice production. Among non-invasive SSI modalities, surface electromyography (sEMG) and video-based lipreading provide complementary articulatory information, yet their integration for continuous speech synthesis remains underexplored. Moreover, existing multimodal approaches rarely address robustness to modality degradation or temporary sensor failure, limiting their applicability in realistic scenarios. In this work, we propose a masked multimodal speech synthesis framework that jointly leverages sEMG and lipreading signals through modality masking during training. Under multispeaker settings, the proposed approach reduces word error rate by up to 14 absolute percentage points compared to the strongest unimodal baseline. Experimental results not only show that masking strategies are critical for these performance gains and robustness under low-bitrate conditions, but also that they generalize better than degradation-specific data augmentations in the presence of modality absence conditions. Phone-level analyses further reveal complementary contributions across modalities, with particularly strong benefits for vowels and for specific consonant groups. Overall, these findings demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of masked multimodal integration for silent speech synthesis, although adaptation to laryngectomized speakers remains an open research challenge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用表面肌电图和唇读的跨模态掩码用于鲁棒静默语音合成
静默语音接口（SSI）通过语音恢复，已成为帮助声带发声受损或缺失人群的有前景的辅助技术。在非侵入式SSI模态中，表面肌电图（sEMG）和基于视频的唇读提供了互补的发音信息，但其用于连续语音合成的集成仍鲜有研究。此外，现有多模态方法很少考虑对模态退化或临时传感器故障的鲁棒性，限制了其在现实场景中的适用性。本文提出一种掩码多模态语音合成框架，在训练过程中通过模态掩码联合利用sEMG和唇读信号。在多说话人设置下，该方法相比最强单模态基线将词错误率降低高达14个百分点。实验结果不仅表明掩码策略对性能提升和低比特率条件下的鲁棒性至关重要，而且在模态缺失条件下，其泛化能力优于针对退化设计的数据增强方法。音素级分析进一步揭示了各模态间的互补贡献，尤其对元音和特定辅音组具有显著优势。总体而言，这些发现证明了掩码多模态集成在静默语音合成中的有效性和鲁棒性，尽管针对声带切除患者的应用仍是一个开放的研究挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust silent speech synthesis for individuals lacking laryngeal voice, this work integrates surface electromyography (sEMG) and lipreading signals using a masked multimodal framework that trains models to handle modality dropout during training. The method employs masking strategies to simulate sensor failure and degradation, enhancing robustness without relying on degradation-specific augmentations. Experimental results show up to 14 absolute percentage point reduction in word error rate over the best unimodal baseline under multispeaker conditions, with phone-level analysis confirming complementary contributions—especially for vowels and specific consonants—and superior generalization under modality absence.
为提升喉切除患者无声语音合成的鲁棒性，本文提出一种跨模态掩码框架，通过训练时交替掩码表面肌电（sEMG）与唇读信号，使模型学会在某一模态缺失时依赖另一模态。多说话人实验显示，该方法相较最佳单模态基线词错误率降低最高达14个百分点，且掩码策略在模态缺失场景下优于特定退化数据增强。音素级分析表明，各模态对元音及特定辅音组贡献互补，验证了该框架的有效性与泛化能力。
Frequency-based Constrained Sampling for Interval Patterns
Authors: Djawad Bekkoucha, Abdelkader Ouali, Bruno Crémilleux
First: 2026-06-08T15:49:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:49:18+00:00
Comments: 16 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Output space pattern sampling is a powerful alternative to exhaustive pattern mining for exploring large pattern spaces, as it enables users to focus on representative patterns drawn according to a chosen interestingness measure. In this paper, we address the problem of sampling interval patterns under user-defined syntactic constraints. We introduce CFips, a sampling approach that incorporates constraints directly into the sampling procedure. The approach relies on a multi-step sampling framework and supports several syntactic constraints by decomposing them into elementary predicates on interval bounds while preserving exact sampling guarantees. We formally prove that CFips samples interval patterns proportionally to their frequency within the constrained pattern space. The experimental results show that integrating constraints into the sampling procedure enables to complete mining tasks that would otherwise fail within a given time out.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于频率的约束区间模式采样
输出空间模式采样是探索大规模模式空间的一种强大替代方法，它使用户能够根据选定的有趣性度量聚焦于代表性模式。本文解决了在用户定义的句法约束下采样区间模式的问题。我们提出了CFips，一种将约束直接融入采样过程的采样方法。该方法依赖于多步骤采样框架，通过将句法约束分解为区间边界上的基本谓词来支持多种句法约束，同时保持精确的采样保证。我们形式化证明了CFips在约束模式空间内按频率比例采样区间模式。实验结果表明，将约束融入采样过程可完成在给定超时内否则无法完成的挖掘任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently explore large pattern spaces while respecting user-defined syntactic constraints, this paper proposes CFips, a frequency-based constrained sampling method for interval patterns. CFips integrates constraints directly into the sampling process by decomposing them into elementary predicates on interval bounds, enabling exact sampling guarantees within the constrained space. Experimental results demonstrate that CFips successfully completes mining tasks that would otherwise timeout under unconstrained sampling, while ensuring sampled patterns are proportional to their frequency in the constrained space.
为高效探索受用户约束的大型模式空间，本文提出CFips方法，该方法在频率基础上对区间模式进行约束采样。CFips通过将约束分解为区间边界上的基本谓词，直接融入采样过程，确保精确采样保证。该方法按约束空间内模式频率比例进行采样，实验表明其能完成无约束采样无法在时限内完成的挖掘任务。
In-Context Learning for Latent Space Bayesian Optimization
Authors: Tuan A. Vu, Harri Lähdesmäki, Julien Martinelli
First: 2026-06-08T15:45:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:45:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian optimization (BO) is a central tool for sample-efficient design, and latent-space Bayesian optimization (LSBO) extends it to structured objects such as molecules and proteins. In parallel, tabular foundation models such as TabPFN and TabICL now achieve state-of-the-art regression performance and are increasingly used as BO surrogates. Because their Bayesian behavior is induced by large synthetic pretraining collections, the composition of this pretraining distribution is crucial. LSBO creates a distinctive mismatch: the induced map from latent code to objective value differs markedly from the regression tasks used to train current in-context models. We address this mismatch by complementing the pretraining stage of tabular foundation model surrogates with synthetic optimization tasks defined on the latent space of a molecular VAE. The continued-pretraining objective features a regularizer that anchors the model to the original checkpoint, preserving its broad regression prior while avoiding overspecialization to the adaptation tasks. On held-out molecular optimization benchmarks, the resulting model achieves strong performance, supporting the relevance of LSBO-specific adaptation for in-context surrogates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜空间贝叶斯优化中的上下文学习
贝叶斯优化（BO）是样本高效设计的核心工具，而潜空间贝叶斯优化（LSBO）将其扩展至分子和蛋白质等结构化对象。与此同时，TabPFN 和 TabICL 等表格基础模型如今已实现最先进的回归性能，并越来越多地被用作 BO 替代模型。由于其贝叶斯行为源于大规模合成预训练数据集，该预训练分布的构成至关重要。LSBO 产生了一种独特的不匹配：从潜码到目标值的诱导映射与训练当前上下文模型所用的回归任务存在显著差异。我们通过在表格基础模型替代模型的预训练阶段补充定义于分子 VAE 潜空间上的合成优化任务来解决这一不匹配。持续预训练目标包含一个正则化项，使模型锚定原始检查点，保留其广泛的回归先验，同时避免过度专业化于适应任务。在未见的分子优化基准测试中，所得模型表现优异，支持 LSBO 特定适应对上下文替代模型的相关性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve Bayesian optimization for structured objects like molecules, this work addresses a mismatch between latent-space Bayesian optimization (LSBO) and in-context learning models trained on tabular regression tasks. The method adapts tabular foundation models by augmenting their pretraining with synthetic optimization tasks in the latent space of a molecular VAE, using a regularization term to preserve the original regression prior. Experimental results on molecular optimization benchmarks show that this adapted model outperforms baseline in-context surrogates, demonstrating that LSBO-specific pretraining enhances performance for latent-space optimization.
为提升分子等结构化对象的贝叶斯优化效果，该研究针对当前基于表格的上下文学习模型在潜在空间优化任务中表现不佳的问题，提出在分子VAE潜在空间中引入合成优化任务进行预训练，同时通过正则化项保留原始回归先验。实验表明，该方法在分子优化基准测试中显著优于基线模型，验证了针对潜在空间优化任务进行专门预训练的有效性。
From 0-to-1 to 1-to-N: Reproducible Engineering Evidence for MetaAI Recursive Self-Design
Authors: Dun Li, Jiatao Li, Hongzhi Li
First: 2026-06-08T15:45:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:45:15+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 2 figures, 7 tables. Supplementary code: https://github.com/DunLi-Tsinghua/MetaAI-Mini
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recursive self-design refers to AI-assisted modification of the mechanisms by which an AI system is built, evaluated, and improved. This paper treats MetaAI not as a mature paradigm, but as a working term for a human-seeded, AI-expanded development pattern in which the design space itself becomes a target of modification. We propose an operational evidence framework with four criteria: inspectable target system, meta-level modifier, feedback-directed selection, and recursive continuation. We then map public systems, including Darwin Goedel Machine (DGM), STOP, Goedel Agent, and ShinkaEvolve, against these criteria. DGM provides the most direct currently reported evidence: its published results show improvement from 20% to 50% on SWE-bench Verified and from 14.2% to 30.7% on full Polyglot after 80 iterations, with ablations suggesting that both open-ended exploration and self-improvement contribute. Finally, we provide MetaAI-Mini, a reproducible HumanEval-based protocol and codebase. Because no completed model run is included in this build, MetaAI-Mini is reported as a protocol rather than as an experimental result.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从0到1再到1到N：MetaAI递归自设计的可复现工程证据
递归自设计指AI辅助修改构建、评估和改进AI系统的机制。本文将MetaAI视为人类引导、AI扩展的开发模式的工作术语，而非成熟范式，其中设计空间本身成为修改目标。我们提出包含四个标准的操作性证据框架：可检视的目标系统、元级修改器、反馈导向选择、递归延续。随后将公开系统（包括达尔文哥德尔机DGM、STOP、哥德尔代理、ShinkaEvolve）映射至这些标准。DGM提供了目前最直接的证据：其发表结果表明，在80次迭代后，SWE-bench Verified上性能从20%提升至50%，在完整Polyglot上从14.2%提升至30.7%，消融实验显示开放探索与自我改进均起作用。最后，我们提供MetaAI-Mini，一个基于HumanEval的可复现协议与代码库。因本构建未包含完整模型运行，MetaAI-Mini被报告为协议而非实验结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to establish empirical evidence for recursive self-design in AI systems, this paper proposes a four-criteria framework to evaluate whether an AI system can autonomously modify its own design and improve iteratively. The authors analyze public systems like DGM, STOP, Goedel Agent, and ShinkaEvolve against these criteria, with DGM showing the strongest evidence: after 80 iterations, it improved from 20% to 50% on SWE-bench Verified and from 14.2% to 30.7% on full Polyglot, with ablations indicating that both open-ended exploration and self-improvement drive gains. The paper also introduces MetaAI-Mini, a reproducible HumanEval-based protocol and codebase, though it lacks a completed model run and is thus presented as a methodological template rather than a finalized experiment.
为建立AI系统自我递归设计的实证基础，本文将MetaAI视为人类引导、AI扩展的开发范式，其中设计过程本身可被修改。提出四维证据框架——可检视的目标系统、元级修改器、反馈导向选择、递归延续——用于评估系统。在DGM、STOP、Goedel Agent和ShinkaEvolve等公开系统中，DGM表现最突出，80轮迭代后在SWE-bench Verified上性能从20%提升至50%，在Polyglot上从14.2%提升至30.7%，消融实验表明开放探索与自我改进共同驱动进步。本文还提供MetaAI-Mini，一个基于HumanEval的可复现协议与代码库，但因未包含完整模型运行，仅作为协议报告而非实验结果。
When Built-in Thinking Helps and Hurts: Constraint-Level Error Shifts in Instruction Following
Authors: Sai Adith Senthil Kumar
First: 2026-06-08T15:45:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:45:04+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 7 figures, 15 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large reasoning models (LRMs) often improve math and coding performance, but their effect on instruction following is unclear. We study IFEval with Qwen3 models (1.7B-32B), using same-weights Thinking ON/OFF controls; four Hunyuan models provide directional cross-family support. Aggregate pass-rate changes are small (-0.55 to -3.52 pp), yet 10-20% of prompts switch between pass and fail across modes, suggesting that thinking changes the pattern of errors--some prompts improve while others worsen--rather than uniformly degrading performance. Under a post-hoc Qwen3-derived grouping, constraint types separate into Planning (global counting, structure, coordination), which improves at the class level under thinking, and Precision (exact local form), which consistently worsens; the class-level Planning/Precision sign pattern holds directionally for all four Hunyuan models despite Hunyuan's opposite aggregate direction. Thinking also changes final-answer length; matched-length analyses substantially reduce the Precision drop, but a residual penalty remains. Analyzing thinking traces with a cross-encoder relevance metric reveals three patterns: Neutral shows a positive relevance-compliance link (r approximately 0.15); Planning shows near-zero predictive correlation (r approximately 0.02) despite measurable trace engagement, consistent with an execution gap between CE-measured trace relevance and final-answer compliance; Precision shows a small negative correlation (r approximately -0.05), with failing instances having higher mean relevance than passing ones. Activation patching across four model sizes (1.7B-14B) shows that Precision flip instances are more often restored than Planning flip instances (32-58% vs. 14-40% mean layer-restoration), with the largest gap at 14B (about 30 pp).
中文标题/摘要
标题：内置思维何时有益何时有害：指令遵循中的约束级别错误转移
大型推理模型（LRMs）通常能提升数学和编程表现，但其对指令遵循的影响尚不明确。我们使用Qwen3模型（1.7B-32B）研究IFEval，通过相同权重的思维开启/关闭对照实验；四个Hunyuan模型提供跨家族方向性支持。整体通过率变化较小（-0.55至-3.52个百分点），但10-20%的提示在不同模式间切换通过与失败状态，表明思维改变了错误模式——部分提示表现改善，部分恶化，而非均匀降低性能。在事后基于Qwen3衍生的分组中，约束类型分为规划（全局计数、结构、协调），在思维开启下类别层面表现改善；精度（精确局部形式）则持续恶化；尽管Hunyuan模型整体方向相反，四类模型均保持规划/精度的类别层面符号模式。思维还改变最终答案长度；匹配长度分析显著减少精度下降，但残余惩罚仍存。通过交叉编码器相关性度量分析思维轨迹，发现三种模式：中性模式显示正相关性（r约0.15）；规划模式虽有可测量轨迹参与，但预测相关性近零（r约0.02），符合CE度量轨迹相关性与最终答案合规性之间的执行差距；精度模式显示微弱负相关（r约-0.05），失败实例平均相关性高于通过实例。在四个模型尺寸（1.7B-14B）间进行激活修补显示，精度翻转实例比规划翻转实例更常恢复（平均层恢复率32-58% vs. 14-40%），14B模型差距最大（约30个百分点）。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how built-in reasoning mechanisms in large language models affect instruction-following performance, motivated by the ambiguity of whether such mechanisms uniformly help or hurt. Using Qwen3 models (1.7B-32B) with Thinking ON/OFF controls and four Hunyuan models for cross-family validation, the authors find that while aggregate pass rates change minimally (−0.55 to −3.52 pp), a significant portion of prompts (10–20%) flip between pass/fail depending on thinking mode, indicating error pattern shifts rather than uniform degradation. Analysis reveals two constraint types: Planning (global structure, coordination) improves under thinking, while Precision (exact local form) consistently worsens; this pattern holds across models despite differing aggregate trends. Thinking also alters answer length, and matched-length analyses reduce but do not eliminate the Precision penalty. Trace analysis with a cross-encoder metric shows Neutral, Planning, and Precision exhibit distinct relevance-compliance correlations (r≈0.15, 0.02, −0.05), with Planning showing an execution gap. Activation patching across sizes shows Precision flip instances are more easily restored (mean 32–58%) than Planning flips (14–40%), with the largest gap at 14B (≈30 pp).
该研究探讨大型模型内置推理机制对指令遵循性能的影响，旨在澄清此类机制是否普遍有益或有害。通过在Qwen3模型（1.7B-32B）中启用/禁用内置思考功能，并结合四个Hunyuan模型进行跨家族验证，作者发现虽然整体通过率变化较小（−0.55至−3.52个百分点），但10–20%的提示在开启/关闭思考时切换通过/失败状态，表明思考改变了错误分布模式而非统一降低性能。分析显示两类约束：规划类（全局结构）在思考下表现改善，而精确类（局部精确性）持续恶化；该模式在所有模型中方向一致，尽管Hunyuan模型整体趋势相反。思考还影响答案长度，匹配长度分析可部分缓解精确类下降，但残余惩罚仍存。通过交叉编码器相关性分析思考轨迹，发现中性、规划、精确三类呈现不同相关性（r≈0.15、0.02、−0.05），表明轨迹与最终输出的对齐程度各异。跨模型尺寸的激活修补实验显示，精确类切换实例比规划类更易恢复，14B模型中差距最大（约30个百分点）。
The Flexibility Trap: Rethinking the Value of Arbitrary Order in Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Zanlin Ni, Shenzhi Wang, Yang Yue, Tianyu Yu, Weilin Zhao, Yeguo Hua, Tianyi Chen, Jun Song, Cheng Yu, Bo Zheng, Gao Huang
First: 2026-01-21T16:41:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:43:52+00:00
Comments: Code and pre-trained models: https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/JustGRPO
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) break the rigid left-to-right constraint of traditional LLMs, enabling token generation in arbitrary orders. Intuitively, this flexibility implies a solution space that strictly supersets the fixed autoregressive trajectory, theoretically unlocking superior reasoning potential. However, in this paper, we find that for general reasoning tasks (e.g., mathematics and coding), arbitrary order generation may in fact limit the reasoning potential of dLLMs. We observe that dLLMs tend to exploit this order flexibility to bypass high-uncertainty tokens that are crucial for exploration, which can lead to a premature collapse of solution coverage. This observation motivates a rethink of RL approaches for dLLMs, where considerable complexities, such as handling combinatorial trajectories and intractable likelihoods, are often devoted to preserving this flexibility. We show that effective reasoning can be elicited by simply forgoing arbitrary order and applying standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) instead. Our approach, JustGRPO, is minimalist yet surprisingly effective (e.g., 89.1% accuracy on GSM8K) while fully retaining the parallel decoding ability of dLLMs. Project page: https://nzl-thu.github.io/the-flexibility-trap
中文标题/摘要
标题：灵活性陷阱：重新思考扩散语言模型中任意顺序的价值
扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）打破了传统大语言模型从左到右的刚性约束，允许按任意顺序生成token。直观上，这种灵活性意味着其解空间严格超越固定自回归轨迹，理论上可解锁更优的推理潜力。然而，本文发现，对于一般推理任务（如数学和编程），任意顺序生成反而可能限制dLLMs的推理潜力。我们观察到，dLLMs倾向于利用这种顺序灵活性绕过对探索至关重要的高不确定性token，从而导致解空间过早坍缩。这一发现促使我们重新思考dLLMs的强化学习方法，其中大量复杂性（如处理组合轨迹和不可计算似然）常用于维持这种灵活性。我们证明，仅通过放弃任意顺序，转而采用标准的组相对策略优化（GRPO），即可有效激发推理能力。我们的方法JustGRPO简洁却效果惊人（如在GSM8K上准确率达89.1%），同时完全保留了dLLMs的并行解码能力。项目页面：https://nzl-thu.github.io/the-flexibility-trap
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether the arbitrary token generation order in diffusion language models (dLLMs) enhances reasoning performance, as intuitively expected. The authors find that this flexibility often hinders reasoning by causing models to skip high-uncertainty tokens critical for exploration, leading to premature collapse in solution coverage. To address this, they propose JustGRPO, a minimalist approach that discards arbitrary order and instead applies standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), achieving strong performance (89.1% accuracy on GSM8K) while preserving parallel decoding. Their results suggest that enforcing sequential generation may be more effective for reasoning tasks than leveraging arbitrary order, challenging prevailing assumptions about dLLM design.
本文探讨扩散语言模型（dLLMs）中任意顺序生成是否能提升推理能力，但发现该灵活性反而会阻碍推理，因为模型倾向于跳过关键的高不确定性token，导致解空间过早坍缩。为此，作者提出JustGRPO方法，放弃任意顺序，采用标准的Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO），在保持并行解码能力的同时取得优异效果（如GSM8K上89.1%准确率），表明在推理任务中，顺序生成可能比任意顺序更有效。
End-to-End Context Compression at Scale
Authors: Ang Li, Sean McLeish, Haozhe Chen, Nimit Kalra, Zaiqian Chen, Artem Gazizov, Venkata Anoop Suhas Kumar Morisetty, Bhavya Kailkhura, Harshitha Menon, Zhuang Liu, Brian R. Bartoldson, Tom Goldstein, Sanae Lotfi, Micah Goldblum, Pavel Izmailov
First: 2026-06-08T15:43:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-08T15:43:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-context language model inference is bottlenecked by memory, as the KV cache grows with context length. Recent techniques to compress the KV cache fall short: they either degrade model quality substantially or require considerable time and compute to compress a single long prompt. Furthermore, many methods require the input to fit within the target model's context window, and are generally incompatible with modern production inference engines. Encoder-decoder compressors, which map a long token sequence to a shorter sequence of latent embeddings consumed by a decoder, are an appealing alternative in principle. However, existing approaches are not competitive with KV cache compression on the accuracy-efficiency frontier. In this work, we revisit encoder-decoder compression and close this gap. We first perform an architecture search, pre-training many variants from scratch to determine how best to design and train encoder-decoder compressors. Guided by our findings, we continually pre-train a family of 0.6B-encoder, 4B-decoder models on over 350B tokens each, at compression ratios of 1:4, 1:8, and 1:16. We introduce Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs), a family of compressors that improve the Pareto frontier across general-task performance, compression speed, and peak memory usage. We demonstrate that LCLMs serve as efficient backbones for long-horizon agents, letting the agent skim through a compressed long context and adaptively expand relevant segments on demand.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大规模端到端上下文压缩
长上下文语言模型推理受内存限制，因KV缓存随上下文长度增长。近期的KV缓存压缩技术存在不足：要么显著降低模型质量，要么压缩单个长提示需耗费大量时间和计算资源。此外，许多方法要求输入适配目标模型的上下文窗口，且通常与现代生产推理引擎不兼容。理论上，编码器-解码器压缩器（将长token序列映射为解码器消费的较短潜在嵌入序列）是更优选择。然而，现有方法在准确率-效率权衡上无法与KV缓存压缩竞争。本文重新审视编码器-解码器压缩，弥合此差距。我们首先进行架构搜索，从零开始预训练多种变体，以确定如何最佳设计和训练编码器-解码器压缩器。根据发现，我们持续预训练一系列0.6B编码器、4B解码器模型，每个模型在1:4、1:8、1:16压缩比下训练超3500亿token。我们提出潜在上下文语言模型（LCLMs），该系列压缩器在通用任务性能、压缩速度和峰值内存使用方面均提升帕累托前沿。我们证明LCLMs可作为长时程代理的高效骨干，使代理能快速浏览压缩长上下文，并按需动态扩展相关片段。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory bottlenecks in long-context language model inference due to growing KV caches, this work revisits encoder-decoder compression as a viable alternative. The authors conduct an architecture search and pre-train a family of 0.6B-encoder, 4B-decoder models on 350B tokens each at 1:4, 1:8, and 1:16 compression ratios. The resulting Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs) outperform existing methods by improving the accuracy-efficiency tradeoff across memory usage, compression speed, and general-task performance. LCLMs enable long-horizon agents to efficiently skim compressed contexts and adaptively expand relevant segments on demand, making them suitable for production inference engines.
为了解决长上下文语言模型推理中因KV缓存随上下文增长而引发的内存瓶颈，本文重新审视了编码器-解码器压缩方法，该方法在现有KV压缩技术常牺牲精度或需大量计算的情况下更具潜力。作者通过架构搜索指导压缩器设计，随后预训练了多个0.6B编码器-4B解码器模型，每个模型在1:4、1:8、1:16压缩比下训练3500亿tokens。所提出的潜伏上下文语言模型（LCLMs）在通用任务性能、压缩速度和峰值内存使用之间实现了更优的帕累托前沿，使长时程智能体能高效压缩上下文并按需扩展相关片段。
